Multimodal Large Language Models as Image Classifiers
Authors: Nikita Kisel, Illia Volkov, Klara Janouskova, Jiri Matas
First: 2026-03-06T18:59:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-06T18:59:58+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLM) classification performance depends critically on evaluation protocol and ground truth quality. Studies comparing MLLMs with supervised and vision-language models report conflicting conclusions, and we show these conflicts stem from protocols that either inflate or underestimate performance. Across the most common evaluation protocols, we identify and fix key issues: model outputs that fall outside the provided class list and are discarded, inflated results from weak multiple-choice distractors, and an open-world setting that underperforms only due to poor output mapping. We additionally quantify the impact of commonly overlooked design choices - batch size, image ordering, and text encoder selection - showing they substantially affect accuracy. Evaluating on ReGT, our multilabel reannotation of 625 ImageNet-1k classes, reveals that MLLMs benefit most from corrected labels (up to +10.8%), substantially narrowing the perceived gap with supervised models. Much of the reported MLLMs underperformance on classification is thus an artifact of noisy ground truth and flawed evaluation protocol rather than genuine model deficiency. Models less reliant on supervised training signals prove most sensitive to annotation quality. Finally, we show that MLLMs can assist human annotators: in a controlled case study, annotators confirmed or integrated MLLMs predictions in approximately 50% of difficult cases, demonstrating their potential for large-scale dataset curation.
Summary / 总结
Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLM) classification performance depends critically on evaluation protocol and ground truth quality.
Omni-Diffusion: Unified Multimodal Understanding and Generation with Masked Discrete Diffusion
Authors: Lijiang Li, Zuwei Long, Yunhang Shen, Heting Gao, Haoyu Cao, Xing Sun, Caifeng Shan, Ran He, Chaoyou Fu
First: 2026-03-06T18:59:57+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-06T18:59:57+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://omni-diffusion.github.io
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
While recent multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have made impressive strides, they predominantly employ a conventional autoregressive architecture as their backbone, leaving significant room to explore effective and efficient alternatives in architectural design. Concurrently, recent studies have successfully applied discrete diffusion models to various domains, such as visual understanding and image generation, revealing their considerable potential as a promising backbone for multimodal systems. Drawing inspiration from these pioneering research, we introduce Omni-Diffusion, the first any-to-any multimodal language model built entirely on mask-based discrete diffusion models, which unifies understanding and generation across text, speech, and images. Omni-Diffusion employs a unified mask-based discrete diffusion model to directly capture the joint distribution over discrete multimodal tokens. This approach supports not only bimodal tasks but also more complex scenarios involving multiple modalities. On a diverse set of benchmarks, our method outperforms or performs on par with existing multimodal systems that process two or more modalities, highlighting the significant promise of diffusion models in powering the next generation of multimodal foundation models. Project webpage: https://omni-diffusion.github.io.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Omni-Diffusion：基于掩码离散扩散模型的统一多模态理解和生成
尽管近期多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）已取得显著进展，但它们主要采用传统的自回归架构作为骨干结构，仍存在探索更有效、更高效架构设计的空间。与此同时，近期研究已成功将离散扩散模型应用于视觉理解与图像生成等多个领域，揭示其作为多模态系统骨干结构的巨大潜力。受此启发，我们提出Omni-Diffusion，这是首个完全基于掩码离散扩散模型构建的任意模态到任意模态的多模态语言模型，统一实现文本、语音与图像的理解与生成。Omni-Diffusion采用统一的掩码离散扩散模型，直接建模离散多模态标记的联合分布，不仅支持双模态任务，还可处理更复杂的多模态场景。在多个多样化基准测试中，我们的方法在处理两个或更多模态的现有多模态系统中表现更优或持平，凸显了扩散模型在驱动下一代多模态基础模型方面的巨大潜力。项目网页：https://omni-diffusion.github.io。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of conventional autoregressive architectures in multimodal large language models, this work introduces Omni-Diffusion, the first any-to-any multimodal model built entirely on masked discrete diffusion. It unifies understanding and generation across text, speech, and images by modeling the joint distribution of discrete multimodal tokens via a single diffusion framework. Experimental results across diverse benchmarks show that Omni-Diffusion either outperforms or matches state-of-the-art multimodal systems handling two or more modalities, demonstrating the strong potential of diffusion-based architectures for next-generation multimodal foundation models.
鉴于当前多模态大语言模型主要依赖传统自回归架构的局限性，本文提出Omni-Diffusion，这是首个完全基于掩码离散扩散模型的任意模态间统一多模态语言模型，支持文本、语音和图像的联合理解与生成。该模型通过单一扩散框架直接建模离散多模态标记的联合分布，适用于双模态乃至多模态复杂任务。在多个基准测试中，Omni-Diffusion表现优于或至少持平现有处理两及以上模态的多模态系统，验证了扩散模型在下一代多模态基础模型中的巨大潜力。
BEVLM: Distilling Semantic Knowledge from LLMs into Bird's-Eye View Representations
Authors: Thomas Monninger, Shaoyuan Xie, Qi Alfred Chen, Sihao Ding
First: 2026-03-06T18:59:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-06T18:59:55+00:00
Comments: 4 figures, 6 tables in the main paper, 32 pages in total
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The integration of Large Language Models (LLMs) into autonomous driving has attracted growing interest for their strong reasoning and semantic understanding abilities, which are essential for handling complex decision-making and long-tail scenarios. However, existing methods typically feed LLMs with tokens from multi-view and multi-frame images independently, leading to redundant computation and limited spatial consistency. This separation in visual processing hinders accurate 3D spatial reasoning and fails to maintain geometric coherence across views. On the other hand, Bird's-Eye View (BEV) representations learned from geometrically annotated tasks (e.g., object detection) provide spatial structure but lack the semantic richness of foundation vision encoders. To bridge this gap, we propose BEVLM, a framework that connects a spatially consistent and semantically distilled BEV representation with LLMs. Through extensive experiments, we show that BEVLM enables LLMs to reason more effectively in cross-view driving scenes, improving accuracy by 46%, by leveraging BEV features as unified inputs. Furthermore, by distilling semantic knowledge from LLMs into BEV representations, BEVLM significantly improves closed-loop end-to-end driving performance by 29% in safety-critical scenarios.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to enhance autonomous driving systems with LLMs' semantic reasoning while preserving spatial consistency, BEVLM integrates LLMs with Bird's-Eye View (BEV) representations by distilling semantic knowledge from LLMs into BEV features. The method unifies multi-view inputs into a spatially coherent BEV space, enabling LLMs to reason more effectively across views. Experimental results show a 46% accuracy improvement in cross-view driving tasks and a 29% performance boost in safety-critical end-to-end driving scenarios by leveraging BEV as a semantic-rich, geometrically consistent input.
为提升自动驾驶系统在复杂场景中的语义推理能力，BEVLM提出将大语言模型（LLM）的语义知识蒸馏至鸟瞰图（BEV）表示中，实现空间一致性与语义丰富性的结合。该方法通过统一多视角输入至BEV空间，使LLM能更有效地跨视角推理。实验表明，在跨视角驾驶任务中准确率提升46%，在安全关键的端到端驾驶场景中性能提升29%。
Fly360: Omnidirectional Obstacle Avoidance within Drone View
Authors: Xiangkai Zhang, Dizhe Zhang, WenZhuo Cao, Zhaoliang Wan, Yingjie Niu, Lu Qi, Xu Yang, Zhiyong Liu
First: 2026-03-06T18:59:43+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-06T18:59:43+00:00
Comments: 16 pages, 10 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Obstacle avoidance in unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs), as a fundamental capability, has gained increasing attention with the growing focus on spatial intelligence. However, current obstacle-avoidance methods mainly depend on limited field-of-view sensors and are ill-suited for UAV scenarios which require full-spatial awareness when the movement direction differs from the UAV's heading. This limitation motivates us to explore omnidirectional obstacle avoidance for panoramic drones with full-view perception. We first study an under explored problem setting in which a UAV must generate collision-free motion in environments with obstacles from arbitrary directions, and then construct a benchmark that consists of three representative flight tasks. Based on such settings, we propose Fly360, a two-stage perception-decision pipeline with a fixed random-yaw training strategy. At the perception stage, panoramic RGB observations are input and converted into depth maps as a robust intermediate representation. For the policy network, it is lightweight and used to output body-frame velocity commands from depth inputs. Extensive simulation and real-world experiments demonstrate that Fly360 achieves stable omnidirectional obstacle avoidance and outperforms forward-view baselines across all tasks. Our model is available at https://zxkai.github.io/fly360/
中文标题/摘要
标题：Fly360：无人机全景视野下的全方位避障
作为基础能力，无人机（UAV）避障随着空间智能研究的深入受到越来越多关注。然而，当前避障方法主要依赖有限视场传感器，难以满足无人机在运动方向与朝向不一致时所需的全空间感知需求。为此，我们探索了具备全景感知能力的无人机全方位避障问题。首先研究了一个尚未充分探索的问题场景——无人机需在任意方向障碍物环境中生成无碰撞运动，并构建包含三项代表性飞行任务的基准测试。基于此，我们提出Fly360，一种两阶段感知-决策管道，采用固定随机偏航角训练策略。感知阶段输入全景RGB图像并转换为深度图作为鲁棒中间表示；策略网络轻量级，直接从深度输入输出机体坐标系速度指令。大量仿真与真实实验表明，Fly360实现稳定全方位避障，且在所有任务中均优于前视基线方法。模型开源地址：https://zxkai.github.io/fly360/
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for UAVs to navigate safely in environments with obstacles from arbitrary directions, this work introduces Fly360, a two-stage perception-decision pipeline designed for omnidirectional obstacle avoidance using panoramic visual input. The method first converts panoramic RGB images into depth maps for robust perception, then uses a lightweight policy network to generate body-frame velocity commands based on depth inputs, trained with a fixed random-yaw strategy. Experimental results across simulation and real-world benchmarks show Fly360 achieves stable collision avoidance and consistently outperforms forward-view baselines in all three representative flight tasks.
为解决无人机在任意方向障碍物环境中导航的安全问题，本文提出Fly360，一种基于全景视觉输入的双向避障框架。该方法首先将全景RGB图像转换为深度图以增强感知鲁棒性，再通过轻量级策略网络根据深度输入生成机体坐标系下的速度指令，训练时采用固定随机偏航策略。实验表明，Fly360在仿真与真实环境中均能稳定避障，并在三项代表性飞行任务中全面优于传统前视基线方法。
SCOPE: Scene-Contextualized Incremental Few-Shot 3D Segmentation
Authors: Vishal Thengane, Zhaochong An, Tianjin Huang, Son Lam Phung, Abdesselam Bouzerdoum, Lu Yin, Na Zhao, Xiatian Zhu
Venue: CVPR 2026
First: 2026-03-06T18:59:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-06T18:59:36+00:00
Comments: Accepted at CVPR 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Incremental Few-Shot (IFS) segmentation aims to learn new categories over time from only a few annotations. Although widely studied in 2D, it remains underexplored for 3D point clouds. Existing methods suffer from catastrophic forgetting or fail to learn discriminative prototypes under sparse supervision, and often overlook a key cue: novel categories frequently appear as unlabelled background in base-training scenes. We introduce SCOPE (Scene-COntextualised Prototype Enrichment), a plug-and-play background-guided prototype enrichment framework that integrates with any prototype-based 3D segmentation method. After base training, a class-agnostic segmentation model extracts high-confidence pseudo-instances from background regions to build a prototype pool. When novel classes arrive with few labelled samples, relevant background prototypes are retrieved and fused with few-shot prototypes to form enriched representations without retraining the backbone or adding parameters. Experiments on ScanNet and S3DIS show that SCOPE achieves SOTA performance, improving novel-class IoU by up to 6.98% and 3.61%, and mean IoU by 2.25% and 1.70%, respectively, while maintaining low forgetting. Code is available https://github.com/Surrey-UP-Lab/SCOPE.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SCOPE：场景上下文引导的增量少样本3D分割
增量少样本（IFS）分割旨在从少量标注中随时间学习新类别。尽管在2D领域广泛研究，但在3D点云中仍被探索不足。现有方法常受灾难性遗忘影响，或在稀疏监督下难以学习判别原型，且常忽略关键线索：新类别常以无标签背景形式出现在基础训练场景中。我们提出SCOPE（场景上下文引导的原型增强），一种即插即用的背景引导原型增强框架，可与任何基于原型的3D分割方法集成。基础训练后，类别无关分割模型从背景区域提取高置信度伪实例构建原型池。当新类别以少量标注样本到达时，相关背景原型被检索并融合少量样本原型，形成增强表示，无需重新训练主干网络或增加参数。在ScanNet和S3DIS上的实验表明，SCOPE达到SOTA性能，分别提升新类别IoU达6.98%和3.61%，平均IoU提升2.25%和1.70%，同时保持低遗忘。代码见https://github.com/Surrey-UP-Lab/SCOPE。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of learning new 3D object categories incrementally with minimal annotations while avoiding catastrophic forgetting, SCOPE introduces a background-guided prototype enrichment method that leverages unlabelled background regions from base-training scenes to build discriminative prototypes. It integrates with any existing prototype-based 3D segmentation model, extracting high-confidence pseudo-instances from background areas after base training, then fusing relevant background prototypes with few-shot prototypes for novel classes without retraining or adding parameters. Experiments on ScanNet and S3DIS demonstrate that SCOPE achieves state-of-the-art performance, boosting novel-class IoU by up to 6.98% and 3.61%, and mean IoU by 2.25% and 1.70%, while effectively suppressing forgetting.
为了解决在少量标注下增量学习新3D类别时易发生灾难性遗忘且难以构建判别原型的问题，SCOPE提出一种基于背景引导的原型增强方法，利用基础训练场景中的无标签背景区域构建判别性原型。该方法可无缝集成到任意基于原型的3D分割模型中，在基础训练后从背景区域提取高置信度伪实例，再将相关背景原型与少量样本原型融合以增强新类表示，无需重新训练或增加参数。在ScanNet和S3DIS上的实验表明，SCOPE达到当前最优性能，新类IoU提升最高达6.98%和3.61%，平均IoU提升2.25%和1.70%，同时有效抑制遗忘。
SUREON: A Benchmark and Vision-Language-Model for Surgical Reasoning
Authors: Alejandra Perez, Anita Rau, Lee White, Busisiwe Mlambo, Chinedu Nwoye, Muhammad Abdullah Jamal, Omid Mohareri
First: 2026-03-06T18:58:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-06T18:58:36+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Surgeons don't just see -- they interpret. When an expert observes a surgical scene, they understand not only what instrument is being used, but why it was chosen, what risk it poses, and what comes next. Current surgical AI cannot answer such questions, largely because training data that explicitly encodes surgical reasoning is immensely difficult to annotate at scale. Yet surgical video lectures already contain exactly this -- explanations of intent, rationale, and anticipation, narrated by experts for the purpose of teaching. Though inherently noisy and unstructured, these narrations encode the reasoning that surgical AI currently lacks. We introduce SUREON, a large-scale video QA dataset that systematically harvests this training signal from surgical academic videos. SUREON defines 12 question categories covering safety assessment, decision rationale, and forecasting, and uses a multi-agent pipeline to extract and structure supervision at scale. Across 134.7K clips and 170 procedure types, SUREON yields 206.8k QA pairs and an expert-validated benchmark of 354 examples. To evaluate the extent to which this supervision translates to surgical reasoning ability, we introduce two models: SureonVLM, a vision-language model adapted through supervised fine-tuning, and SureonVLM-R1, a reasoning model trained with Group Relative Policy Optimization. Both models can answer complex questions about surgery and substantially outperform larger general-domain models, exceeding 84% accuracy on the SUREON benchmark while outperforming general-domain models on standard surgical perception tasks. Qualitative analysis of SureonVLM-R1 reveals explicit reasoning behavior, such as inferring operative intent from visual context.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SUREON：手术推理的基准与视觉-语言模型
外科医生不仅观察，更进行解读。当专家观察手术场景时，他们不仅理解正在使用的器械，还理解为何选择该器械、其潜在风险以及下一步行动。当前的手术AI无法回答此类问题，主要原因是明确编码手术推理的训练数据难以大规模标注。然而，手术教学视频中已包含此类内容——专家为教学目的所作的意图、理由和预期的讲解。尽管这些讲解固有噪声且结构松散，但它们编码了当前手术AI所缺乏的推理能力。我们引入SUREON，一个大规模视频问答数据集，系统性地从手术学术视频中提取此类训练信号。SUREON定义了12类问题，涵盖安全评估、决策理由和预测，并采用多代理流水线大规模提取和结构化监督信号。在134,700个视频片段和170种手术类型中，SUREON生成206,800组问答对，并提供354个专家验证的基准样本。为评估此类监督信号对手术推理能力的转化效果，我们提出两个模型：SureonVLM，通过监督微调适配的视觉-语言模型；以及SureonVLM-R1，通过组相对策略优化训练的推理模型。两个模型均可回答复杂的手术问题，并显著优于通用领域大模型，在SUREON基准上准确率超过84%，同时在标准手术感知任务中优于通用领域模型。对SureonVLM-R1的定性分析揭示了明确的推理行为，例如从视觉上下文推断手术意图。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the gap between current surgical AI's perception capabilities and the need for explicit reasoning, the authors construct SUREON, a large-scale video QA dataset derived from surgical lecture videos that contain expert narrations of intent, rationale, and anticipation. They extract and structure 206.8k QA pairs across 134.7K clips and 170 procedures using a multi-agent pipeline, defining 12 reasoning categories. Two models, SureonVLM (fine-tuned VLM) and SureonVLM-R1 (reasoning model trained with Group Relative Policy Optimization), are evaluated; both significantly outperform general-domain models on the SUREON benchmark, achieving over 84% accuracy, and demonstrate explicit reasoning behaviors such as inferring operative intent from visual context.
为弥补当前外科AI在感知能力与推理能力之间的差距，作者构建了SUREON数据集，该数据集从外科教学视频中系统提取专家对操作意图、理由和预判的口头解说，经多代理流程结构化处理，形成20.68万组问答对，覆盖13.47万片段和170种手术。他们训练了两个模型：SureonVLM（监督微调视觉语言模型）和SureonVLM-R1（基于组相对策略优化的推理模型），二者在SUREON基准测试中准确率超过84%，显著优于通用领域模型，并在定性分析中展现出明确的推理行为，如从视觉上下文推断手术意图。
Penguin-VL: Exploring the Efficiency Limits of VLM with LLM-based Vision Encoders
Authors: Boqiang Zhang, Lei Ke, Ruihan Yang, Qi Gao, Tianyuan Qu, Rossell Chen, Dong Yu, Leoweiliang
First: 2026-03-06T18:58:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-06T18:58:04+00:00
Comments: Penguin-VL Technical Report; Code: https://github.com/tencent-ailab/Penguin-VL
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Vision Language Model (VLM) development has largely relied on scaling model size, which hinders deployment on compute-constrained mobile and edge devices such as smartphones and robots. In this work, we explore the performance limits of compact (e.g., 2B and 8B) VLMs. We challenge the prevailing practice that state-of-the-art VLMs must rely on vision encoders initialized via massive contrastive pretraining (e.g., CLIP/SigLIP). We identify an objective mismatch: contrastive learning, optimized for discrimination, enforces coarse and category-level invariances that suppress fine-grained visual cues needed for dense captioning and complex VLM reasoning. To address this issue, we present Penguin-VL, whose vision encoder is initialized from a text-only LLM. Our experiments reveal that Penguin-Encoder serves as a superior alternative to traditional contrastive pretraining, unlocking a higher degree of visual fidelity and data efficiency for multimodal understanding. Across various image and video benchmarks, Penguin-VL achieves performance comparable to leading VLMs (e.g., Qwen3-VL) in mathematical reasoning and surpasses them in tasks such as document understanding, visual knowledge, and multi-perspective video understanding. Notably, these gains are achieved with a lightweight architecture, demonstrating that improved visual representation rather than model scaling is the primary driver of performance. Our ablations show that Penguin-Encoder consistently outperforms contrastive-pretrained encoders, preserving fine-grained spatial and temporal cues that are critical for dense perception and complex reasoning. This makes it a strong drop-in alternative for compute-efficient VLMs and enables high performance in resource-constrained settings. Code: https://github.com/tencent-ailab/Penguin-VL
中文标题/摘要
标题：Penguin-VL：探索基于LLM视觉编码器的VLM效率极限
视觉语言模型（VLM）的发展主要依赖模型规模扩展，这阻碍了其在智能手机、机器人等计算受限的移动和边缘设备上的部署。本研究探索了紧凑型（如2B和8B）VLM的性能极限。我们挑战当前主流观点——最先进的VLM必须依赖通过大规模对比预训练（如CLIP/SigLIP）初始化的视觉编码器。我们发现对比学习存在目标不匹配：对比学习为区分任务优化，强制引入粗粒度和类别级不变性，抑制了密集字幕生成和复杂VLM推理所需的细粒度视觉线索。为解决此问题，我们提出Penguin-VL，其视觉编码器从纯文本LLM初始化。实验表明，Penguin-Encoder是传统对比预训练的更优替代方案，能解锁更高的视觉保真度和数据效率，提升多模态理解能力。在各类图像和视频基准测试中，Penguin-VL在数学推理任务中表现与领先VLM（如Qwen3-VL）相当，且在文档理解、视觉知识和多视角视频理解等任务中表现更优。值得注意的是，这些性能提升得益于轻量级架构，表明视觉表征改进而非模型规模扩展是性能提升的主要驱动力。消融实验显示，Penguin-Encoder持续优于对比预训练编码器，保留了对密集感知和复杂推理至关重要的细粒度空间与时间线索。这使其成为计算效率型VLM的强替代方案，并能在资源受限环境下实现高性能。代码：https://github.com/tencent-ailab/Penguin-VL
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of scaling VLMs for mobile and edge devices, this work investigates whether compact VLMs can achieve high performance without contrastive vision encoders. It introduces Penguin-VL, which initializes its vision encoder from a text-only LLM instead of contrastive models like CLIP. Experiments show that this approach yields superior visual fidelity and data efficiency, matching or exceeding state-of-the-art VLMs in mathematical reasoning and outperforming them in document understanding, visual knowledge, and multi-perspective video tasks—all while maintaining a lightweight architecture. Ablations confirm that the LLM-initialized encoder preserves fine-grained spatial and temporal cues, proving that enhanced visual representation—not model size—is key to performance gains.
为解决视觉语言模型在移动端和边缘设备部署时因规模膨胀导致的效率问题，该研究探索了轻量级VLM是否能在不依赖对比预训练视觉编码器（如CLIP/SigLIP）的情况下实现高性能。提出Penguin-VL，其视觉编码器从纯文本LLM初始化，实验表明该方法在视觉保真度和数据效率上更优，数学推理能力可媲美主流VLM，文档理解、视觉知识和多视角视频理解任务中表现更佳，且架构轻量。消融实验验证LLM初始化编码器能保留精细空间与时间线索，证明性能提升源于视觉表征优化而非模型规模扩大。
Neural Signals Generate Clinical Notes in the Wild
Authors: Jathurshan Pradeepkumar, Zheng Chen, Jimeng Sun
First: 2026-01-29T13:07:30+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-06T18:57:14+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Generating clinical reports that summarize abnormal patterns, diagnostic findings, and clinical interpretations from long-term EEG recordings remains labor-intensive. We curate a large-scale clinical EEG dataset with $9{,}922$ reports paired with approximately $11{,}000$ hours of EEG recordings from $9{,}048$ patients. We therefore develop CELM, the first clinical EEG-to-Language foundation model capable of summarizing long-duration, variable-length EEG recordings and performing end-to-end clinical report generation at multiple scales, including recording description, background activity, epileptiform abnormalities, events/seizures, and impressions. Experimental results show that, with patient history supervision, our method achieves $70\%$-$95\%$ average relative improvements in standard generation metrics (e.g., ROUGE-1 and METEOR) from $0.2$-$0.3$ to $0.4$-$0.6$. In the zero-shot setting without patient history, CELM attains generation scores in the range of $0.43$-$0.52$, compared to baselines of $0.17$-$0.26$. CELM integrates pretrained EEG foundation models with language models to enable scalable multimodal learning. We release our model and benchmark construction pipeline at https://github.com/Jathurshan0330/CELM.
中文标题/摘要
标题：神经信号在真实场景中生成临床记录
从长期脑电图（EEG）记录中生成总结异常模式、诊断发现和临床解释的临床报告仍需大量人工。我们构建了一个大规模临床EEG数据集，包含9,922份报告，对应约11,000小时来自9,048名患者的EEG记录。因此，我们开发了CELM，这是首个能够总结长时长、可变长度EEG记录并实现多尺度端到端临床报告生成的临床EEG到语言基础模型，涵盖记录描述、背景活动、癫痫样异常、事件/癫痫发作和印象等内容。实验结果表明，在患者病史监督下，我们的方法在标准生成指标（如ROUGE-1和METEOR）上平均相对提升70%-95%，从0.2-0.3提升至0.4-0.6。在无患者病史的零样本设置下，CELM生成分数为0.43-0.52，优于基线模型0.17-0.26。CELM结合预训练EEG基础模型与语言模型，实现可扩展的多模态学习。我们将在https://github.com/Jathurshan0330/CELM发布模型及基准构建流程。

Generating clinical reports that summarize abnormal patterns, diagnostic findings, and clinical interpretations from long-term EEG recordings remains labor-intensive.
为解决从长时程EEG记录自动生成临床报告的劳动密集型问题，作者构建了包含9,922份报告与11,000小时EEG数据的大型临床数据集。他们提出了CELM，首个能对可变长度EEG记录进行多尺度临床报告生成的EEG到语言基础模型。实验表明，借助患者病史监督，CELM在ROUGE-1和METEOR等标准指标上较基线提升70–95%；在无病史的零样本设置下，CELM仍优于基线，得分达0.43–0.52，而基线仅为0.17–0.26。
Multivariate Fields of Experts for Convergent Image Reconstruction
Authors: Stanislas Ducotterd, Michael Unser
First: 2025-08-08T17:58:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-06T18:57:10+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We introduce the multivariate fields of experts, a new framework for the learning of image priors. Our model generalizes existing fields of experts methods by incorporating multivariate potential functions constructed via Moreau envelopes of the $\ell_\infty$-norm. We demonstrate the effectiveness of our proposal across a range of inverse problems that include image denoising, deblurring, compressed-sensing magnetic-resonance imaging, and computed tomography. The proposed approach outperforms comparable univariate models and achieves performance close to that of deep-learning-based regularizers while being significantly faster, requiring fewer parameters, and being trained on substantially fewer data. In addition, our model retains a high level of interpretability due to its structured design. It is supported by theoretical convergence guarantees which ensure reliability in sensitive reconstruction tasks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：多变量专家场用于收敛图像重建
我们引入了多变量专家场，这是一种新的图像先验学习框架。我们的模型通过结合利用 $\ell_\infty$-范数的莫雷包络构造的多变量势函数，扩展了现有专家场方法。我们在包括图像去噪、去模糊、压缩感知磁共振成像和计算机断层扫描等多种逆问题中展示了该方法的有效性。所提方法优于同类单变量模型，在性能上接近基于深度学习的正则化方法，同时显著更快、参数更少、训练数据需求更少。此外，由于其结构化设计，该模型保持了较高的可解释性，并有理论收敛保证，确保在敏感重建任务中的可靠性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for efficient and interpretable image priors in inverse problems, this paper proposes multivariate fields of experts, a framework that extends traditional fields of experts by using multivariate potential functions derived from Moreau envelopes of the $\ell_\infty$-norm. The method is applied to tasks such as denoising, deblurring, MRI, and CT reconstruction, where it consistently outperforms univariate models and rivals deep-learning-based regularizers in performance while being faster, requiring fewer parameters, and needing less training data. Theoretical convergence guarantees further validate its reliability in sensitive applications.
为解决逆问题中对高效且可解释图像先验的需求，本文提出多变量领域专家框架，该框架通过使用基于 $\ell_\infty$ 范数 Moreau 包络构造的多变量势函数扩展了传统领域专家方法。该方法在图像去噪、去模糊、压缩感知 MRI 和 CT 重建等任务中表现优异，优于单变量模型，性能接近深度学习正则化方法，同时计算更快、参数更少、训练数据需求更低。其结构化设计保证了高可解释性，且具备理论收敛保证，确保在敏感重建任务中的可靠性。
A recipe for scalable attention-based MLIPs: unlocking long-range accuracy with all-to-all node attention
Authors: Eric Qu, Brandon M. Wood, Aditi S. Krishnapriyan, Zachary W. Ulissi
First: 2026-03-06T18:57:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-06T18:57:04+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Machine-learning interatomic potentials (MLIPs) have advanced rapidly, with many top models relying on strong physics-based inductive biases. However, as models scale to larger systems like biomolecules and electrolytes, they struggle to accurately capture long-range (LR) interactions, leading current approaches to rely on explicit physics-based terms or components. In this work, we propose AllScAIP, a straightforward, attention-based, and energy-conserving MLIP model that scales to O(100 million) training samples. It addresses the long-range challenge using an all-to-all node attention component that is data-driven. Extensive ablations reveal that in low-data/small-model regimes, inductive biases improve sample efficiency. However, as data and model size scale, these benefits diminish or even reverse, while all-to-all attention remains critical for capturing LR interactions. Our model achieves state-of-the-art energy/force accuracy on molecular systems, as well as a number of physics-based evaluations (OMol25), while being competitive on materials (OMat24) and catalysts (OC20). Furthermore, it enables stable, long-timescale MD simulations that accurately recover experimental observables, including density and heat of vaporization predictions.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the difficulty of scaling machine-learning interatomic potentials (MLIPs) to accurately model long-range interactions in large systems, this work introduces AllScAIP, an attention-based, energy-conserving MLIP that scales to tens of millions of training samples. It employs an all-to-all node attention mechanism to capture long-range physics without explicit handcrafted terms. Experiments show that while inductive biases enhance sample efficiency in small models, their advantage fades with larger data and models, whereas all-to-all attention remains essential for long-range accuracy. AllScAIP achieves state-of-the-art energy and force predictions on molecular benchmarks, performs competitively on materials and catalysts, and enables stable molecular dynamics simulations that reproduce key experimental observables.
为了解决机器学习原子势（MLIPs）在大规模系统中难以准确建模长程相互作用的问题，该研究提出AllScAIP模型，这是一种基于注意力机制、能量守恒的MLIP，可扩展至数千万训练样本。它通过全连接节点注意力机制捕捉长程物理效应，无需显式物理项。实验表明，尽管在小模型和低数据场景下归纳偏置能提升样本效率，但随着数据和模型规模增大，其优势减弱甚至逆转，而全连接注意力对长程精度至关重要。AllScAIP在分子系统上达到最先进能量与力预测精度，在材料与催化剂数据集上表现竞争力，并支持稳定分子动力学模拟，准确重现密度与汽化热等实验可观测量。
Boosting deep Reinforcement Learning using pretraining with Logical Options
Authors: Zihan Ye, Phil Chau, Raban Emunds, Jannis Blüml, Cedric Derstroff, Quentin Delfosse, Oleg Arenz, Kristian Kersting
First: 2026-03-06T18:55:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-06T18:55:15+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Deep reinforcement learning agents are often misaligned, as they over-exploit early reward signals. Recently, several symbolic approaches have addressed these challenges by encoding sparse objectives along with aligned plans. However, purely symbolic architectures are complex to scale and difficult to apply to continuous settings. Hence, we propose a hybrid approach, inspired by humans' ability to acquire new skills. We use a two-stage framework that injects symbolic structure into neural-based reinforcement learning agents without sacrificing the expressivity of deep policies. Our method, called Hybrid Hierarchical RL (H^2RL), introduces a logical option-based pretraining strategy to steer the learning policy away from short-term reward loops and toward goal-directed behavior while allowing the final policy to be refined via standard environment interaction. Empirically, we show that this approach consistently improves long-horizon decision-making and yields agents that outperform strong neural, symbolic, and neuro-symbolic baselines.
中文标题/摘要
标题：利用逻辑选项预训练提升深度强化学习
深度强化学习代理常因过度利用早期奖励信号而出现对齐偏差。近期，若干符号方法通过编码稀疏目标与对齐计划解决了这些问题。然而，纯符号架构难以扩展且难应用于连续环境。为此，我们提出一种受人类习得新技能启发的混合方法：采用两阶段框架，将符号结构注入基于神经网络的强化学习代理，同时不牺牲深度策略的表达能力。该方法称为混合分层强化学习（H^2RL），引入基于逻辑选项的预训练策略，引导学习策略脱离短期奖励循环，转向目标导向行为，同时允许最终策略通过标准环境交互进行优化。实验证明，该方法持续提升长时序决策能力，并优于强神经、符号及神经符号基线模型。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the tendency of deep reinforcement learning agents to over-exploit early rewards, this work proposes Hybrid Hierarchical RL (H^2RL), a hybrid method that integrates symbolic logical options into neural policies to guide learning toward goal-directed behavior. The approach uses a two-stage framework: first, a logical option-based pretraining phase steers the policy away from short-term reward loops, then standard environment interaction refines the policy. Experiments show H^2RL consistently improves long-horizon decision-making and outperforms strong neural, symbolic, and neuro-symbolic baselines.
为解决深度强化学习代理过度依赖早期奖励的问题，本文提出混合分层强化学习（H^2RL）方法，通过在神经策略中引入逻辑选项进行预训练，引导其向目标导向行为发展，再通过环境交互优化策略。实验表明，该方法在长时序决策中表现更优，且在多个任务上超越了纯神经、纯符号及神经符号基线模型。
The Pen: Episodic Cognitive Assistance via an Ear-Worn Interface
Authors: Yonatan Tussa, Andy Heredia
First: 2026-03-06T18:53:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-06T18:53:04+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Wearable AI is often designed as always-available, yet continuous availability can conflict with how people work and socialize, creating discomfort around privacy, disruption, and unclear system boundaries. This paper explores episodic use of wearable AI, where assistance is intentionally invoked for short periods of focused activity and set aside when no longer needed, with a form factor that reflects this paradigm of wearing and taking off a device between sessions. We present The Pen, an ear-worn device resembling a pen, for episodic, situated cognitive assistance. The device supports short, on-demand assistance sessions using voice and visual context, with clear start/end boundaries and local processing. We report findings from an exploratory study showing how layered activation boundaries shape users' sense of agency, cognitive flow, and social comfort.

EgoReasoner: Learning Egocentric 4D Reasoning via Task-Adaptive Structured Thinking
Authors: Fangrui Zhu, Yunfeng Xi, Jianmo Ni, Mu Cai, Boqing Gong, Long Zhao, Chen Qu, Ian Miao, Yi Li, Cheng Zhong, Huaizu Jiang, Shwetak Patel
First: 2026-03-06T18:49:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-06T18:49:04+00:00
Comments: preprint
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Egocentric video understanding is inherently complex due to the dynamic 4D nature of the environment, where camera motion and object displacements necessitate a continuous re-evaluation of spatial relations. In this work, we target a suite of under-explored egocentric 4D reasoning tasks, including fixture interaction counting, viewpoint-relative fixture location, object movement itinerary tracking, and stationary object localization, that require fundamentally different cognitive operations: spatial anchoring, temporal tracking, and duration reasoning. We observe that these structural differences make task-agnostic approaches insufficient: generic Chain-of-Thought methods lack task-appropriate reasoning primitives, and uniform reinforcement learning actively destabilizes performance on spatial tasks. To address this, we propose EgoReasoner, a two-stage framework that aligns both the reasoning scaffold and the reward signal to each task's cognitive structure. In the first stage, Task-Adaptive Thinking Templates guide the synthesis of structured CoT traces that teach the model to reason adaptively across task types via supervised fine-tuning. In the second stage, task-aware reward functions verify entity grounding, temporal alignment, and task-adaptive logical consistency, selectively strengthening each reasoning pathway via reinforcement fine-tuning with GRPO. Our 3B-parameter model, trained on only 16K samples, achieves 37.5% average accuracy on the challenging HD-EPIC benchmark, surpassing Qwen2.5-VL-7B (25.7%) by over 10 points.
中文标题/摘要
标题：EgoReasoner：通过任务自适应结构化思维学习第一人称4D推理
由于环境的动态4D特性，第一人称视频理解固有复杂，其中相机运动和物体位移需要持续重新评估空间关系。本文针对一系列未被充分探索的第一人称4D推理任务，包括固定物交互计数、视角相对固定物定位、物体移动轨迹追踪和静止物体定位，这些任务需要根本不同的认知操作：空间锚定、时间追踪和持续时间推理。我们观察到，这些结构差异使得任务无关方法不足：通用Chain-of-Thought方法缺乏任务适配的推理原语，统一强化学习反而会破坏空间任务表现。为此，我们提出EgoReasoner，一个两阶段框架，将推理框架和奖励信号对齐至每个任务的认知结构。第一阶段，任务自适应思维模板指导生成结构化CoT轨迹，通过监督微调教会模型在不同任务间自适应推理。第二阶段，任务感知奖励函数验证实体定位、时间对齐和任务自适应逻辑一致性，通过GRPO强化学习选择性增强各推理路径。我们的3B参数模型仅用16K样本训练，在具有挑战性的HD-EPIC基准上达到37.5%平均准确率，超越Qwen2.5-VL-7B（25.7%）超过10个百分点。

Motivated by the complexity of egocentric video understanding due to dynamic 4D environmental changes, this work addresses four under-explored reasoning tasks requiring distinct cognitive operations: spatial anchoring, temporal tracking, and duration reasoning. The authors propose EgoReasoner, a two-stage framework that adapts reasoning structure and reward signals to each task’s cognitive demands. In the first stage, task-adaptive thinking templates guide supervised fine-tuning to generate structured reasoning traces. In the second stage, task-aware reward functions reinforce grounding, temporal alignment, and logical consistency via GRPO-based reinforcement learning. Evaluated on the HD-EPIC benchmark with only 16K training samples, the 3B-parameter model achieves 37.5% average accuracy, outperforming Qwen2.5-VL-7B by over 10 percentage points.
为应对因动态4D环境导致的自拍视频理解复杂性，本文针对需不同认知操作（如空间锚定与时间追踪）的未充分探索任务提出EgoReasoner框架。该框架分两阶段：第一阶段通过监督微调引导任务自适应思维模板生成结构化推理轨迹；第二阶段通过GRPO强化学习结合任务感知奖励函数，强化实体定位、时间对齐与逻辑一致性。在仅16K样本的HD-EPIC基准上，3B参数模型达成37.5%平均准确率，超越Qwen2.5-VL-7B超10个百分点。
Accelerating Scientific Research with Gemini: Case Studies and Common Techniques
Authors: David P. Woodruff, Vincent Cohen-Addad, Lalit Jain, Jieming Mao, Song Zuo, MohammadHossein Bateni, Simina Branzei, Michael P. Brenner, Lin Chen, Ying Feng, Lance Fortnow, Gang Fu, Ziyi Guan, Zahra Hadizadeh, Mohammad T. Hajiaghayi, Mahdi JafariRaviz, Adel Javanmard, Karthik C. S., Ken-ichi Kawarabayashi, Ravi Kumar, Silvio Lattanzi, Euiwoong Lee, Yi Li, Ioannis Panageas, Dimitris Paparas, Benjamin Przybocki, Bernardo Subercaseaux, Ola Svensson, Shayan Taherijam, Xuan Wu, Eylon Yogev, Morteza Zadimoghaddam, Samson Zhou, Yossi Matias, James Manyika, Vahab Mirrokni
First: 2026-02-03T18:56:17+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-06T18:48:45+00:00
Comments: The changes over version 2 are that we cleaned up the last paragraph on color-coding at the end of section 2. Also, for section 6.1 we added a reference to followup work of the authors, and other minor edits in that section
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have opened new avenues for accelerating scientific research. While models are increasingly capable of assisting with routine tasks, their ability to contribute to novel, expert-level mathematical discovery is less understood. We present a collection of case studies demonstrating how researchers have successfully collaborated with advanced AI models, specifically Google's Gemini-based models (in particular Gemini Deep Think and its advanced variants), to solve open problems, refute conjectures, and generate new proofs across diverse areas in theoretical computer science, as well as other areas such as economics, optimization, and physics. Based on these experiences, we extract common techniques for effective human-AI collaboration in theoretical research, such as iterative refinement, problem decomposition, and cross-disciplinary knowledge transfer. While the majority of our results stem from this interactive, conversational methodology, we also highlight specific instances that push beyond standard chat interfaces. These include deploying the model as a rigorous adversarial reviewer to detect subtle flaws in existing proofs, and embedding it within a "neuro-symbolic" loop that autonomously writes and executes code to verify complex derivations. Together, these examples highlight the potential of AI not just as a tool for automation, but as a versatile, genuine partner in the creative process of scientific discovery.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用Gemini加速科学研究：案例研究与常用技术
大型语言模型（LLMs）的最新进展为加速科学研究开辟了新途径。虽然这些模型在协助常规任务方面日益强大，但其在推动新颖、专家级数学发现方面的潜力尚不为人所熟知。我们呈现了一系列案例研究，展示研究人员如何成功与先进的AI模型（特别是谷歌的Gemini系列模型，尤其是Gemini Deep Think及其高级变体）协作，解决开放性问题、反驳猜想、在理论计算机科学及其他领域（如经济学、优化和物理学）生成新证明。基于这些经验，我们提炼出有效的人机协作通用技术，如迭代优化、问题分解和跨学科知识迁移。尽管大多数成果源于这种交互式对话方法，我们也强调了一些超越标准聊天界面的实例，例如将模型部署为严谨的对抗性审稿人以检测现有证明中的细微缺陷，或将其嵌入“神经符号”循环中，自主编写并执行代码以验证复杂推导。这些案例共同凸显了AI不仅作为自动化工具，更可作为科学发现创造性过程中的多功能、真实伙伴的潜力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the potential of large language models to augment scientific discovery beyond routine tasks, this paper explores how researchers collaborated with Google's Gemini Deep Think and its variants to solve open problems, refute conjectures, and generate new proofs in theoretical computer science and related fields. The authors identify key collaborative techniques including iterative refinement, problem decomposition, and cross-disciplinary knowledge transfer, derived from real-world case studies. Experimental results demonstrate that AI can serve as a genuine creative partner, not merely an automation tool, through methods such as adversarial proof review and neuro-symbolic loops that autonomously generate and execute code to verify complex derivations.
鉴于大型语言模型在辅助科学发现方面的潜力，本文探讨了研究人员如何利用谷歌的Gemini Deep Think及其变体，在理论计算机科学、经济学、优化和物理学等领域解决开放性问题、反驳猜想并生成证明。研究通过案例分析展示了人类与AI协作中常用的技巧，如迭代优化、问题分解和跨学科知识迁移，多通过对话式界面实现。主要成果表明，AI不仅能作为严格的对抗性审稿人发现证明中的细微缺陷，还能在神经符号循环中自主编写并执行代码以验证复杂推导，体现出其作为创造性合作者而非单纯自动化工具的价值。
Towards Scalable Pre-training of Visual Tokenizers for Generation
Authors: Jingfeng Yao, Yuda Song, Yucong Zhou, Xinggang Wang
First: 2025-12-15T18:59:54+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-06T18:47:02+00:00
Comments: Our pre-trained models are available at https://github.com/MiniMax-AI/VTP
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
The quality of the latent space in visual tokenizers (e.g., VAEs) is crucial for modern generative models. However, the standard reconstruction-based training paradigm produces a latent space that is biased towards low-level information, leading to a foundation flaw: better pixel-level accuracy does not lead to higher-quality generation. This implies that pouring extensive compute into visual tokenizer pre-training translates poorly to improved performance in generation. We identify this as the ``pre-training scaling problem`` and suggest a necessary shift: to be effective for generation, a latent space must concisely represent high-level semantics. We present VTP, a unified visual tokenizer pre-training framework, pioneering the joint optimization of image-text contrastive, self-supervised, and reconstruction losses. Our large-scale study reveals two principal findings: (1) understanding is a key driver of generation, and (2) much better scaling properties, where generative performance scales effectively with compute, parameters, and data allocated to the pretraining of the visual tokenizer. After large-scale pre-training, our tokenizer delivers a competitive profile (78.2 zero-shot accuracy and 0.36 rFID on ImageNet) and 4.1 times faster convergence on generation compared to advanced distillation methods. More importantly, it scales effectively: without modifying standard DiT training specs, solely investing more FLOPS in pretraining VTP achieves 65.8\% FID improvement in downstream generation, while conventional autoencoder stagnates very early at 1/10 FLOPS. Our pre-trained models are available at https://github.com/MiniMax-AI/VTP.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向生成任务的视觉标记器可扩展预训练方法
视觉标记器（如VAE）的潜在空间质量对现代生成模型至关重要。然而，标准的重建式训练范式导致潜在空间偏向低层次信息，造成基础性缺陷：更高的像素级精度并不意味着生成质量提升。这表明，将大量计算资源投入视觉标记器预训练，难以有效转化为生成性能的提升。我们将此问题称为“预训练扩展问题”，并提出必要转变：为生成任务有效，潜在空间必须简洁地表征高层次语义。我们提出VTP，一个统一的视觉标记器预训练框架，首次联合优化图像-文本对比、自监督和重建损失。大规模研究表明两大发现：（1）理解能力是生成的关键驱动力；（2）预训练具有更优的扩展性，生成性能随预训练所分配的计算、参数和数据量有效提升。大规模预训练后，我们的标记器在ImageNet上达到78.2%零样本准确率和0.36 rFID，并比先进蒸馏方法快4.1倍收敛。更重要的是，其具备良好扩展性：在不修改标准DiT训练规格的前提下，仅增加预训练VTP的FLOPS，即可在下游生成中实现65.8% FID提升，而传统自编码器在1/10 FLOPS时即停滞不前。预训练模型见https://github.com/MiniMax-AI/VTP。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that standard visual tokenizer pre-training prioritizes pixel-level reconstruction over high-level semantic understanding, which harms generative performance, the authors propose VTP, a unified pre-training framework that jointly optimizes image-text contrastive, self-supervised, and reconstruction losses. Their large-scale experiments reveal that semantic understanding drives generation quality and that VTP exhibits superior scaling properties: increasing compute, parameters, or data during pre-training leads to proportional gains in downstream generation. VTP achieves competitive ImageNet results (78.2 zero-shot accuracy, 0.36 rFID) and converges 4.1x faster than distillation methods, while conventional autoencoders show minimal improvement even with 10x more compute.
由于标准视觉标记器预训练过度关注像素级重建，导致生成质量受限，作者提出VTP框架，联合优化图像-文本对比、自监督与重建损失。大规模实验表明，语义理解是生成质量的关键驱动因素，且VTP具备优异的扩展性：增加预训练的计算、参数或数据量，下游生成性能可成比例提升。VTP在ImageNet上达到78.2零样本准确率和0.36 rFID的竞争力表现，比先进蒸馏方法快4.1倍收敛，而传统自编码器即使增加10倍计算量也几乎无提升。
CASA: Cross-Attention over Self-Attention for Efficient Vision-Language Fusion
Authors: Moritz Böhle, Amélie Royer, Juliette Marrie, Edouard Grave, Patrick Pérez
First: 2025-12-22T16:21:39+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-06T18:46:27+00:00
Comments: updated with improved CA results
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Vision-language models (VLMs) are commonly trained by directly inserting image tokens from a pretrained vision encoder into the text stream of a language model. This allows text and image information to fully attend to one another within the model, but becomes rapidly costly for long multi-image conversations or streaming video applications, both in terms of memory and compute. VLMs leveraging cross-attention (CA) are an efficient alternative to token insertion as image tokens are not added to the KV cache. Despite being introduced early on, multimodal CA models are scarce in the current VLM literature and often underperform their token insertion counterparts. In this work, we reinvestigate the effectiveness of cross-attention for vision-language modeling: (i) We analyze the core differences between the cross-attention and self-attention mechanisms, (ii) we train cross-attention VLMs both from a text-only LLM and by adapting a pretrained insertion-based VLM, showing that simple cross-attention is far more competitive with token insertion than previously reported, and (iii) we demonstrate the practical advantages of cross-attention on real-time video captioning, where it naturally maintains low latency and near-constant memory cost. For samples and code, please see our project page at https://kyutai.org/casa .
中文标题/摘要
标题：CASA：基于自注意力的跨注意力高效视觉-语言融合
视觉-语言模型（VLMs）通常通过将预训练视觉编码器生成的图像标记直接插入语言模型的文本流中进行训练。这使得文本和图像信息能在模型内充分相互关注，但在长多图像对话或流媒体视频应用中，其内存和计算成本会迅速上升。利用跨注意力（CA）的VLMs是图像标记不加入KV缓存的高效替代方案。尽管跨注意力模型早已有提出，但当前VLM文献中此类模型仍较少见，且常表现不如标记插入方案。本研究重新评估跨注意力在视觉-语言建模中的有效性：（i）分析跨注意力与自注意力机制的核心差异；（ii）从纯文本LLM和适配预训练插入式VLM两种方式训练跨注意力VLMs，表明简单跨注意力比以往报告的更具竞争力；（iii）在实时视频字幕生成任务中展示跨注意力的实用优势，其天然保持低延迟和近常量内存开销。项目示例与代码请见：https://kyutai.org/casa。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high memory and compute costs of inserting image tokens into language models for vision-language tasks, this work re-examines cross-attention as a more efficient alternative. It compares cross-attention with self-attention mechanisms, trains cross-attention models from scratch and by adapting existing token-insertion models, and finds that cross-attention performs competitively with token insertion. Experiments on real-time video captioning confirm that cross-attention maintains low latency and stable memory usage, making it practical for streaming applications.
为解决视觉-语言模型中插入图像token导致的高内存与计算开销问题，本文重新评估了跨注意力机制的有效性。通过对比跨注意力与自注意力机制，从零训练及适配现有插入式模型，结果表明跨注意力表现优于以往报道的水平。在实时视频字幕生成任务中，跨注意力天然保持低延迟与稳定内存消耗，具备实际应用价值。
Causal Interpretation of Neural Network Computations with Contribution Decomposition
Authors: Joshua Brendan Melander, Zaki Alaoui, Shenghua Liu, Surya Ganguli, Stephen A. Baccus
Venue: ICLR 2026 poster
First: 2026-03-06T18:46:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-06T18:46:06+00:00
Comments: 32 pages, 19 figures. ICLR 2026 poster
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Understanding how neural networks transform inputs into outputs is crucial for interpreting and manipulating their behavior. Most existing approaches analyze internal representations by identifying hidden-layer activation patterns correlated with human-interpretable concepts. Here we take a direct approach to examine how hidden neurons act to drive network outputs. We introduce CODEC (Contribution Decomposition), a method that uses sparse autoencoders to decompose network behavior into sparse motifs of hidden-neuron contributions, revealing causal processes that cannot be determined by analyzing activations alone. Applying CODEC to benchmark image-classification networks, we find that contributions grow in sparsity and dimensionality across layers and, unexpectedly, that they progressively decorrelate positive and negative effects on network outputs. We further show that decomposing contributions into sparse modes enables greater control and interpretation of intermediate layers, supporting both causal manipulations of network output and human-interpretable visualizations of distinct image components that combine to drive that output. Finally, by analyzing state-of-the-art models of neural activity in the vertebrate retina, we demonstrate that CODEC uncovers combinatorial actions of model interneurons and identifies the sources of dynamic receptive fields. Overall, CODEC provides a rich and interpretable framework for understanding how nonlinear computations evolve across hierarchical layers, establishing contribution modes as an informative unit of analysis for mechanistic insights into artificial neural networks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过贡献分解对神经网络计算的因果解释
理解神经网络如何将输入转化为输出，对于解释和操控其行为至关重要。现有大多数方法通过识别与人类可解释概念相关的隐藏层激活模式来分析内部表示。本文采用直接方法，考察隐藏神经元如何驱动网络输出。我们引入CODEC（贡献分解）方法，利用稀疏自编码器将网络行为分解为隐藏神经元贡献的稀疏模式，揭示仅通过激活分析无法确定的因果过程。将CODEC应用于基准图像分类网络，我们发现贡献在层间逐渐变得稀疏且维度增加，且意外地发现其正负效应在输出上逐步解相关。进一步，我们将贡献分解为稀疏模式，使中间层的控制与解释能力增强，支持对网络输出的因果操控及对驱动该输出的图像成分的人类可解释可视化。最后，通过对脊椎动物视网膜神经活动的前沿模型分析，我们证明CODEC揭示了模型中间神经元的组合性作用，并识别了动态感受野的来源。总体而言，CODEC提供了一个丰富且可解释的框架，用于理解非线性计算如何在分层结构中演化，确立贡献模式作为解析人工神经网络机制的有用分析单元。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand how neural networks causally transform inputs into outputs, this work introduces CODEC (Contribution Decomposition), a method using sparse autoencoders to break down network behavior into sparse, interpretable contributions from hidden neurons. Applied to image classification models, CODEC reveals that neuron contributions become sparser and more dimensionally distinct across layers, and surprisingly, increasingly decorrelate positive and negative effects on outputs. The method enables precise causal manipulation of outputs and interpretable visualizations of image components driving predictions, and when applied to retinal neural models, it identifies combinatorial interneuron actions and dynamic receptive field origins, offering a novel framework for mechanistic analysis of nonlinear computations in deep networks.
为理解神经网络如何因果地将输入转化为输出，本文提出CODEC（贡献分解）方法，利用稀疏自编码器将网络行为分解为隐藏神经元的稀疏贡献。在图像分类模型中应用时，发现神经元贡献随层数增加而更稀疏、维度更分散，且正负影响逐渐解耦。该方法支持对输出的精准因果操控和图像驱动成分的可解释可视化，并在视网膜神经模型中揭示了中间神经元的组合作用与动态感受野来源，为深度网络非线性计算机制提供了新的分析框架。
Capability at a Glance: Design Guidelines for Intuitive Avatars Communicating Augmented Actions in Virtual Reality
Authors: Yang Lu, Tianyu Zhang, Jiamu Tang, Yanna Lin, Jiankun Yang, Longyu Zhang, Shijian Luo, Yukang Yan
First: 2026-03-06T18:45:30+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-06T18:45:30+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Virtual Reality (VR) enables users to engage with capabilities beyond human limitations, but it is not always obvious how to trigger these capabilities. Taking the lens of Affordance, we believe avatar design is the key to solving this issue, which ideally should communicate its capabilities and how to activate them. To understand the current practice, we selected eight capabilities across four categories and invited twelve professional designers to design avatars that communicate the capabilities and their corresponding interactions. From the resulting designs, we formed 16 guidelines to provide general and category-specific recommendations. Then, we validated these guidelines by letting two groups of twelve participants design avatars with and without guidelines. Participants rated the guidelines' clarity and usefulness highly. External judges confirmed that avatars designed with the guidelines were more intuitive in conveying the capabilities and interaction methods. Finally, we demonstrated the applicability of the guidelines in avatar design for four VR applications.
中文标题/摘要
标题：一目了然的能力：虚拟现实中文身角色传达增强动作的设计指南
虚拟现实（VR）使用户能够体验超越人类极限的能力，但如何触发这些能力并不总是显而易见。从可操作性视角出发，我们认为文身角色设计是解决此问题的关键，理想情况下应传达其能力及如何激活。为了解当前实践，我们选取了四个类别中的八种能力，邀请十二位专业设计师设计能传达能力及其对应交互的文身角色。从所得设计中，我们提炼出16条通用及类别特定的建议。随后，我们通过让两组十二名参与者分别在有无指南的情况下设计文身角色，验证了这些指南的有效性。参与者对指南的清晰度和实用性评价很高。外部评审确认，依据指南设计的文身角色在传达能力与交互方式上更为直观。最后，我们展示了这些指南在四个VR应用中的适用性。
Summary / 总结
This study addresses the challenge in VR of intuitively communicating augmented capabilities to users, proposing that avatar design should leverage affordance principles to make interactions obvious. Through a design study involving twelve professionals who created avatars for eight distinct capabilities across four categories, the researchers distilled 16 design guidelines tailored to both general and category-specific contexts. These guidelines were then validated via a user study with two groups of twelve participants, who rated them highly for clarity and usefulness; external judges also confirmed that avatars designed using the guidelines were more intuitive in conveying capabilities and interaction methods, demonstrating their practical applicability across four VR applications.
该研究旨在解决VR中增强能力直观传达的问题，提出通过“可操作性”视角，使虚拟形象设计能清晰指示可用功能及其激活方式。研究邀请12位专业设计师为四大类别的八项能力设计虚拟形象，提炼出16条设计指南。随后通过用户实验，两组参与者分别在有无指南条件下设计虚拟形象，结果显示用户和外部评审均认为指南组设计更清晰直观，验证了指南的有效性，并在四项VR应用中成功应用。
Hierarchical Industrial Demand Forecasting with Temporal and Uncertainty Explanations
Authors: Harshavardhan Kamarthi, Shangqing Xu, Xinjie Tong, Xingyu Zhou, James Peters, Joseph Czyzyk, B. Aditya Prakash
First: 2026-03-06T18:44:37+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-06T18:44:37+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Hierarchical time-series forecasting is essential for demand prediction across various industries. While machine learning models have obtained significant accuracy and scalability on such forecasting tasks, the interpretability of their predictions, informed by application, is still largely unexplored. To bridge this gap, we introduce a novel interpretability method for large hierarchical probabilistic time-series forecasting, adapting generic interpretability techniques while addressing challenges associated with hierarchical structures and uncertainty. Our approach offers valuable interpretative insights in response to real-world industrial supply chain scenarios, including 1) the significance of various time-series within the hierarchy and external variables at specific time points, 2) the impact of different variables on forecast uncertainty, and 3) explanations for forecast changes in response to modifications in the training dataset. To evaluate the explainability method, we generate semi-synthetic datasets based on real-world scenarios of explaining hierarchical demands for over ten thousand products at a large chemical company. The experiments showed that our explainability method successfully explained state-of-the-art industrial forecasting methods with significantly higher explainability accuracy. Furthermore, we provide multiple real-world case studies that show the efficacy of our approach in identifying important patterns and explanations that help stakeholders better understand the forecasts. Additionally, our method facilitates the identification of key drivers behind forecasted demand, enabling more informed decision-making and strategic planning. Our approach helps build trust and confidence among users, ultimately leading to better adoption and utilization of hierarchical forecasting models in practice.
中文标题/摘要
标题：具有时序与不确定性解释的分层工业需求预测
分层时间序列预测对于各行业的需求预测至关重要。尽管机器学习模型在该类预测任务中已取得显著精度和可扩展性，但其预测的可解释性，特别是结合实际应用场景，仍鲜有探索。为弥合这一差距，我们提出了一种针对大规模分层概率时间序列预测的新颖可解释性方法，在借鉴通用可解释性技术的同时，解决了分层结构和不确定性带来的挑战。该方法针对现实工业供应链场景，提供了有价值的解释性洞察，包括：1）层级内各时间序列及特定时间点外部变量的重要性；2）不同变量对预测不确定性的影；3）训练数据变更时预测变化的解释。为评估该可解释性方法，我们基于一家大型化工企业对一万多种产品分层需求的现实场景，生成了半合成数据集。实验表明，我们的可解释性方法能显著提升对当前最先进工业预测方法的解释准确性。此外，我们提供了多个现实案例，证明该方法能有效识别重要模式和解释，帮助利益相关者更好地理解预测结果。同时，该方法有助于识别驱动预测需求的关键因素，支持更明智的决策和战略规划。我们的方法有助于建立用户对模型的信任与信心，最终推动分层预测模型在实践中的更好采纳与应用。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for interpretable hierarchical demand forecasting in industrial settings, this paper proposes a novel explainability method tailored for large-scale probabilistic time-series models, addressing challenges posed by hierarchical structures and uncertainty. The method adapts generic interpretability techniques to provide insights into the significance of time-series components and external variables, the influence of variables on forecast uncertainty, and how forecast changes respond to training data modifications. Evaluated on semi-synthetic datasets derived from real-world demand scenarios across over ten thousand products at a chemical company, the approach demonstrated significantly higher explainability accuracy compared to state-of-the-art forecasting models. Real-world case studies further validated its ability to uncover key demand drivers, enhancing stakeholder understanding and facilitating more informed decision-making.
为解决工业场景中需求预测模型缺乏可解释性的问题，本文提出了一种面向分层概率时间序列预测的新型可解释性方法。该方法将通用可解释性技术适配于分层结构与不确定性挑战，能够揭示时间序列组件、外部变量及训练数据变化对预测结果的影响。在基于某大型化工企业真实需求场景构建的半合成数据集上，该方法显著优于现有先进预测模型的可解释性精度。多个实际案例验证了其识别关键需求驱动因素的能力，有助于提升决策者对预测结果的理解，从而支持更明智的战略规划。
Measuring AI R&D Automation
Authors: Alan Chan, Ranay Padarath, Joe Kwon, Hilary Greaves, Markus Anderljung
First: 2026-03-04T12:36:13+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-06T18:41:24+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The automation of AI R&D (AIRDA) could have significant implications, but its extent and ultimate effects remain uncertain. We need empirical data to resolve these uncertainties, but existing data (primarily capability benchmarks) may not reflect real-world automation or capture its broader consequences, such as whether AIRDA accelerates capabilities more than safety progress or whether our ability to oversee AI R&D can keep pace with its acceleration. To address these gaps, this work proposes metrics to track the extent of AIRDA and its effects on AI progress and oversight. The metrics span dimensions such as capital share of AI R&D spending, researcher time allocation, and AI subversion incidents, and could help decision makers understand the potential consequences of AIRDA, implement appropriate safety measures, and maintain awareness of the pace of AI development. We recommend that companies and third parties (e.g. non-profit research organisations) start to track these metrics, and that governments support these efforts.
中文标题/摘要
标题：衡量人工智能研发自动化
人工智能研发自动化（AIRDA）可能产生重大影响，但其程度和最终效果仍不确定。我们需要实证数据来解决这些不确定性，但现有数据（主要为能力基准）可能无法反映现实中的自动化或捕捉其更广泛的影响，例如AIRDA是否比安全进展更快地加速能力，或我们对AI研发的监管能力能否跟上其加速步伐。为弥补这些不足，本研究提出指标，用于追踪AIRDA的程度及其对AI进展和监管的影响。这些指标涵盖AI研发支出中的资本占比、研究人员时间分配、AI系统越狱事件等维度，有助于决策者理解AIRDA的潜在后果，实施适当的安全措施，并保持对AI发展速度的关注。我们建议企业及第三方（如非营利研究机构）开始追踪这些指标，政府应支持这些努力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand the real-world impact and consequences of AI R&D automation, this study proposes a set of empirical metrics to measure its extent and effects on AI progress and oversight. The method involves tracking dimensions such as the capital share of AI R&D spending, researcher time allocation, and incidents of AI subversion, aiming to capture whether automation accelerates capability gains faster than safety progress and whether oversight can keep pace. Experimental findings suggest that current benchmarks fail to reflect automation’s true scope, and the proposed metrics offer a more comprehensive view to inform policy and safety measures.
为了解决对AI研发自动化（AIRDA）实际影响和后果缺乏实证数据的问题，本研究提出一套用于衡量AIRDA程度及其对AI进展与监管影响的指标体系。方法涵盖AI研发支出中资本占比、研究人员时间分配及AI系统越权事件等维度，旨在评估自动化是否加速能力提升超过安全进展，以及监管能力能否跟上发展速度。实验结果表明，现有能力基准无法反映自动化的真实范围，所提指标可为政策制定和安全措施提供更全面的依据。
Conditionally Site-Independent Neural Evolution of Antibody Sequences
Authors: Stephen Zhewen Lu, Aakarsh Vermani, Kohei Sanno, Jiarui Lu, Frederick A Matsen, Milind Jagota, Yun S. Song
First: 2026-02-21T23:23:30+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-06T18:40:20+00:00
Comments: 24 pages, 14 figures. Currently under review
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Common deep learning approaches for antibody engineering focus on modeling the marginal distribution of sequences. By treating sequences as independent samples, however, these methods overlook affinity maturation as a rich and largely untapped source of information about the evolutionary process by which antibodies explore the underlying fitness landscape. In contrast, classical phylogenetic models explicitly represent evolutionary dynamics but lack the expressivity to capture complex epistatic interactions. We bridge this gap with CoSiNE, a continuous-time Markov chain parameterized by a deep neural network. Mathematically, we prove that CoSiNE provides a first-order approximation to the intractable sequential point mutation process, capturing epistatic effects with an error bound that is quadratic in branch length. Empirically, CoSiNE outperforms state-of-the-art language models in zero-shot variant effect prediction by explicitly disentangling selection from context-dependent somatic hypermutation. Finally, we introduce Guided Gillespie, a classifier-guided sampling scheme that steers CoSiNE at inference time, enabling efficient optimization of antibody binding affinity toward specific antigens.
中文标题/摘要
标题：条件独立于位点的抗体序列神经进化
抗体工程的常用深度学习方法侧重于建模序列的边缘分布。然而，将序列视为独立样本的方法忽略了亲和力成熟作为抗体探索潜在适应度景观的丰富且尚未充分挖掘的进化信息来源。相比之下，经典系统发育模型虽明确表示进化动力学，但缺乏表达复杂上位互作的能力。我们通过CoSiNE弥合这一差距，CoSiNE是一个由深度神经网络参数化的连续时间马尔可夫链。数学上，我们证明CoSiNE对不可计算的顺序点突变过程提供了一阶近似，其上位效应误差与分支长度的平方成正比。实证上，CoSiNE在零样本变异效应预测中优于当前最先进的语言模型，通过显式分离选择与上下文依赖的体细胞超突变。最后，我们引入了引导式吉尔斯皮采样方案，该方案在推理时引导CoSiNE，从而高效优化抗体对特定抗原的结合亲和力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to model antibody evolution beyond marginal sequence distributions, the authors propose CoSiNE, a neural network parameterized continuous-time Markov chain that captures epistatic interactions during affinity maturation. CoSiNE approximates the intractable sequential mutation process with a first-order error bound quadratic in branch length, bridging the gap between classical phylogenetic models and modern deep learning. Experimentally, CoSiNE surpasses state-of-the-art language models in zero-shot variant effect prediction by disentangling selection from somatic hypermutation context, and Guided Gillespie enables efficient affinity optimization toward specific antigens.
为更准确建模抗体进化过程，作者提出CoSiNE模型，该模型通过神经网络参数化连续时间马尔可夫链，捕捉抗体成熟过程中的表型互作效应。该模型对复杂突变序列过程提供一阶近似，误差与分支长度平方成正比，能有效分离选择压力与体细胞突变的上下文依赖性。实验表明，CoSiNE在零样本变体效应预测上优于现有语言模型，并通过引导Gillespie采样方案实现对特定抗原结合亲和力的高效优化。
KCLarity at SemEval-2026 Task 6: Encoder and Zero-Shot Approaches to Political Evasion Detection
Authors: Archie Sage, Salvatore Greco
First: 2026-03-06T18:39:37+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-06T18:39:37+00:00
Comments: Under review at SemEval 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper describes the KCLarity team's participation in CLARITY, a shared task at SemEval 2026 on classifying ambiguity and evasion techniques in political discourse. We investigate two modelling formulations: (i) directly predicting the clarity label, and (ii) predicting the evasion label and deriving clarity through the task taxonomy hierarchy. We further explore several auxiliary training variants and evaluate decoder-only models in a zero-shot setting under the evasion-first formulation. Overall, the two formulations yield comparable performance. Among encoder-based models, RoBERTa-large achieves the strongest results on the public test set, while zero-shot GPT-5.2 generalises better on the hidden evaluation set.
中文标题/摘要
标题：KCLarity在SemEval-2026任务6中的表现：政治规避检测的编码器与零样本方法
本文描述了KCLarity团队在SemEval 2026的CLARITY共享任务中的参与情况，该任务旨在对政治话语中的模糊性和规避技术进行分类。我们研究了两种建模形式：(i) 直接预测清晰度标签，(ii) 预测规避标签并通过任务分类学层级推导清晰度。我们进一步探索了多种辅助训练变体，并在规避优先的建模形式下评估了仅解码器模型的零样本表现。总体而言，两种建模形式表现相当。在编码器模型中，RoBERTa-large在公开测试集上表现最佳，而零样本GPT-5.2在隐藏评估集上泛化能力更强。

Motivated by the need to detect political evasion and ambiguity in discourse, the KCLarity team explored two modeling approaches: direct clarity prediction and evasion prediction followed by hierarchical derivation. They evaluated encoder-based models and zero-shot decoder-only models, including RoBERTa-large and GPT-5.2, with auxiliary training variants. Results showed comparable performance between the two formulations, with RoBERTa-large leading on the public test set and GPT-5.2 demonstrating superior generalization on the hidden evaluation set.
为检测政治话语中的规避与模糊现象，KCLarity团队探索了两种建模方法：直接预测清晰度标签，或预测规避标签并通过分类学层级推导清晰度。他们评估了包括RoBERTa-large在内的编码器模型和零样本解码器模型（如GPT-5.2），并测试了多种辅助训练变体。实验表明两种方法性能相当，其中RoBERTa-large在公开测试集上表现最佳，而GPT-5.2在隐藏评估集上泛化能力更强。
ContextBench: Modifying Contexts for Targeted Latent Activation
Authors: Robert Graham, Edward Stevinson, Leo Richter, Alexander Chia, Joseph Miller, Joseph Isaac Bloom
Venue: ICLR 2026
First: 2025-06-15T16:54:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-06T18:37:24+00:00
Comments: Published at ICLR 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Identifying inputs that trigger specific behaviours or latent features in language models could have a wide range of safety use cases. We investigate a class of methods capable of generating targeted, linguistically fluent inputs that activate specific latent features or elicit model behaviours. We formalise this approach as context modification and present ContextBench -- a benchmark with tasks assessing core method capabilities and potential safety applications. Our evaluation framework measures both elicitation strength (activation of latent features or behaviours) and linguistic fluency, highlighting how current state-of-the-art methods struggle to balance these objectives. We enhance Evolutionary Prompt Optimisation (EPO) with LLM-assistance and diffusion model inpainting, and demonstrate that these variants achieve state-of-the-art performance in balancing elicitation effectiveness and fluency.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ContextBench：通过修改上下文实现目标潜在激活
识别能触发语言模型特定行为或潜在特征的输入，可广泛应用于安全场景。我们研究了一类能够生成目标导向、语言流畅的输入的方法，以激活特定潜在特征或引发模型行为。我们将此方法形式化为上下文修改，并提出ContextBench——一个评估核心方法能力及潜在安全应用的基准测试。我们的评估框架同时衡量激活强度（潜在特征或行为的激活程度）与语言流畅性，揭示当前最先进方法在平衡这两项目标时的困难。我们通过引入大语言模型辅助和扩散模型修复，改进了进化提示优化（EPO），并证明这些变体在平衡激活效果与流畅性方面达到最先进水平。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to safely control language model behaviors through targeted latent activation, this work introduces ContextBench, a benchmark evaluating methods that generate linguistically fluent inputs to elicit specific model behaviors. The authors formalize this as context modification and enhance Evolutionary Prompt Optimisation (EPO) with LLM-assisted guidance and diffusion model inpainting to improve performance. Experimental results show that these enhanced variants outperform existing methods by better balancing elicitation strength—measured as activation of target latent features—with linguistic fluency, demonstrating improved control over model outputs without sacrificing naturalness.
为实现对语言模型行为的安全可控，本研究提出ContextBench基准，用于评估能生成语言流畅输入以激活特定模型行为的方法。作者将该任务形式化为上下文修改，并通过结合大语言模型辅助与扩散模型修复增强进化提示优化（EPO）方法。实验表明，这些改进版本在激活目标潜在特征的同时更优地保持了语言流畅性，显著优于现有方法，实现了对模型输出更精细的控制。
Uncertainty-Aware Adaptive Dynamics For Underwater Vehicle-Manipulator Robots
Authors: Edward Morgan, Nenyi K Dadson, Corina Barbalata
First: 2026-03-06T18:35:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-06T18:35:48+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Accurate and adaptive dynamic models are critical for underwater vehicle-manipulator systems where hydrodynamic effects induce time-varying parameters. This paper introduces a novel uncertainty-aware adaptive dynamics model framework that remains linear in lumped vehicle and manipulator parameters, and embeds convex physical consistency constraints during online estimation. Moving horizon estimation is used to stack horizon regressors, enforce realizable inertia, damping, friction, and hydrostatics, and quantify uncertainty from parameter evolution. Experiments on a BlueROV2 Heavy with a 4-DOF manipulator demonstrate rapid convergence and calibrated predictions. Manipulator fits achieve R2 = 0.88 to 0.98 with slopes near unity, while vehicle surge, heave, and roll are reproduced with good fidelity under stronger coupling and noise. Median solver time is approximately 0.023 s per update, confirming online feasibility. A comparison against a fixed parameter model shows consistent reductions in MAE and RMSE across degrees of freedom. Results indicate physically plausible parameters and confidence intervals with near 100% coverage, enabling reliable feedforward control and simulation in underwater environments.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向水下车辆- manipulator 机器人系统的不确定性感知自适应动力学模型
对于受水动力效应影响而产生时变参数的水下车辆- manipulator 系统，精确且自适应的动力学模型至关重要。本文提出了一种新颖的不确定性感知自适应动力学模型框架，该框架在聚合的车辆与 manipulator 参数上保持线性，并在在线估计过程中嵌入凸物理一致性约束。采用移动地平线估计方法，堆叠地平线回归器，强制实现可实现的惯性、阻尼、摩擦和静水力，并量化参数演化带来的不确定性。在配备4自由度 manipulator 的 BlueROV2 Heavy 上的实验表明，该模型收敛迅速且预测校准良好。 manipulator 的拟合 R² 值为 0.88 至 0.98，斜率接近1；在更强耦合与噪声条件下，车辆纵荡、垂荡和横滚运动的再现效果良好。每更新的中位数求解时间为约0.023秒，证实了在线可行性。与固定参数模型相比，各自由度的 MAE 和 RMSE 均持续降低。结果表明，参数与置信区间具有物理合理性，覆盖率达近100%，从而支持水下环境中可靠的前馈控制与仿真。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for accurate and adaptive dynamic models in underwater vehicle-manipulator systems affected by time-varying hydrodynamic parameters, this paper proposes an uncertainty-aware adaptive dynamics framework that remains linear in lumped parameters while embedding convex physical constraints during online estimation. It employs moving horizon estimation to stack regressors, enforce realizable inertia and damping, and quantify parameter uncertainty. Experiments on a BlueROV2 with a 4-DOF manipulator show rapid convergence, high R2 values (0.88–0.98) for manipulator fits, and faithful reproduction of vehicle motion under coupling and noise. The solver runs in 0.023 s per update, proving online feasibility, and outperforms a fixed-parameter model with consistent reductions in MAE and RMSE across degrees of freedom, yielding physically plausible parameters with near 100% confidence interval coverage.
为应对水下车辆- manipulator 系统中因水动力效应导致的时变参数问题，本文提出一种不确定性感知的自适应动力学框架，该框架在参数聚合形式下保持线性，并在在线估计过程中嵌入凸物理一致性约束。通过移动地平线估计方法，该方法堆叠回归器以强制执行可实现的惯性、阻尼、摩擦和静水参数，同时量化参数演化带来的不确定性。在搭载4自由度 manipulator 的BlueROV2实验中，系统快速收敛， manipulator 动态拟合R2达0.88至0.98，斜率接近1，车辆纵向、垂向和横滚运动在强耦合与噪声下仍能高保真复现。每步更新平均耗时0.023秒，证实在线可行性；相较固定参数模型，各自由度MAE和RMSE均显著降低，参数结果物理合理，置信区间覆盖率达近100%，支持可靠前馈控制与水下仿真。
PepEDiff: Zero-Shot Peptide Binder Design via Protein Embedding Diffusion
Authors: Po-Yu Liang, Tibo Duran, Jun Bai
First: 2026-01-19T19:07:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-06T18:34:27+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
We present PepEDiff, a novel peptide binder generator that designs binding sequences given a target receptor protein sequence and its pocket residues. Peptide binder generation is critical in therapeutic and biochemical applications, yet many existing methods rely heavily on intermediate structure prediction, adding complexity and limiting sequence diversity. Our approach departs from this paradigm by generating binder sequences directly in a continuous latent space derived from a pretrained protein embedding model, without relying on predicted structures, thereby improving structural and sequence diversity. To encourage the model to capture binding-relevant features rather than memorizing known sequences, we perform latent-space exploration and diffusion-based sampling, enabling the generation of peptides beyond the limited distribution of known binders. This zero-shot generative strategy leverages the global protein embedding manifold as a semantic prior, allowing the model to propose novel peptide sequences in previously unseen regions of the protein space. We evaluate PepEDiff on TIGIT, a challenging target with a large, flat protein-protein interaction interface that lacks a druggable pocket. Despite its simplicity, our method outperforms state-of-the-art approaches across benchmark tests and in the TIGIT case study, demonstrating its potential as a general, structure-free framework for zero-shot peptide binder design. The code for this research is available at GitHub: https://github.com/LabJunBMI/PepEDiff-An-Peptide-binder-Embedding-Diffusion-Model
中文标题/摘要
标题：PepEDiff：通过蛋白质嵌入扩散实现零样本肽结合物设计
我们提出了PepEDiff，一种新颖的肽结合物生成器，可根据目标受体蛋白序列及其口袋残基设计结合序列。肽结合物生成在治疗和生化应用中至关重要，但许多现有方法高度依赖中间结构预测，增加了复杂性并限制了序列多样性。我们的方法突破了这一范式，直接在预训练蛋白质嵌入模型导出的连续潜在空间中生成结合序列，无需依赖预测结构，从而提升了结构和序列多样性。为鼓励模型捕捉与结合相关的特征而非记忆已知序列，我们进行潜在空间探索和基于扩散的采样，使模型能够生成超出已知结合物有限分布范围的肽类。这种零样本生成策略利用全局蛋白质嵌入流形作为语义先验，使模型能够在蛋白质空间中此前未见的区域提出新颖的肽序列。我们在TIGIT（一个具有大而平坦的蛋白-蛋白相互作用界面、缺乏可成药口袋的挑战性靶点）上评估PepEDiff，尽管方法简单，但在基准测试和TIGIT案例研究中均优于现有最先进方法，证明其作为零样本肽结合物设计通用、无结构框架的潜力。本研究代码可在GitHub获取：https://github.com/LabJunBMI/PepEDiff-An-Peptide-binder-Embedding-Diffusion-Model
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for efficient, structure-free peptide binder design for therapeutic applications, PepEDiff generates binding sequences directly in a continuous latent space derived from a pretrained protein embedding model, bypassing intermediate structure prediction. It employs diffusion-based sampling and latent-space exploration to encourage the model to discover binding-relevant features rather than memorizing known sequences, enabling zero-shot generation of novel peptides. Evaluated on TIGIT—a challenging target with a flat interaction interface lacking a druggable pocket—PepEDiff outperforms state-of-the-art methods, demonstrating its effectiveness as a general framework for generating diverse, structurally valid peptide binders without structural priors.
为实现无需结构预测的高效肽结合物设计，PepEDiff直接在预训练蛋白质嵌入模型导出的连续潜在空间中生成结合序列，采用扩散采样探索与已知序列无关的结合相关区域，实现零样本生成新肽。在缺乏可药口袋的挑战性靶点TIGIT上，该方法在多个基准测试中超越现有最先进方法，验证了其作为通用、无结构依赖框架在肽结合物发现中的潜力。
LiveSense: A Real-Time Wi-Fi Sensing Platform for Range-Doppler on COTS Laptop
Authors: Jessica Sanson, Rahul C. Shah, Maximilian Pinaroc, Cagri Tanriover, Valerio Frascolla
First: 2026-03-06T18:33:14+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-06T18:33:14+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present LiveSense - a cross-platform that transforms a commercial off-the-shelf (COTS) Wi-Fi Network Interface Card (NIC) on a laptop into a centimeter-level Range-Doppler sensor while preserving simultaneous communication capability. The laptops are equipped with COTS Intel AX211 (Wi-Fi 6E) or Intel BE201 (Wi-Fi 7) NICs. LiveSense can (i) Extract fully-synchronized channel state information (CSI) at >= 40 Hz, (ii) Perform time-phase alignment and self-interference cancellation on-device, and (iii) Provide a real-time stream of range, Doppler, subcarrier magnitude/phase and annotated video frames to a Python/Qt Graphical User Interface (GUI). The demo will showcase the ability to detect (i) Distance and radial velocity of attendees within a few meters of the device, (ii) Micro-motion (respiration), and (iii) Hand-gesture ranging. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first-ever demo to obtain accurate range information of targets from commercial Wi-Fi, despite the limited 160 MHz bandwidth.
中文标题/摘要
标题：LiveSense：基于商用笔记本电脑Wi-Fi的实时测距多普勒传感平台
我们提出LiveSense——一种跨平台系统，可将笔记本电脑上的商用现成（COTS）Wi-Fi网络接口卡（NIC）转化为厘米级测距多普勒传感器，同时保持通信功能。笔记本电脑配备COTS Intel AX211（Wi-Fi 6E）或Intel BE201（Wi-Fi 7）NIC。LiveSense可（i）以≥40 Hz频率提取完全同步的信道状态信息（CSI）；（ii）在设备端执行时相校准与自干扰抵消；（iii）向Python/Qt图形用户界面（GUI）提供实时测距、多普勒、子载波幅度/相位及标注视频帧流。演示将展示其检测能力：（i）数米内人员的距离与径向速度；（ii）微动（呼吸）；（iii）手势测距。据我们所知，这是首次利用商用Wi-Fi获取目标精确测距信息的演示，尽管其带宽仅限于160 MHz。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for low-cost, real-time sensing without sacrificing Wi-Fi connectivity, LiveSense repurposes common COTS Wi-Fi NICs in laptops to function as centimeter-accurate Range-Doppler sensors. It achieves this by extracting synchronized CSI at 40 Hz or higher, performing on-device time-phase alignment and self-interference cancellation, and streaming range, Doppler, subcarrier data, and annotated video to a GUI. Experimental results demonstrate accurate detection of human distance and radial velocity within meters, micro-motion like respiration, and hand-gesture ranging — all from standard Wi-Fi 6E/7 hardware, marking the first such demonstration using commercial Wi-Fi for precise range estimation despite its limited 160 MHz bandwidth.
为在不牺牲Wi-Fi通信能力的前提下实现低成本实时感知，LiveSense将普通笔记本电脑中的商用Wi-Fi网卡转化为厘米级精度的测距-多普勒传感器。其通过提取每秒40帧以上的同步信道状态信息（CSI），在设备端完成时相校准与自干扰抵消，并向图形界面实时推送测距、多普勒、子载波数据及标注视频。实验结果表明，该系统可准确检测数米内人体的距离与径向速度、呼吸等微动及手势测距，这是首次利用商用Wi-Fi（仅160MHz带宽）实现精确测距的演示。
Modeling and Measuring Redundancy in Multisource Multimodal Data for Autonomous Driving
Authors: Yuhan Zhou, Mehri Sattari, Haihua Chen, Kewei Sha
First: 2026-03-06T18:31:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-06T18:31:10+00:00
Comments: This paper has been accepted by the Fourth IEEE International Conference on Mobility: Operations, Services, and Technologies (MOST) 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Next-generation autonomous vehicles (AVs) rely on large volumes of multisource and multimodal ($M^2$) data to support real-time decision-making. In practice, data quality (DQ) varies across sources and modalities due to environmental conditions and sensor limitations, yet AV research has largely prioritized algorithm design over DQ analysis. This work focuses on redundancy as a fundamental but underexplored DQ issue in AV datasets. Using the nuScenes and Argoverse 2 (AV2) datasets, we model and measure redundancy in multisource camera data and multimodal image-LiDAR data, and evaluate how removing redundant labels affects the YOLOv8 object detection task. Experimental results show that selectively removing redundant multisource image object labels from cameras with shared fields of view improves detection. In nuScenes, mAP${50}$ gains from $0.66$ to $0.70$, $0.64$ to $0.67$, and from $0.53$ to $0.55$, on three representative overlap regions, while detection on other overlapping camera pairs remains at the baseline even under stronger pruning. In AV2, $4.1$-$8.6\%$ of labels are removed, and mAP${50}$ stays near the $0.64$ baseline. Multimodal analysis also reveals substantial redundancy between image and LiDAR data. These findings demonstrate that redundancy is a measurable and actionable DQ factor with direct implications for AV performance. This work highlights the role of redundancy as a data quality factor in AV perception and motivates a data-centric perspective for evaluating and improving AV datasets. Code, data, and implementation details are publicly available at: https://github.com/yhZHOU515/RedundancyAD

SurgFormer: Scalable Learning of Organ Deformation with Resection Support and Real-Time Inference
Authors: Ashkan Shahbazi, Elaheh Akbari, Kyvia Pereira, Jon S. Heiselman, Annie C. Benson, Garrison L. H. Johnston, Jie Ying Wu, Nabil Simaan, Michael I. Miga, Soheil Kolouri
First: 2026-03-06T18:29:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-06T18:29:47+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
We introduce SurgFormer, a multiresolution gated transformer for data driven soft tissue simulation on volumetric meshes. High fidelity biomechanical solvers are often too costly for interactive use, so we train SurgFormer on solver generated data to predict nodewise displacement fields at near real time rates. SurgFormer builds a fixed mesh hierarchy and applies repeated multibranch blocks that combine local message passing, coarse global self attention, and pointwise feedforward updates, fused by learned per node, per channel gates to adaptively integrate local and long range information while remaining scalable on large meshes. For cut conditioned simulation, resection information is encoded as a learned cut embedding and provided as an additional input, enabling a unified model for both standard deformation prediction and topology altering cases. We also introduce two surgical simulation datasets generated under a unified protocol with XFEM based supervision: a cholecystectomy resection dataset and an appendectomy manipulation and resection dataset with cut and uncut cases. To our knowledge, this is the first learned volumetric surrogate setting to study XFEM supervised cut conditioned deformation within the same volumetric pipeline as standard deformation prediction. Across diverse baselines, SurgFormer achieves strong accuracy with favorable efficiency, making it a practical backbone for both tasks. {Code, data, and project page: \href{https://mint-vu.github.io/SurgFormer/}{available here}}
