RAMoEA-QA: Hierarchical Specialization for Robust Respiratory Audio Question Answering
Authors: Gaia A. Bertolino, Yuwei Zhang, Tong Xia, Domenico Talia, Cecilia Mascolo
First: 2026-03-06T18:29:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-06T18:29:15+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Conversational generative AI is rapidly entering healthcare, where general-purpose models must integrate heterogeneous patient signals and support diverse interaction styles while producing clinically meaningful outputs. In respiratory care, non-invasive audio, such as recordings captured via mobile microphones, enables scalable screening and longitudinal monitoring, but the heterogeneity challenge is particularly acute: recordings vary widely across devices, environments, and acquisition protocols, and questions span multiple intents and question formats. Existing biomedical audio-language QA systems are typically monolithic, without any specialization mechanisms for tackling diverse respiratory corpora and query intents. They are also only validated in limited settings, leaving it unclear how reliably they handle the shifts encountered in real-world settings. To address these limitations, we introduce RAMoEA-QA, a hierarchically routed generative model for respiratory audio question answering that unifies multiple question types and supports both discrete and continuous targets within a single multimodal system. RAMoEA-QA applies two-stage conditional specialization: an Audio Mixture-of-Experts routes each recording to a suitable pre-trained audio encoder, and a Language Mixture-of-Adapters selects a LoRA adapter on a shared frozen LLM to match the query intent and answer format. By specializing both acoustic representations and generation behaviour per example, RAMoEA-QA consistently outperforms strong baselines and routing ablations with minimal parameter overhead, improving in-domain test accuracy to 0.72 (vs. 0.61 and 0.67 for state-of-the-art baselines) and exhibiting the strongest generalization for diagnosis under domain, modality, and task shifts.
中文标题/摘要
标题：RAMoEA-QA：面向鲁棒呼吸音频问答的分层专业化方法
对话式生成式AI正迅速进入医疗领域，其中通用模型需整合异构患者信号，支持多样交互风格，并生成具有临床意义的输出。在呼吸护理中，非侵入式音频（如通过移动麦克风录制的音频）可实现可扩展筛查与纵向监测，但异构性挑战尤为突出：录音在设备、环境和采集协议上差异显著，问题涵盖多种意图与格式。现有生物医学音频-语言问答系统通常为单一体系，缺乏针对多样呼吸语料和查询意图的专业化机制，且仅在有限场景下验证，难以明确其在真实场景中应对变化的可靠性。 为解决上述局限，我们提出RAMoEA-QA，一种面向呼吸音频问答的分层路由生成模型，统一处理多种问题类型，并在单一多模态系统中支持离散与连续目标。RAMoEA-QA采用两阶段条件专业化：音频Mixture-of-Experts将每段录音路由至合适的预训练音频编码器；语言Mixture-of-Adapters则在共享冻结的大语言模型上选择LoRA适配器，以匹配查询意图与答案格式。通过按样本专业化声学表示与生成行为，RAMoEA-QA在参数开销极小的情况下持续超越强基线与路由消融模型，将领域内测试准确率提升至0.72（优于现有最先进基线0.61与0.67），并在领域、模态与任务迁移下展现出最强的诊断泛化能力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for robust respiratory audio question answering in real-world clinical settings, this paper introduces RAMoEA-QA, a hierarchically routed generative model that handles diverse audio inputs and query intents. It employs a two-stage specialization: an audio MoE routes recordings to appropriate pre-trained encoders, while a language MoA selects a LoRA adapter on a frozen LLM to match query intent and answer format. Experiments show RAMoEA-QA significantly outperforms existing baselines, achieving 0.72 in-domain accuracy (vs. 0.61 and 0.67) and demonstrating superior generalization across domain, modality, and task shifts, all with minimal parameter overhead.
为应对临床环境中呼吸音频问答的多样性与鲁棒性需求，RAMoEA-QA提出一种分层路由生成模型，通过两阶段条件专业化处理：音频MoE将音频输入路由至合适编码器，语言MoA则在冻结大语言模型上选择LoRA适配器以匹配查询意图与答案格式。在多个领域、模态和任务偏移场景下，该模型表现稳定，准确率达0.72（优于现有SOTA基线0.61和0.67），且泛化能力最强。
Unified Learning of Temporal Task Structure and Action Timing for Bimanual Robot Manipulation
Authors: Christian Dreher, Patrick Dormanns, Andre Meixner, Tamim Asfour
First: 2026-03-06T18:25:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-06T18:25:42+00:00
Comments: This work has been submitted to the IEEE for possible publication
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Temporal task structure is fundamental for bimanual manipulation: a robot must not only know that one action precedes or overlaps another, but also when each action should occur and how long it should take. While symbolic temporal relations enable high-level reasoning about task structure and alternative execution sequences, concrete timing parameters are equally essential for coordinating two hands at the execution level. Existing approaches address these two levels in isolation, leaving a gap between high-level task planning and low-level movement synchronization. This work presents an approach for learning both symbolic and subsymbolic temporal task constraints from human demonstrations and deriving executable, temporally parametrized plans for bimanual manipulation. Our contributions are (i) a 3-dimensional representation of timings between two actions with methods based on multivariate Gaussian Mixture Models to represent temporal relationships between actions on a subsymbolic level, (ii) a method based on the Davis-Putnam-Logemann-Loveland (DPLL) algorithm that finds and ranks all contradiction-free assignments of Allen relations to action pairs, representing different modes of a task, and (iii) an optimization-based planning system that combines the identified symbolic and subsymbolic temporal task constraints to derive temporally parametrized plans for robot execution. We evaluate our approach on several datasets, demonstrating that our method generates temporally parametrized plans closer to human demonstrations than the most characteristic demonstration baseline.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于双手机器人操作的时序任务结构与动作时机的统一学习
时序任务结构对双手操作至关重要：机器人不仅需要知道一个动作先于或与另一个动作重叠，还需要明确每个动作何时发生以及持续多久。虽然符号时序关系支持对任务结构和替代执行序列的高层推理，但具体的时序参数同样对执行层面双手协调至关重要。现有方法分别处理这两个层面，导致高层任务规划与低层运动同步之间存在差距。本文提出一种从人类演示中学习符号与次符号时序任务约束的方法，并生成可执行的、时序参数化的双手操作计划。我们的贡献包括：(i) 一种基于多元高斯混合模型的三维动作间时序表示方法，用于在次符号层面表示动作间的时序关系；(ii) 一种基于Davis-Putnam-Logemann-Loveland (DPLL) 算法的方法，用于查找并排序所有无矛盾的Allen关系分配，代表任务的不同模式；(iii) 一种基于优化的规划系统，将识别出的符号与次符号时序约束结合，生成机器人可执行的时序参数化计划。我们在多个数据集上评估了该方法，结果表明，我们的方法生成的时序参数化计划比最典型的演示基线更接近人类演示。
Summary / 总结
This work addresses the gap between high-level symbolic task planning and low-level movement synchronization in bimanual robot manipulation by learning both symbolic and subsymbolic temporal constraints from human demonstrations. It introduces a 3D representation of action timings using multivariate Gaussian Mixture Models for subsymbolic timing, a DPLL-based method to enumerate and rank contradiction-free Allen relation assignments for symbolic task modes, and an optimization-based planner that integrates both representations to generate temporally parametrized executable plans. Experimental results show that the method produces plans more closely aligned with human demonstrations than the most representative demonstration baseline.
该研究旨在弥合双臂机器人操作中高层任务规划与底层动作同步之间的鸿沟，通过从人类演示中学习符号与子符号层面的时序约束。其方法包括使用多元高斯混合模型构建动作间时序的三维表示，采用Davis-Putnam-Logemann-Loveland算法枚举并排序所有无矛盾的Allen关系赋值以表征任务模式，并将二者整合进基于优化的规划器生成时序参数化的可执行计划。实验表明，该方法生成的计划比最典型演示基线更贴近人类演示。
Agri-Query: A Case Study on RAG vs. Long-Context LLMs for Cross-Lingual Technical Question Answering
Authors: Julius Gun, Timo Oksanen
Venue: Technical University of Munich. 2026. ISBN 978-3-911430-11-1. https://mediatum.ub.tum.de/1845092
First: 2025-08-25T14:54:46+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-06T18:23:23+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present a case study evaluating large language models (LLMs) with 128K-token context windows on a technical question answering (QA) task. Our benchmark is built on a user manual for an agricultural machine, available in English, French, and German. It simulates a cross-lingual information retrieval scenario where questions are posed in English against all three language versions of the manual. The evaluation focuses on realistic "needle-in-a-haystack" challenges and includes unanswerable questions to test for hallucinations. We compare nine long-context LLMs using direct prompting against three Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) strategies (keyword, semantic, hybrid), with an LLM-as-a-judge for evaluation. Our findings for this specific manual show that Hybrid RAG consistently outperforms direct long-context prompting. Models like Gemini 2.5 Flash and the smaller Qwen 2.5 7B achieve high accuracy (over 85%) across all languages with RAG. This paper contributes a detailed analysis of LLM performance in a specialized industrial domain and an open framework for similar evaluations, highlighting practical trade-offs and challenges.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Agri-Query：在跨语言技术问答任务中RAG与长上下文LLM的案例研究
我们提出了一项案例研究，评估具有128K标记上下文窗口的大语言模型（LLM）在技术问答任务中的表现。我们的基准基于一份农业机械用户手册，提供英文、法文和德文版本。该研究模拟了跨语言信息检索场景，即用英文提问并检索三语版本的手册。评估聚焦于现实中的“大海捞针”挑战，并包含无答案问题以检测幻觉。我们对比了九种长上下文LLM通过直接提示法，与三种检索增强生成（RAG）策略（关键词、语义、混合）的表现，采用LLM作为裁判进行评估。针对该特定手册，我们的研究发现混合RAG始终优于直接长上下文提示法。Gemini 2.5 Flash和较小的Qwen 2.5 7B模型在所有语言中使用RAG均达到高准确率（超过85%）。本文贡献了对LLM在特定工业领域表现的详细分析，以及一个开放框架用于类似评估，突出了实际权衡与挑战。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to evaluate large language models in cross-lingual technical QA, this study benchmarks nine long-context LLMs (up to 128K tokens) and three RAG strategies (keyword, semantic, hybrid) against a multilingual agricultural machine manual. Questions in English are matched against manuals in English, French, and German, testing real-world retrieval challenges including unanswerable queries to detect hallucinations. Results show that Hybrid RAG consistently outperforms direct prompting, with models like Gemini 2.5 Flash and Qwen 2.5 7B achieving over 85% accuracy across all languages when using RAG, demonstrating the effectiveness of retrieval-augmented approaches in specialized industrial domains.
为评估大语言模型在跨语言技术问答中的表现，本研究基于一份农业机械手册（含英、法、德三语版本），对比九个长上下文LLM与三种RAG策略（关键词、语义、混合）在真实稀疏信息场景下的表现，包括无答案问题以检测幻觉。结果表明，混合RAG策略始终优于直接提示法，其中Gemini 2.5 Flash和Qwen 2.5 7B等模型在三语环境下使用RAG均达到85%以上准确率，凸显了RAG在专业工业领域中的有效性。
NEGATE: Constrained Semantic Guidance for Linguistic Negation in Text-to-Video Diffusion
Authors: Taewon Kang, Ming C. Lin
First: 2026-03-06T18:21:49+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-06T18:21:49+00:00
Comments: 50 pages, 32 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Negation is a fundamental linguistic operator, yet it remains inadequately modeled in diffusion-based generative systems. In this work, we present a formal treatment of linguistic negation in diffusion-based generative models by modeling it as a structured feasibility constraint on semantic guidance within diffusion dynamics. Rather than introducing heuristics or retraining model parameters, we reinterpret classifier-free guidance as defining a semantic update direction and enforce negation by projecting the update onto a convex constraint set derived from linguistic structure. This novel formulation provides a unified framework for handling diverse negation phenomena, including object absence, graded non-inversion semantics, multi-negation composition, and scope-sensitive disambiguation. Our approach is training-free, compatible with pretrained diffusion backbones, and naturally extends from image generation to temporally evolving video trajectories. In addition, we introduce a structured negation-centric benchmark suite that isolates distinct linguistic failure modes in generative systems, to further research in this area. Experiments demonstrate that our method achieves robust negation compliance while preserving visual fidelity and structural coherence, establishing the first unified formulation of linguistic negation in diffusion-based generative models beyond representation-level evaluation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：NEGATE：基于语义约束的文本到视频扩散模型中的语言否定处理
否定是基本的语言运算符，但在基于扩散的生成系统中仍缺乏有效建模。本文通过将否定建模为扩散动态中语义引导的结构可行性约束，提出对扩散生成模型中语言否定的正式处理方法。我们不引入启发式方法或重新训练模型参数，而是将无分类器引导重新诠释为定义语义更新方向，并通过将更新投影到由语言结构导出的凸约束集来实现否定。该新颖公式提供了一个统一框架，用于处理多样化的否定现象，包括物体缺失、分级非反转语义、多重否定组合及范围敏感的歧义消解。我们的方法无需训练，兼容预训练扩散主干网络，并自然扩展至时序演变的视频轨迹生成。此外，我们引入了一个结构化的以否定为中心的基准测试套件，用于隔离生成系统中的不同语言失败模式，以促进该领域进一步研究。实验表明，我们的方法在保持视觉保真度和结构连贯性的同时，实现了稳健的否定合规性，建立了超越表征级评估的扩散生成模型中语言否定的首个统一公式。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inadequacy of diffusion models in handling linguistic negation, this work introduces NEGATE, a training-free method that formalizes negation as a structured feasibility constraint within semantic guidance. By reinterpreting classifier-free guidance as a semantic update direction and projecting it onto a convex set derived from linguistic structure, the method enables robust handling of diverse negation phenomena such as object absence, graded non-inversion, multi-negation, and scope-sensitive disambiguation. Experiments show that NEGATE achieves strong negation compliance without compromising visual fidelity or structural coherence, and it generalizes from images to video generation, establishing the first unified framework for linguistic negation in diffusion models.
为解决扩散模型在处理语言否定时的不足，本文提出NEGATE方法，将否定形式建模为语义引导中的结构化可行性约束。通过将无分类器引导重新解释为语义更新方向，并将其投影到由语言结构导出的凸约束集中，该方法可统一处理对象缺失、程度否定、多重否定及范围敏感歧义等复杂否定现象。实验表明，该方法在不损失视觉保真度和结构连贯性的前提下，显著提升否定表达的准确性，并可扩展至图像与视频生成，同时配套构建了专门评估语言否定失败模式的基准数据集。
Spatial Calibration of Diffuse LiDARs
Authors: Nikhil Behari, Ramesh Raskar
First: 2026-03-06T18:18:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-06T18:18:07+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Diffuse direct time-of-flight LiDARs report per-pixel depth histograms formed by aggregating photon returns over a wide instantaneous field of view, violating the single-ray assumption behind standard LiDAR-RGB calibration. We present a simple spatial calibration procedure that estimates, for each diffuse LiDAR pixel, its footprint (effective support region) and relative spatial sensitivity in a co-located RGB image plane. Using a scanned retroreflective patch with background subtraction, we recover per-pixel response maps that provide an explicit LiDAR-to-RGB correspondence for cross-modal alignment and fusion. We demonstrate the method on the ams OSRAM TMF8828.
中文标题/摘要
标题：漫反射激光雷达的空间校准
漫反射直接飞行时间激光雷达报告由宽瞬时视场内光子返回聚合形成的每像素深度直方图，违反了标准激光雷达-RGB校准背后的单射线假设。我们提出了一种简单空间校准程序，为每个漫反射激光雷达像素估计其足迹（有效支撑区域）和相对于共置RGB图像平面的空间灵敏度。通过扫描反射片并进行背景减除，我们恢复出每像素响应图，为跨模态对齐与融合提供明确的激光雷达-RGB对应关系。我们在ams OSRAM TMF8828上验证了该方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of calibrating diffuse direct time-of-flight LiDARs that violate the single-ray assumption of standard LiDAR-RGB calibration, this work introduces a spatial calibration method that estimates per-pixel footprint and relative sensitivity for each LiDAR pixel in alignment with an RGB image plane. The method uses a scanned retroreflective patch with background subtraction to generate per-pixel response maps, enabling explicit cross-modal correspondence. Experimental results on the ams OSRAM TMF8828 demonstrate successful recovery of spatial sensitivity maps and accurate alignment between LiDAR and RGB data.
为解决扩散型直接飞行时间激光雷达因宽瞬时视场导致的单射线假设失效问题，本文提出一种空间校准方法，通过扫描反光贴片并结合背景减除，估计每个激光雷达像素的有效覆盖区域和相对空间灵敏度，并生成像素级响应图以实现与RGB图像的显式对齐。在ams OSRAM TMF8828设备上的实验验证了该方法能有效重建空间对应关系并支持跨模态融合。
AV-Unified: A Unified Framework for Audio-visual Scene Understanding
Authors: Guangyao Li, Xin Wang, Wenwu Zhu
First: 2026-03-06T18:16:30+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-06T18:16:30+00:00
Comments: Accepted by IEEE Transactions on Multimedia (TMM)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
When humans perceive the world, they naturally integrate multiple audio-visual tasks within dynamic, real-world scenes. However, current works such as event localization, parsing, segmentation and question answering are mostly explored individually, making it challenging to comprehensively understand complex audio-visual scenes and explore inter-task relationships. Hence, we propose \textbf{AV-Unified}, a unified framework that enables joint learning across a wide range of audio-visual scene understanding tasks. AV-Unified standardizes the diverse input-output formats of each task and incorporates a multi-scale spatiotemporal perception network to effectively capture audio-visual associations. Specifically, we unify the inputs and outputs of all supported tasks by converting them into sequences of discrete tokens, establishing a shared representation that allows a single architecture to be trained jointly across heterogeneous varied datasets. Considering the varying temporal granularity of audio-visual events, a multi-scale temporal perception module is designed to capture key cues. Meanwhile, to overcome the lack of auditory supervision in the visual domain, we design a cross-modal guidance-based spatial perception module that models spatial audio-visual associations. Furthermore, task-specific text prompts are employed to enhance the model's adaptability and task-awareness. Extensive experiments on benchmark datasets (e.g., AVE, LLP, MUSIC-AVQA, VGG-SS and AVS) demonstrate the effectiveness of AV-Unified across temporal, spatial, and spatiotemporal tasks.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to holistically understand dynamic audio-visual scenes by integrating multiple tasks, AV-Unified proposes a unified framework that jointly learns diverse audio-visual scene understanding tasks through standardized tokenized inputs and outputs. It employs a multi-scale spatiotemporal perception network, including a temporal module for varying event granularities and a cross-modal spatial module to bridge auditory and visual cues, while using task-specific prompts to enhance adaptability. Experiments across benchmarks like AVE, LLP, and AVS show consistent performance gains over single-task models, demonstrating strong generalization across temporal, spatial, and spatiotemporal tasks.
为实现对动态视听场景的全面理解，AV-Unified 提出一个统一框架，通过标准化的离散化输入输出格式，联合训练多种视听场景理解任务。该框架采用多尺度时空感知网络，包含处理事件时序粒度差异的模块和建模视听空间关联的跨模态引导模块，并利用任务特定提示提升模型适应性。在AVE、LLP、AVS等多个基准数据集上的实验表明，该框架在时序、空间及时空任务上均优于单任务模型，展现出良好的泛化能力。
Underactuated multimodal jumping robot for extraterrestrial exploration
Authors: Neil R. Wagner, Justin K. Yim
Venue: ICRA 2026
First: 2026-03-06T18:08:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-06T18:08:32+00:00
Comments: 8 pages, 14 figures, Accepted for ICRA 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present a rolling and jumping underactuated monopedal robot designed to explore multimodal locomotion on low-gravity bodies. It uses only two reaction wheels to control its spatial orientation with two controllers: a balancing controller which can aim the robot's jump direction on the ground, and an aerial reorientation controller which can aim the robot's leg for landing after flight. We demonstrate rolling, targeted jumping and landing, and self-righting using only three actuators total, keeping system size to 0.33m and 1.25kg. Simple switching between locomotion modes enables the system to deal with differing landscapes and environmental conditions.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for lightweight, low-power mobility on low-gravity extraterrestrial surfaces, this paper introduces an underactuated monopedal robot that achieves multimodal locomotion using only two reaction wheels and three actuators. The robot employs two controllers: one for balancing and directing jump trajectories, and another for aerial leg reorientation to enable precise landing. Experimental results show the robot successfully performs rolling, targeted jumping, and self-righting maneuvers while maintaining a compact size of 0.33m and mass of 1.25kg, demonstrating robust adaptability across varied terrain and environmental conditions through simple mode switching.
为满足在低重力天体表面实现轻量化、低功耗移动的需求，本文提出一种仅用两个反作用轮和三个执行器的欠驱动单足机器人，实现多模态运动。机器人采用两个控制器——一个用于平衡并控制跳跃方向，另一个用于空中姿态调整以精准着陆——可完成滚动、定向跳跃与自翻正功能。实验结果表明，该机器人可在不同地形自主导航，且体积仅0.33米、重量仅1.25公斤。
CoME: Empowering Channel-of-Mobile-Experts with Informative Hybrid-Capabilities Reasoning
Authors: Yuxuan Liu, Weikai Xu, Kun Huang, Changyu Chen, Jiankun Zhao, Pengzhi Gao, Wei Liu, Jian Luan, Shuo Shang, Bo Du, Ji-Rong Wen, Rui Yan
First: 2026-02-27T16:19:45+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-06T18:07:28+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Mobile Agents can autonomously execute user instructions, which requires hybrid-capabilities reasoning, including screen summary, subtask planning, action decision and action function. However, existing agents struggle to achieve both decoupled enhancement and balanced integration of these capabilities. To address these challenges, we propose Channel-of-Mobile-Experts (CoME), a novel agent architecture consisting of four distinct experts, each aligned with a specific reasoning stage, CoME activates the corresponding expert to generate output tokens in each reasoning stage via output-oriented activation. To empower CoME with hybrid-capabilities reasoning, we introduce a progressive training strategy: Expert-FT enables decoupling and enhancement of different experts' capability; Router-FT aligns expert activation with the different reasoning stage; CoT-FT facilitates seamless collaboration and balanced optimization across multiple capabilities. To mitigate error propagation in hybrid-capabilities reasoning, we propose InfoGain-Driven DPO (Info-DPO), which uses information gain to evaluate the contribution of each intermediate step, thereby guiding CoME toward more informative reasoning. Comprehensive experiments show that CoME outperforms dense mobile agents and MoE methods on both AITZ and AMEX datasets.
中文标题/摘要
标题：CoME：赋能移动专家通道的具信息性混合能力推理
移动代理可自主执行用户指令，这需要混合能力推理，包括屏幕摘要、子任务规划、动作决策和动作函数。然而，现有代理难以同时实现这些能力的解耦增强与平衡集成。为应对这些挑战，我们提出通道式移动专家（CoME），一种由四个不同专家组成的新型代理架构，每个专家对应一个特定推理阶段。CoME在每个推理阶段通过输出导向激活激活相应专家生成输出令牌。为赋予CoME混合能力推理能力，我们引入渐进式训练策略：专家微调（Expert-FT）实现不同专家能力的解耦与增强；路由微调（Router-FT）使专家激活与不同推理阶段对齐；思维链微调（CoT-FT）促进多能力间无缝协作与平衡优化。为缓解混合能力推理中的错误传播，我们提出信息增益驱动的DPO（Info-DPO），利用信息增益评估每一步中间结果的贡献，引导CoME进行更具信息性的推理。综合实验表明，CoME在AITZ和AMEX数据集上均优于密集型移动代理和MoE方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for mobile agents to autonomously execute complex user tasks through hybrid-capabilities reasoning, this paper introduces CoME, a novel agent architecture comprising four specialized experts aligned with distinct reasoning stages. Each expert is activated output-orientedly to generate tokens at its designated stage, and the system employs a progressive training strategy—Expert-FT for capability decoupling, Router-FT for stage alignment, and CoT-FT for collaborative optimization—to enhance individual and joint performance. To reduce error propagation, InfoGain-Driven DPO evaluates intermediate steps by information gain, guiding more informative reasoning. Experiments on AITZ and AMEX datasets demonstrate that CoME surpasses both dense agents and MoE methods in overall performance.
为提升移动代理在复杂任务中自主执行能力，作者提出CoME架构，包含四个分别对应不同推理阶段的专家模块。CoME通过输出导向激活机制在各阶段调用对应专家，并采用渐进式训练策略：Expert-FT增强各专家能力，Router-FT对齐专家激活与推理阶段，CoT-FT促进多能力协同优化。为减少推理过程中的误差传播，引入基于信息增益的DPO方法指导更有效的推理路径。实验表明，CoME在AITZ与AMEX数据集上均优于密集型代理与MoE方法。
Artificial Intelligence for Detecting Fetal Orofacial Clefts and Advancing Medical Education
Authors: Yuanji Zhang, Yuhao Huang, Haoran Dou, Xiliang Zhu, Chen Ling, Zhong Yang, Lianying Liang, Jiuping Li, Siying Liang, Rui Li, Yan Cao, Yuhan Zhang, Jiewei Lai, Yongsong Zhou, Hongyu Zheng, Xinru Gao, Cheng Yu, Liling Shi, Mengqin Yuan, Honglong Li, Xiaoqiong Huang, Chaoyu Chen, Jialin Zhang, Wenxiong Pan, Alejandro F. Frangi, Guangzhi He, Xin Yang, Yi Xiong, Linliang Yin, Xuedong Deng, Dong Ni
First: 2026-03-06T18:06:20+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-06T18:06:20+00:00
Comments: 28 pages, 10 figures, 11 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Orofacial clefts are among the most common congenital craniofacial abnormalities, yet accurate prenatal detection remains challenging due to the scarcity of experienced specialists and the relative rarity of the condition. Early and reliable diagnosis is essential to enable timely clinical intervention and reduce associated morbidity. Here we show that an artificial intelligence system, trained on over 45,139 ultrasound images from 9,215 fetuses across 22 hospitals, can diagnose fetal orofacial clefts with sensitivity and specificity exceeding 93% and 95% respectively, matching the performance of senior radiologists and substantially outperforming junior radiologists. When used as a medical copilot, the system raises junior radiologists' sensitivity by more than 6%. Beyond direct diagnostic assistance, the system also accelerates the development of clinical expertise. A pilot study involving 24 radiologists and trainees demonstrated that the model can improve the expertise development for rare conditions. This dual-purpose approach offers a scalable solution for improving both diagnostic accuracy and specialist training in settings where experienced radiologists are scarce.
中文标题/摘要
标题：人工智能用于胎儿唇腭裂检测及医学教育推进
唇腭裂是最常见的先天性颅面畸形之一，但由于经验丰富的专科医生稀缺且该病发病率相对较低，准确的产前诊断仍具挑战性。早期可靠诊断对于及时临床干预、降低相关发病率至关重要。本研究显示，一个基于来自22家医院9,215名胎儿的45,139张超声图像训练的人工智能系统，可对胎儿唇腭裂诊断的敏感性和特异性分别超过93%和95%，与资深放射科医生表现相当，显著优于初级放射科医生。当作为医学协作者使用时，该系统可使初级放射科医生的敏感性提升6%以上。除直接诊断辅助外，该系统还能加速临床专业能力发展。一项涉及24名放射科医生及学员的试点研究表明，该模型可促进罕见病诊疗能力的提升。这种双重用途方法为在经验丰富的放射科医生稀缺地区，同时提升诊断准确性和专科培训提供了可扩展的解决方案。
Summary / 总结

为应对胎儿唇腭裂产前诊断难、专业人才稀缺的问题，该研究训练了一个AI模型，基于9215例胎儿的45139张超声图像，诊断准确率（敏感性与特异性）分别超过93%和95%，与资深放射科医生相当，显著优于初级医生。作为辅助工具，该系统使初级医生敏感性提升6%以上；在24名放射科医生和学员的试点中，也加速了对罕见病的临床能力培养，兼具诊断与培训双重价值。
An Adaptive Model Selection Framework for Demand Forecasting under Horizon-Induced Degradation to Support Business Strategy and Operations
Authors: Adolfo González, Víctor Parada
First: 2026-02-15T00:24:46+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-06T18:05:22+00:00
Comments: 35 pages, 24 figures and Appendix
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Business environments characterized by structural demand intermittency, high variability, and multi-step planning horizons require robust and reproducible model selection mechanisms. Empirical evidence shows that no forecasting model is universally dominant and that relative rankings vary across error metrics, demand regimes, and forecast horizons, generating ambiguity in multi-SKU decision contexts. This study proposes AHSIV (Adaptive Hybrid Selector for Intermittency and Variability), a horizon-aware and regime-conditioned model selection framework designed to address horizon-induced ranking instability. The proposed approach integrates scaled and absolute error metrics adjusted through a Metric Degradation by Forecast Horizon (MDFH) procedure, structural demand classification, multi-objective Pareto dominance, and hierarchical bias refinement within a unified decision architecture. The empirical evaluation is conducted on the Walmart, M3, M4, and M5 datasets under multiple train-test partition schemes and twelve-step forecasting horizons. Results indicate that AHSIV achieves statistical equivalence with the strongest monometric baseline in terms of aggregated performance while increasing the frequency of horizon-specific best-model selection. The findings demonstrate that model selection in heterogeneous demand environments cannot be treated as a static ranking problem, and that horizon-consistent, structurally adaptive mechanisms provide a principled, operationally coherent solution for multi-SKU forecasting.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向时程诱导退化需求预测的自适应模型选择框架，以支持商业战略与运营
在结构化需求间歇性、高变异性及多步规划时程的商业环境中，需要稳健且可复现的模型选择机制。实证研究表明，不存在普适主导的预测模型，不同误差指标、需求环境与预测时程下模型相对排名各异，导致多SKU决策场景中的不确定性。本研究提出AHSIV（面向间歇性与变异性自适应混合选择器），一种感知时程与环境条件的模型选择框架，旨在解决时程诱导的排名不稳定性。该方法整合了经“预测时程误差退化程序（MDFH）”调整的缩放与绝对误差指标、结构性需求分类、多目标帕累托支配及分层偏差优化，构建统一决策架构。实证评估基于Walmart、M3、M4、M5数据集，在多种训练-测试划分方案与十二步预测时程下进行。结果表明，AHSIV在综合性能上与最强单指标基线统计等效，同时显著提升时程特定最优模型选择频率。研究发现，在异质需求环境中，模型选择不能视为静态排名问题，而需采用时程一致、结构自适应的机制，以提供原则性且操作连贯的多SKU预测解决方案。
Summary / 总结
Business environments characterized by structural demand intermittency, high variability, and multi-step planning horizons require robust and reproducible model selection mechanisms.
SPoT: Subpixel Placement of Tokens in Vision Transformers
Authors: Martine Hjelkrem-Tan, Marius Aasan, Gabriel Y. Arteaga, Adín Ramírez Rivera
Venue: ICCV 2025
First: 2025-07-02T12:30:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-06T18:03:01+00:00
Comments: Appeared in Workshop on Efficient Computing under Limited Resources: Visual Computing (ICCV 2025). Code available at https://github.com/dsb-ifi/SPoT
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Vision Transformers naturally accommodate sparsity, yet standard tokenization methods confine features to discrete patch grids. This constraint prevents models from fully exploiting sparse regimes, forcing awkward compromises. We propose Subpixel Placement of Tokens (SPoT), a novel tokenization strategy that positions tokens continuously within images, effectively sidestepping grid-based limitations. With our proposed oracle-guided search, we uncover substantial performance gains achievable with ideal subpixel token positioning, drastically reducing the number of tokens necessary for accurate predictions during inference. SPoT provides a new direction for flexible, efficient, and interpretable ViT architectures, redefining sparsity as a strategic advantage rather than an imposed limitation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SPoT：视觉Transformer中令牌的亚像素定位
视觉Transformer天然支持稀疏性，但标准分词方法将特征限制在离散的图像块网格上。这一限制阻碍了模型充分利用稀疏区域，迫使模型做出不自然的妥协。我们提出亚像素令牌定位（SPoT），一种新颖的分词策略，使令牌在图像中连续定位，有效规避了基于网格的限制。通过我们提出的oracle引导搜索，我们发现理想亚像素令牌定位可带来显著性能提升，并大幅减少推理时准确预测所需的令牌数量。SPoT为灵活、高效且可解释的ViT架构提供了新方向，将稀疏性从限制转变为战略优势。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of fixed grid tokenization in Vision Transformers, which limits sparse feature utilization, the authors introduce SPoT, a method that places tokens continuously within images to bypass discrete patch constraints. Using an oracle-guided search, they demonstrate that optimal subpixel token positions significantly improve performance while drastically reducing token count during inference. Experimental results confirm SPoT’s effectiveness in enhancing model efficiency and accuracy without sacrificing interpretability, repositioning sparsity as a strategic design asset.
为了解决传统网格化token化方法限制视觉Transformer利用稀疏特征的问题，作者提出了SPoT方法，通过在亚像素位置连续放置token以突破网格约束。借助oracle引导搜索，实验表明最优亚像素token位置可显著提升模型性能并大幅减少推理所需token数量。实验结果显示，SPoT在准确率和效率上优于标准ViT方法，验证了其将稀疏性转化为设计优势的潜力。
The Limits of Long-Context Reasoning in Automated Bug Fixing
Authors: Ravi Raju, Mengmeng Ji, Shubhangi Upasani, Bo Li, Urmish Thakker
Venue: ICLR 2026
First: 2026-02-17T22:51:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-06T18:01:03+00:00
Comments: Accepted to ICLR 2026 ICBINB workshop
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Rapidly increasing context lengths have led to the assumption that large language models (LLMs) can directly reason over entire codebases. Concurrently, recent advances in LLMs have enabled strong performance on software engineering benchmarks, particularly when paired with agentic workflows. In this work, we systematically evaluate whether current LLMs can reliably perform long-context code debugging and patch generation. Using SWE-bench Verified as a controlled experimental setting, we first evaluate state-of-the-art models within an agentic harness (mini-SWE-agent), where performance improves substantially: GPT-5-nano achieves up to a 31\% resolve rate on 100 samples, and open-source models such as Deepseek-R1-0528 obtain competitive results. However, token-level analysis shows that successful agentic trajectories typically remain under 20k-30k tokens, and that longer accumulated contexts correlate with lower success rates, indicating that agentic success primarily arises from task decomposition into short-context steps rather than effective long-context reasoning. To directly test long-context capability, we construct a data pipeline where we artificially inflate the context length of the input by placing the relevant files into the context (ensuring perfect retrieval recall); we then study single-shot patch generation under genuinely long contexts (64k tokens). Despite this setup, performance degrades sharply: Qwen3-Coder-30B-A3B achieves only a 7\% resolve rate at 64k context, while GPT-5-nano solves none of the tasks. Qualitative analysis reveals systematic failure modes, including hallucinated diffs, incorrect file targets, and malformed patch headers. Overall, our findings highlight a significant gap between nominal context length and usable context capacity in current LLMs, and suggest that existing agentic coding benchmarks do not meaningfully evaluate long-context reasoning.
中文标题/摘要
标题：自动化漏洞修复中长上下文推理的局限性
上下文长度的快速增长导致人们假设大语言模型（LLMs）可直接对整个代码库进行推理。同时，LLMs的最新进展使其在软件工程基准测试中表现强劲，尤其在与代理工作流结合时。本研究系统评估当前LLMs是否能可靠执行长上下文代码调试与补丁生成。在SWE-bench Verified这一受控实验环境中，我们首先在代理框架（mini-SWE-agent）中评估最先进模型，性能显著提升：GPT-5-nano在100个样本上最高解决率达31%，开源模型如Deepseek-R1-0528亦表现优异。但词元级分析显示，成功代理轨迹通常少于2万至3万词元，且累积上下文越长，成功率越低，表明代理成功主要源于将任务分解为短上下文步骤，而非有效长上下文推理。为直接测试长上下文能力，我们构建数据管道，通过将相关文件置于上下文以人工扩充输入长度（确保完美检索召回），并研究在真实长上下文（64k词元）下单次补丁生成表现。尽管如此，性能急剧下降：Qwen3-Coder-30B-A3B在64k上下文下仅7%解决率，GPT-5-nano无一任务解决。定性分析揭示系统性失败模式，包括幻觉补丁、错误文件目标及格式错误补丁头。总体而言，我们的发现揭示当前LLMs名义上下文长度与可用上下文容量间存在显著差距，并表明现有代理编码基准未有效评估长上下文推理能力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the belief that increasing context lengths in large language models (LLMs) enables direct reasoning over entire codebases, this study evaluates whether LLMs can reliably debug and generate patches from long code contexts. Using an agentic framework (mini-SWE-agent) on SWE-bench Verified, models like GPT-5-nano and Deepseek-R1-0528 show strong performance, achieving up to 31% resolve rates on short-context tasks. However, token-level analysis reveals that success stems from task decomposition into short steps, not long-context reasoning. When forced to operate under genuinely long contexts (64k tokens) via artificial context inflation, performance collapses: Qwen3-Coder-30B-A3B achieves only 7% resolve rate, and GPT-5-nano solves none. Qualitative analysis identifies failure modes such as hallucinated diffs and incorrect file targets, indicating that current LLMs lack true long-context reasoning capacity, despite nominal support for large context windows.
鉴于大语言模型（LLM）上下文长度的快速增长，人们普遍认为其可直接处理整个代码库进行推理。本研究旨在检验当前LLM是否能可靠地从长上下文输入中完成代码调试与补丁生成。在SWE-bench Verified基准上，采用代理框架（mini-SWE-agent）时，GPT-5-nano等模型表现优异，解决率达31%。但细粒度分析显示，成功轨迹通常不超过2万至3万tokens，且上下文越长成功率越低，表明代理成功源于任务分解而非长上下文推理。在人工扩展上下文长度至64k的实验中，性能急剧下降：Qwen3-Coder-30B-A3B仅7%解决率，GPT-5-nano全数失败。定性分析发现模型常生成幻觉补丁、错误文件目标或格式错误的补丁头，揭示当前LLM的名义上下文长度与其实际可用容量存在显著差距。
Shoot First, Ask Questions Later? Building Rational Agents that Explore and Act Like People
Authors: Gabriel Grand, Valerio Pepe, Jacob Andreas, Joshua B. Tenenbaum
Venue: ICLR 2026
First: 2025-10-23T17:57:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-06T18:00:11+00:00
Comments: ICLR 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Many emerging applications of AI--from scientific discovery to medical diagnosis--require agents to seek information strategically: forming hypotheses, asking targeted questions, and making decisions under uncertainty. In high-stakes settings with limited resources, do language models (LMs) behave like rational agents? Drawing on insights from human cognition, we develop methods to evaluate and enhance agentic information-seeking. First, we introduce a decision-oriented dialogue task called Collaborative Battleship, in which a Captain must balance exploration (asking questions) and action (taking shots), while a Spotter must supply accurate, contextually-grounded answers. Compared to human players (N=42), we find that many LM agents struggle to ask informative questions, produce accurate answers, and identify high-utility actions. To address these gaps, we develop novel Monte Carlo inference strategies for LMs inspired by Bayesian Experimental Design (BED). For Spotter agents, our approach boosts accuracy by up to 14.7% absolute over LM-only baselines; for Captain agents, it raises expected information gain (EIG) by up to 0.227 bits (94.2% of the achievable noise ceiling). Combined, these components yield sharper targeting (+0.303-0.374 F1), and enable weaker LMs, such as Llama-4-Scout, to outperform both humans (8% -> 82% win rate) and frontier models (0% -> 67% win rate vs. GPT-5) at ~1% of GPT-5's cost. We replicate these findings on Guess Who?, where our methods significantly boost accuracy (+28.3-42.4 p.p.), demonstrating their general applicability for building information-seeking agents.
Summary / 总结
Many emerging applications of AI--from scientific discovery to medical diagnosis--require agents to seek information strategically: forming hypotheses, asking targeted questions, and making decisions under uncertainty.
Sysformer: Safeguarding Frozen Large Language Models with Adaptive System Prompts
Authors: Kartik Sharma, Yiqiao Jin, Vineeth Rakesh, Yingtong Dou, Menghai Pan, Mahashweta Das, Srijan Kumar
Venue: ICLR 2026
First: 2025-06-18T05:48:05+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-06T17:55:20+00:00
Comments: ICLR 2026. Code available at https://github.com/Ksartik/sysformer
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
As large language models (LLMs) are deployed in safety-critical settings, it is essential to ensure that their responses comply with safety standards. Prior research has revealed that LLMs often fail to grasp the notion of safe behaviors, resulting in either unjustified refusals to harmless prompts or the generation of harmful content. While substantial efforts have been made to improve their robustness, existing defenses often rely on costly fine-tuning of model parameters or employ suboptimal heuristic techniques. In this work, we take a novel approach to safeguard LLMs by learning to adapt the system prompts in instruction-tuned LLMs. While LLMs are typically pre-trained to follow a fixed system prompt, we investigate the impact of tailoring the system prompt to each specific user input on the safety of the responses. To this end, we propose $\textbf{Sysformer}$, a trans$\textbf{former}$ model that updates an initial $\textbf{sys}$tem prompt to a more robust system prompt in the LLM input embedding space while attending to the user prompt. While keeping the LLM parameters frozen, the Sysformer is trained to refuse to respond to a set of harmful prompts while responding ideally to a set of safe ones. Through extensive experiments on $5$ LLMs from different families and $2$ recent benchmarks, we demonstrate that Sysformer can significantly enhance the robustness of LLMs, leading to upto $80\%$ gain in the refusal rate on harmful prompts while enhancing the compliance with the safe prompts by upto $90\%$. Results also generalize well to sophisticated jailbreaking attacks, making LLMs upto $100\%$ more robust against different attack strategies. We hope our findings lead to cheaper safeguarding of LLMs and motivate future investigations into designing variable system prompts.
Summary / 总结
As large language models (LLMs) are deployed in safety-critical settings, it is essential to ensure that their responses comply with safety standards.
为在不进行昂贵参数微调的前提下保障大语言模型在安全关键场景下的响应安全，本文提出Sysformer，一种在冻结模型参数的前提下通过自适应调整系统提示词来增强安全性的Transformer模型。Sysformer在输入嵌入空间中根据用户提示动态优化系统提示，以拒绝有害输入并响应安全输入。实验表明，Sysformer在五种不同模型和两个基准测试中，有害提示拒绝率提升最高达80%，安全提示合规率提升最高达90%，且对复杂越狱攻击具有强泛化能力，防御效果最高提升100%。
SG-DOR: Learning Scene Graphs with Direction-Conditioned Occlusion Reasoning for Pepper Plants
Authors: Rohit Menon, Niklas Mueller-Goldingen, Sicong Pan, Gokul Krishna Chenchani, Maren Bennewitz
First: 2026-03-06T17:52:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-06T17:52:51+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Robotic harvesting in dense crop canopies requires effective interventions that depend not only on geometry, but also on explicit, direction-conditioned relations identifying which organs obstruct a target fruit. We present SG-DOR (Scene Graphs with Direction-Conditioned Occlusion Reasoning), a relational framework that, given instance-segmented organ point clouds, infers a scene graph encoding physical attachments and direction-conditioned occlusion. We introduce an occlusion ranking task for retrieving and ranking candidate leaves for a target fruit and approach direction, and propose a direction-aware graph neural architecture with per-fruit leaf-set attention and union-level aggregation. Experiments on a multi-plant synthetic pepper dataset show improved occlusion prediction (F1=0.73, NDCG@3=0.85) and attachment inference (edge F1=0.83) over strong ablations, yielding a structured relational signal for downstream intervention planning.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SG-DOR：面向胡椒植物的基于方向条件遮挡推理的场景图学习
在密集作物冠层中进行机器人采收需要有效的干预措施，这不仅依赖于几何结构，还依赖于明确的方向条件关系，以识别哪些器官遮挡目标果实。我们提出了SG-DOR（带方向条件遮挡推理的场景图），一种关系框架，给定器官实例分割的点云，推断出编码物理连接和方向条件遮挡的场景图。我们引入了遮挡排序任务，用于检索并排序目标果实和方向的候选叶片，并提出了一种方向感知的图神经架构，包含每果实叶片集注意力和联合层级聚合。在多株合成胡椒数据集上的实验表明，相比强消融模型，遮挡预测（F1=0.73，NDCG@3=0.85）和连接推断（边F1=0.83）均有所提升，为下游干预规划提供了结构化的关系信号。
Summary / 总结
To enable robotic harvesting in dense pepper canopies, this work addresses the need to model occlusion and physical attachments using direction-conditioned relations. SG-DOR introduces a relational framework that processes instance-segmented organ point clouds to infer scene graphs encoding both physical connections and occlusion based on viewing direction. It employs an occlusion ranking task to identify and rank leaves obstructing target fruits, and uses a direction-aware graph neural network with per-fruit leaf-set attention and union-level aggregation. Experiments on a synthetic multi-plant pepper dataset demonstrate improved occlusion prediction (F1=0.73, NDCG@3=0.85) and attachment inference (edge F1=0.83) compared to ablated models, providing a structured relational representation for robotic intervention planning.
为实现密集辣椒植株中的机器人采摘，本研究旨在通过方向条件化的遮挡关系建模几何与遮挡信息。SG-DOR框架输入实例分割的器官点云，推断包含物理连接与方向条件遮挡关系的场景图。其引入遮挡排序任务以识别并排序遮挡目标果实的叶片，并采用方向感知图神经网络，结合每果实叶片集注意力与全局聚合机制。在合成多植株辣椒数据集上的实验表明，该方法在遮挡预测（F1=0.73，NDCG@3=0.85）和连接推理（边F1=0.83）上优于消融模型，为下游机器人干预规划提供结构化关系信号。
CMRAG: Co-modality-based visual document retrieval and question answering
Authors: Wang Chen, Wenhan Yu, Guanqiang Qi, Weikang Li, Yang Li, Lei Sha, Deguo Xia, Jizhou Huang
Venue: ICLR 2026
First: 2025-09-02T09:17:57+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-06T17:51:55+00:00
Comments: Published at ICLR 2026 Workshop on Multimodal Intelligence
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) has become a core paradigm in document question answering tasks. However, existing methods have limitations when dealing with multimodal documents: one category of methods relies on layout analysis and text extraction, which can only utilize explicit text information and struggle to capture images or unstructured content; the other category treats document segmentation as visual input and directly passes it to visual language models (VLMs) for processing, yet it ignores the semantic advantages of text, leading to suboptimal retrieval and generation results. To address these research gaps, we propose the Co-Modality-based RAG (CMRAG) framework, which can simultaneously leverage texts and images for more accurate retrieval and generation. Our framework includes two key components: (1) a Unified Encoding Model (UEM) that projects queries, parsed text, and images into a shared embedding space via triplet-based training, and (2) a Unified Co-Modality-informed Retrieval (UCMR) method that statistically normalizes similarity scores to effectively fuse cross-modal signals. To support research in this direction, we further construct and release a large-scale triplet dataset of (query, text, image) examples. Experiments demonstrate that our proposed framework consistently outperforms single-modality--based RAG in multiple visual document question-answering (VDQA) benchmarks. The findings of this paper show that integrating co-modality information into the RAG framework in a unified manner is an effective approach to improving the performance of complex VDQA systems.
Summary / 总结
Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) has become a core paradigm in document question answering tasks.
为解决现有RAG方法在处理多模态文档时仅依赖文本或忽略视觉语义的局限，作者提出CMRAG框架，通过联合利用文本与图像提升检索与生成效果。该方法包含统一编码模型，通过三元组训练将查询、文本与图像映射至共享空间，以及统一跨模态信息检索模块，通过统计归一化融合跨模态相似度。在多个视觉文档问答基准测试中，CMRAG持续优于单模态RAG基线，验证了统一融合跨模态信息对提升复杂视觉文档问答性能的有效性。
Culture in Action: Evaluating Text-to-Image Models through Social Activities
Authors: Sina Malakouti, Boqing Gong, Adriana Kovashka
First: 2025-11-07T19:51:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-06T17:45:17+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Text-to-image (T2I) diffusion models achieve impressive photorealism by training on large-scale web data, but models inherit cultural biases and fail to depict underrepresented regions faithfully. Existing cultural benchmarks focus mainly on object-centric categories (e.g., food, attire, and architecture), overlooking the social and daily activities that more clearly reflect cultural norms. Few metrics exist for measuring cultural faithfulness. We introduce CULTIVate, a benchmark for evaluating T2I models on cross-cultural activities (e.g., greetings, dining, games, traditional dances, and cultural celebrations). CULTIVate spans 16 countries with 576 prompts and more than 19,000 images, and provides an explainable descriptor-based evaluation framework across multiple cultural dimensions, including background, attire, objects, and interactions. We propose four metrics to measure cultural alignment, hallucination, exaggerated elements, and diversity. Our findings reveal systematic disparities: models perform better for global north countries than for the global south, with distinct failure modes across T2I systems. Human studies confirm that our metrics correlate more strongly with human judgments than existing text-image metrics.
中文标题/摘要
标题：文化在行动：通过社会活动评估文本到图像模型
文本到图像（T2I）扩散模型通过在大规模网络数据上训练实现了令人印象深刻的逼真效果，但模型继承了文化偏见，难以真实描绘代表性不足的地区。现有文化基准主要关注以物体为中心的类别（如食物、服饰和建筑），忽视了更能反映文化规范的社会和日常活动。目前缺乏衡量文化忠实度的指标。我们提出了CULTIVate，一个用于评估T2I模型在跨文化活动（如问候、用餐、游戏、传统舞蹈和文化庆典）表现的基准。CULTIVate涵盖16个国家，包含576个提示和超过19,000张图像，并提供一个基于可解释描述符的多文化维度评估框架，包括背景、服饰、物品和互动。我们提出了四个指标，用于衡量文化契合度、幻觉、夸张元素和多样性。研究发现存在系统性差异：模型在“全球北方”国家的表现优于“全球南方”国家，且不同T2I系统存在不同的失败模式。人类研究证实，我们的指标与人类判断的相关性高于现有文本-图像指标。
Summary / 总结
Text-to-image (T2I) diffusion models achieve impressive photorealism by training on large-scale web data, but models inherit cultural biases and fail to depict underrepresented regions faithfully.
Whole-Body Model-Predictive Control of Legged Robots with MuJoCo
Authors: John Z. Zhang, Taylor A. Howell, Zeji Yi, Chaoyi Pan, Guanya Shi, Guannan Qu, Tom Erez, Yuval Tassa, Zachary Manchester
Venue: ICRA 2026
First: 2025-03-06T16:59:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-06T17:45:16+00:00
Comments: to appear at ICRA 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
We demonstrate the surprising real-world effectiveness of a very simple approach to whole-body model-predictive control (MPC) of quadruped and humanoid robots: the iterative LQR (iLQR) algorithm with MuJoCo dynamics and finite-difference approximated derivatives. Building upon the previous success of model-based behavior synthesis and control of locomotion and manipulation tasks with MuJoCo in simulation, we show that these policies can easily generalize to the real world with few sim-to-real considerations. Our baseline method achieves real-time whole-body MPC on a variety of hardware experiments, including dynamic quadruped locomotion, quadruped walking on two legs, and full-sized humanoid bipedal locomotion. We hope this easy-to-reproduce hardware baseline lowers the barrier to entry for real-world whole-body MPC research and contributes to accelerating research velocity in the community. Our code and experiment videos will be available online at:https://johnzhang3.github.io/mujoco_ilqr
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于MuJoCo的腿式机器人全身模型预测控制
我们展示了针对四足和人形机器人的全身模型预测控制（MPC）的一种非常简单方法在现实世界中的惊人有效性：使用MuJoCo动力学和有限差分近似导数的迭代LQR（iLQR）算法。在仿真中，我们基于先前在MuJoCo中通过模型驱动行为合成与控制实现的步态和操作任务的成功经验，表明这些策略只需少量仿真到现实的调整即可轻松泛化到真实世界。我们的基线方法在多种硬件实验中实现了实时全身MPC，包括动态四足运动、四足两足行走以及全尺寸人形双足运动。我们希望这一易于复现的硬件基线能降低现实世界全身MPC研究的入门门槛，并加速社区的研究进展。我们的代码和实验视频将发布于：https://johnzhang3.github.io/mujoco_ilqr
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for practical, real-time whole-body control of legged robots, this work introduces a simple iterative LQR (iLQR) algorithm using MuJoCo dynamics and finite-difference derivatives to achieve model-predictive control. The method leverages prior success in simulation-based behavior synthesis and demonstrates strong generalization to real hardware with minimal sim-to-real adaptation. Experimental results show the approach successfully enables real-time whole-body MPC on diverse platforms including dynamic quadruped locomotion, bipedal walking, and full-sized humanoid robots, establishing a reproducible baseline for future research.
为推动腿式机器人实用化的全身模型预测控制（MPC）研究，本文提出一种基于迭代LQR（iLQR）算法、结合MuJoCo动力学与有限差分导数的简单有效方法。该方法继承了仿真中模型驱动行为合成的成功经验，并在真实硬件上表现出良好的泛化能力，仅需极少的仿真到现实调整。实验结果表明，该方法能在多种任务（如动态四足运动、双足行走及全尺寸人形机器人运动）中实现实时控制，为后续研究提供可复现的基准，降低研究门槛。
How Well Does Agent Development Reflect Real-World Work?
Authors: Zora Zhiruo Wang, Sanidhya Vijayvargiya, Aspen Chen, Hanmo Zhang, Venu Arvind Arangarajan, Jett Chen, Valerie Chen, Diyi Yang, Daniel Fried, Graham Neubig
First: 2026-03-01T17:55:49+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-06T17:43:36+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
AI agents are increasingly developed and evaluated on benchmarks relevant to human work, yet it remains unclear how representative these benchmarking efforts are of the labor market as a whole. In this work, we systematically study the relationship between agent development efforts and the distribution of real-world human work by mapping benchmark instances to work domains and skills. We first analyze 43 benchmarks and 72,342 tasks, measuring their alignment with human employment and capital allocation across all 1,016 real-world occupations in the U.S. labor market. We reveal substantial mismatches between agent development that tends to be programming-centric, and the categories in which human labor and economic value are concentrated. Within work areas that agents currently target, we further characterize current agent utility by measuring their autonomy levels, providing practical guidance for agent interaction strategies across work scenarios. Building on these findings, we propose three measurable principles for designing benchmarks that better capture socially important and technically challenging forms of work: coverage, realism, and granular evaluation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：代理开发如何反映现实世界的工作？
AI代理越来越多地在与人类工作相关的基准上进行开发和评估，但这些基准是否能代表整体劳动力市场仍不明确。本研究系统性地研究了代理开发努力与现实世界人类工作的分布之间的关系，通过将基准实例映射到工作领域和技能。我们首先分析了43个基准和72,342个任务，测量其与美国1,016个现实职业中人类就业和资本分配的契合度。我们发现，以编程为中心的代理开发与人类劳动和经济价值集中领域存在显著不匹配。在代理当前所针对的工作领域内，我们进一步通过测量其自主性水平来刻画当前代理的效用，为不同工作场景下的代理交互策略提供实用指导。基于这些发现，我们提出了三个可衡量的原则，用于设计更能捕捉社会重要且技术挑战性工作的基准：覆盖性、现实性与细粒度评估。
Summary / 总结
AI agents are increasingly developed and evaluated on benchmarks relevant to human work, yet it remains unclear how representative these benchmarking efforts are of the labor market as a whole.
该研究旨在评估AI代理开发基准是否真实反映现实劳动力市场，因当前评估多集中于编程领域，与人类劳动实际分布存在显著偏差。作者将43个基准和72,342项任务映射至1,016个美国职业，发现代理开发与人类劳动集中领域严重脱节。同时评估了代理在目标工作领域中的自主性，并提出三个设计原则——覆盖度、现实性与细粒度评估，以提升基准对社会重要且技术复杂的人类工作的捕捉能力。
When One Modality Rules Them All: Backdoor Modality Collapse in Multimodal Diffusion Models
Authors: Qitong Wang, Haoran Dai, Haotian Zhang, Christopher Rasmussen, Binghui Wang
Venue: ICLR 2026
First: 2026-03-06T17:42:08+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-06T17:42:08+00:00
Comments: Accepted to the ICLR 2026 Workshop on Principled Design for Trustworthy AI. The first two authors contributed equally
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
While diffusion models have revolutionized visual content generation, their rapid adoption has underscored the critical need to investigate vulnerabilities, e.g., to backdoor attacks. In multimodal diffusion models, it is natural to expect that attacking multiple modalities simultaneously (e.g., text and image) would yield complementary effects and strengthen the overall backdoor. In this paper, we challenge this assumption by investigating the phenomenon of Backdoor Modality Collapse, a scenario where the backdoor mechanism degenerates to rely predominantly on a subset of modalities, rendering others redundant. To rigorously quantify this behavior, we introduce two novel metrics: Trigger Modality Attribution (TMA) and Cross-Trigger Interaction (CTI). Through extensive experiments across diverse training configurations in multimodal conditional diffusion, we consistently observe a ``winner-takes-all'' dynamic in backdoor behavior. Our results reveal that (1) attacks often collapse into subset-modality dominance, and (2) cross-modal interaction is negligible or even negative, contradicting the intuition of synergistic vulnerability. These findings highlight a critical blind spot in current assessments, suggesting that high attack success rates often mask a fundamental reliance on a subset of modalities. This establishes a principled foundation for mechanistic analysis and future defense development.
中文标题/摘要
标题：当单一模态主宰一切：多模态扩散模型中的后门模态崩溃
尽管扩散模型已革新视觉内容生成，其快速普及凸显了研究其脆弱性（如后门攻击）的迫切需求。在多模态扩散模型中，人们自然预期同时攻击多个模态（如文本与图像）会产生互补效应并增强整体后门效果。本文通过研究“后门模态崩溃”现象挑战这一假设，该现象指后门机制退化为主要依赖部分模态，使其他模态变得冗余。为严谨量化此行为，我们提出两项新指标：触发模态归因（TMA）与跨触发交互（CTI）。通过在多模态条件扩散模型中广泛实验，我们始终观察到后门行为呈现“赢家通吃”动态。结果表明：（1）攻击常退化为子模态主导；（2）跨模态交互微弱甚至为负，与协同脆弱性直觉相悖。这些发现揭示当前评估中的关键盲点，即高攻击成功率常掩盖对部分模态的根本依赖。这为机制分析与未来防御开发奠定了原则性基础。
Summary / 总结
While diffusion models have revolutionized visual content generation, their rapid adoption has underscored the critical need to investigate vulnerabilities, e.g., to backdoor attacks.
为理解多模态扩散模型中的脆弱性，本研究探讨了同时攻击多个模态（如文本与图像）是否能增强整体攻击效果。作者提出了两个新指标——触发模态归因与跨触发交互，以量化模态特异性的后门行为。在多种训练配置下的实验结果一致表明，后门攻击会坍缩为依赖少数模态，跨模态协同效应微弱甚至为负，与预期的协同脆弱性相悖。这一发现揭示了关键盲区：高攻击成功率往往掩盖了对特定模态的依赖，亟需基于机制理解的新防御策略。
Self-Supervised Flow Matching for Scalable Multi-Modal Synthesis
Authors: Hila Chefer, Patrick Esser, Dominik Lorenz, Dustin Podell, Vikash Raja, Vinh Tong, Antonio Torralba, Robin Rombach
First: 2026-03-06T17:41:49+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-06T17:41:49+00:00
Comments: project webpage: https://bfl.ai/research/self-flow
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Strong semantic representations improve the convergence and generation quality of diffusion and flow models. Existing approaches largely rely on external models, which require separate training, operate on misaligned objectives, and exhibit unexpected scaling behavior. We argue that this dependence arises from the model's training objective, which poses a denoising task with little incentive to learn semantic representations. We introduce Self-Flow: a self-supervised flow matching paradigm that integrates representation learning within the generative framework. Our key mechanism, Dual-Timestep Scheduling, applies heterogeneous noise levels across tokens, creating an information asymmetry that forces the model to infer missing information from corrupted inputs. This drives learning strong representations alongside generative capabilities without external supervision. Our method generalizes across modalities and enables multi-modal training while following expected scaling laws, achieving superior image, video, and audio generation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：自监督流匹配用于可扩展多模态合成
强语义表示可提升扩散和流模型的收敛性和生成质量。现有方法大多依赖外部模型，需单独训练、目标不一致且存在意外的扩展行为。我们认为，这种依赖源于模型训练目标——以去噪任务为主，缺乏学习语义表示的激励。我们提出Self-Flow：一种自监督流匹配范式，将表征学习整合进生成框架。核心机制“双时间步调度”在不同token上应用异质噪声水平，制造信息不对称，迫使模型从损坏输入中推断缺失信息，从而在无需外部监督下同时学习强表征与生成能力。该方法可跨模态泛化，支持多模态训练并遵循预期扩展规律，实现图像、视频和音频生成的优越表现。
Summary / 总结
To address the limitation that existing generative models rely on external semantic representations trained separately, which leads to misaligned objectives and unexpected scaling, the authors propose Self-Flow, a self-supervised flow matching method that embeds representation learning directly into the generative process. By introducing Dual-Timestep Scheduling, which applies varying noise levels across tokens to create information asymmetry, the model is compelled to infer missing content from corrupted inputs, thereby learning robust semantic representations without external supervision. Experiments show that Self-Flow generalizes across image, video, and audio modalities, enables multi-modal training, and follows expected scaling laws, outperforming baselines in generation quality and convergence.
为解决现有扩散与流模型依赖外部语义模型导致的训练效率低、目标不一致和扩展性差的问题，作者提出Self-Flow方法，将表征学习直接嵌入生成框架。通过Dual-Timestep Scheduling机制，在不同token上施加异质噪声水平，迫使模型从损坏输入中推断缺失信息，从而在无需外部监督的情况下学习强语义表征。实验表明，该方法在图像、视频和音频生成任务中表现更优，且支持多模态训练并遵循预期的扩展规律。
Semantics-Aware Caching for Concept Learning
Authors: Louis Mozart Kamdem Teyou, Caglar Demir, Axel-Cyrille Ngonga Ngomo
First: 2026-03-06T17:40:13+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-06T17:40:13+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Concept learning is a form of supervised machine learning that operates on knowledge bases in description logics. State-of-the-art concept learners often rely on an iterative search through a countably infinite concept space. In each iteration, they retrieve instances of candidate solutions to select the best concept for the next iteration. While simple learning problems might require a few dozen instance retrieval calls to find a fitting solution, complex learning problems might necessitate thousands of calls. We alleviate the resulting runtime challenge by presenting a semantics-aware caching approach. Our cache is essentially a subsumption-aware map that links concepts to a set of instances via crisp set operations. Our experiments on 5 datasets with 4 symbolic reasoners, a neuro-symbolic reasoner, and 5 popular pagination policies demonstrate that our cache can reduce the runtime of concept retrieval and concept learning by an order of magnitude while being effective for both symbolic and neuro-symbolic reasoners.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向概念学习的语义感知缓存
概念学习是一种在描述逻辑知识库上运行的监督式机器学习形式。当前最先进的概念学习器通常依赖于在可数无限的概念空间中进行迭代搜索。在每次迭代中，它们检索候选解的实例以选择下一个迭代的最佳概念。对于简单学习问题，可能只需几十次实例检索调用即可找到合适解；而复杂问题则可能需要数千次调用。我们通过提出一种语义感知缓存方法来缓解由此产生的运行时挑战。该缓存本质上是一个子句感知映射，通过明确集合运算将概念与一组实例关联起来。我们在5个数据集上，结合4种符号推理器、1种神经符号推理器和5种流行分页策略的实验表明，该缓存可将概念检索和概念学习的运行时间减少一个数量级，同时对符号和神经符号推理器均有效。
Summary / 总结
Concept learning is a form of supervised machine learning that operates on knowledge bases in description logics.
为缓解描述逻辑中概念学习迭代过程的高计算开销，本文提出一种语义感知缓存方法，利用子句关系高效存储与检索概念实例，通过精确集合操作建立概念到实例的映射，减少冗余检索。在五个数据集上，结合四种符号推理器、一个神经符号推理器及五种分页策略的实验表明，该方法可将概念检索与学习的运行时间降低一个数量级，且对符号与神经符号系统均保持高效性能。
Spectral/Spatial Tensor Atomic Cluster Expansion with Universal Embeddings in Cartesian Space
Authors: Zemin Xu, Wenbo Xie, P. Hu
First: 2025-09-18T13:51:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-06T17:38:33+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Equivariant atomistic machine learning models have largely been built on spherical-tensor representations, where explicit angular-momentum coupling introduces substantial complexity and systematic extensions beyond energies and forces remain challenging, often requires problem-specific architectural choices. Here we introduce the Tensor Atomic Cluster Expansion (TACE), which unifies scalar and tensorial modeling in Cartesian and space by decomposing local environments into irreducible Cartesian tensors (ICT) constructing a controlled many-body hierarchy with atomic cluster expansion (ACE). In addition to performing ACE in the frequency domain, we propose an efficient Clebsch-Gordan-free alternative in the spatial domain. TACE provides universal invariant (e.g., fidelity tags and charges) and equivariant (e.g., external electric fields and non-collinear magnetic moments) embeddings and predicted tensorial observables are handled on equal footing and enabling explicit control at inference. We demonstrate the accuracy, stability, and efficiency across finite molecules and extended materials, including in-domain and out-of-domain benchmarks, spectra, Hessian, external-field responses, charged systems, and multi-fidelity/head training. We further show its robustness on nonequilibrium/reactive datasets and controlled scaling when extending to large foundation model datasets.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于笛卡尔空间通用嵌入的谱/空间张量原子簇展开
等变原子机器学习模型大多基于球张量表示，其中显式角动量耦合引入了大量复杂性，且超出能量和力的系统性扩展仍具挑战，常需问题特定的架构选择。本文提出张量原子簇展开（TACE），通过将局部环境分解为不可约笛卡尔张量（ICT），统一了标量与张量建模，并结合原子簇展开（ACE）构建可控的多体层次结构。除在频域执行ACE外，我们提出一种无需Clebsch-Gordan系数的空间域高效替代方案。TACE提供通用不变量（如保真度标签和电荷）与等变量（如外电场和非共线磁矩）嵌入，并对预测张量可观测量一视同仁，实现推理时的显式控制。我们在有限分子与扩展材料中验证了其精度、稳定性和效率，涵盖域内/域外基准、光谱、Hessian矩阵、外场响应、带电系统及多保真度/多头训练。进一步展示其在非平衡/反应性数据集上的鲁棒性，以及扩展至大型基础模型数据集时的可控扩展性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the complexity of spherical-tensor representations in equivariant atomistic machine learning, this work introduces Tensor Atomic Cluster Expansion (TACE), which unifies scalar and tensorial modeling via Cartesian irreducible tensors and atomic cluster expansion. TACE constructs a controlled many-body hierarchy and offers a Clebsch-Gordan-free spatial-domain method for efficient computation. Experimental results show TACE achieves high accuracy, stability, and efficiency across diverse benchmarks including finite molecules, extended materials, spectra, Hessian calculations, external-field responses, charged systems, and multi-fidelity training, while demonstrating robustness in reactive and nonequilibrium scenarios and scalable performance on large datasets.
为解决球张量表示在等变原子机器学习中的复杂性问题，本文提出张量原子簇展开（TACE），通过笛卡尔不可约张量与原子簇展开统一标量与张量建模，并提供无需Clebsch-Gordan系数的空域高效计算方法。实验表明，TACE在分子、材料、光谱、Hessian、外场响应、带电系统、多保真度训练等多类基准测试中表现高精度、高稳定性和高效率，且在非平衡/反应性数据集上具备鲁棒性，可扩展至大规模基础模型数据集。
Localizing and Correcting Errors for LLM-based Planners
Authors: Aditya Kumar, William W. Cohen
First: 2026-01-30T19:56:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-06T17:37:41+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated strong reasoning capabilities on math and coding, but frequently fail on symbolic classical planning tasks. Our studies, as well as prior work, show that LLM-generated plans routinely violate domain constraints given in their instructions (e.g., walking through walls). To address this failure, we propose iteratively augmenting instructions with Localized In-Context Learning (L-ICL) demonstrations: targeted corrections for specific failing steps. Specifically, L-ICL identifies the first constraint violation in a trace and injects a minimal input-output example giving the correct behavior for the failing step. Our proposed technique of L-ICL is much effective than explicit instructions or traditional ICL, which adds complete problem-solving trajectories, and many other baselines. For example, on an 8x8 gridworld, L-ICL produces valid plans 89% of the time with only 60 training examples, compared to 59% for the best baseline, an increase of 30%. L-ICL also shows dramatic improvements in other domains (gridworld navigation, mazes, Sokoban, and BlocksWorld), and on several LLM architectures.
Summary / 总结
Large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated strong reasoning capabilities on math and coding, but frequently fail on symbolic classical planning tasks.
为了解决大语言模型在符号经典规划任务中因违反领域约束而频繁失败的问题，本文提出了一种名为局部上下文学习（L-ICL）的方法，该方法通过在规划轨迹首次出现错误时注入最小化、针对性的示范来迭代修正错误。L-ICL相比显式指令和传统ICL方法表现更优，因其仅针对具体错误步骤进行修正而非完整轨迹。实验表明，在8x8网格世界中，仅用60个训练样本，L-ICL即可实现89%的有效规划生成，比最佳基线方法高出30%，并在多个领域和大语言模型架构上均取得显著提升。
Better Late Than Never: Meta-Evaluation of Latency Metrics for Simultaneous Speech-to-Text Translation
Authors: Peter Polák, Sara Papi, Luisa Bentivogli, Ondřej Bojar
First: 2025-09-22T04:21:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-06T17:37:14+00:00
Comments: Changes: - small change in the name (Evaluation -> Meta-Evaluation); - added reference to the implementation; - excluded two test sets (IWSLT22 En-Zh, En-Ja) because of incorrect and missing segmentation; - main results unchanged; - added Degenerate Policy Test; - added sensitivity of the metrics to change in the metric value
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Simultaneous speech-to-text translation systems must balance translation quality with latency. Although quality evaluation is well established, latency measurement remains a challenge. Existing metrics produce inconsistent results, especially in short-form settings with artificial presegmentation. We present the first comprehensive meta-evaluation of latency metrics across language pairs and systems. We uncover a structural bias in current metrics related to segmentation. We introduce YAAL (Yet Another Average Lagging) for a more accurate short-form evaluation and LongYAAL for unsegmented audio. We propose SoftSegmenter, a resegmentation tool based on soft word-level alignment. We show that YAAL and LongYAAL, together with SoftSegmenter, outperform popular latency metrics, enabling more reliable assessments of short- and long-form simultaneous speech translation systems. We implement all artifacts within the OmniSTEval toolkit: https://github.com/pe-trik/OmniSTEval.

标题：迟到总比不到好：同时语音转文字翻译延迟度量的元评估

Speak in Context: Multilingual ASR with Speech Context Alignment via Contrastive Learning
Authors: Yuchen Zhang, Haralambos Mouratidis, Ravi Shekhar
First: 2026-03-06T17:37:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-06T17:37:06+00:00
Comments: Accepted at LREC 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Automatic speech recognition (ASR) has benefited from advances in pretrained speech and language models, yet most systems remain constrained to monolingual settings and short, isolated utterances. While recent efforts in context-aware ASR show promise, two key challenges persist: limited multilingual support and the absence of principled alignment between speech and contextual representations. In this paper, we introduce a context-aware multilingual ASR framework that supports diverse languages and accents while preserving the modularity of pretrained models. Our approach combines a frozen speech encoder and a decoder-only language model via a lightweight projection module, allowing structured context prompts, including dialogue history and biasing words, to guide transcription. To improve interaction between speech and context, we employ a contrastive learning objective that aligns their representations in a shared embedding space. Evaluations on over 1,500 hours of real-world conversational speech across 11 languages and 5 English dialects show that contextual input consistently improves recognition quality. Contrastive alignment provides additional gains when applied to different context types, with an overall performance gain of over 5%. These results highlight the importance of both contextual modeling and cross-modal alignment in multilingual ASR.

标题：在上下文中说话：通过对比学习实现语音上下文对齐的多语言自动语音识别
自动语音识别（ASR）得益于预训练语音和语言模型的发展，但大多数系统仍局限于单语言和短句孤立语音。尽管近期上下文感知ASR研究展现出前景，但两个关键挑战依然存在：多语言支持有限，以及语音与上下文表示之间缺乏原则性对齐。本文提出一种上下文感知的多语言ASR框架，支持多种语言和口音，同时保留预训练模型的模块化特性。我们的方法通过轻量级投影模块将冻结的语音编码器与解码器语言模型结合，允许结构化上下文提示（如对话历史和引导词）引导转录。为提升语音与上下文的交互，我们采用对比学习目标，在共享嵌入空间中对齐其表示。在11种语言和5种英语方言、超过1500小时真实对话语音上的评估表明，上下文输入持续提升识别质量。对比对齐在不同上下文类型中提供额外增益，整体性能提升超过5%。这些结果凸显了多语言ASR中上下文建模与跨模态对齐的重要性。
