Beyond Rows to Reasoning: Agentic Retrieval for Multimodal Spreadsheet Understanding and Editing
Authors: Anmol Gulati, Sahil Sen, Waqar Sarguroh, Kevin Paul
First: 2026-03-06T17:36:39+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-06T17:36:39+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recent advances in multimodal Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) enable Large Language Models (LLMs) to analyze enterprise spreadsheet workbooks containing millions of cells, cross-sheet dependencies, and embedded visual artifacts. However, state-of-the-art approaches exclude critical context through single-pass retrieval, lose data resolution through compression, and exceed LLM context windows through naive full-context injection, preventing reliable multi-step reasoning over complex enterprise workbooks. We introduce Beyond Rows to Reasoning (BRTR), a multimodal agentic framework for spreadsheet understanding that replaces single-pass retrieval with an iterative tool-calling loop, supporting end-to-end Excel workflows from complex analysis to structured editing. Supported by over 200 hours of expert human evaluation, BRTR achieves state-of-the-art performance across three frontier spreadsheet understanding benchmarks, surpassing prior methods by 25 percentage points on FRTR-Bench, 7 points on SpreadsheetLLM, and 32 points on FINCH. We evaluate five multimodal embedding models, identifying NVIDIA NeMo Retriever 1B as the top performer for mixed tabular and visual data, and vary nine LLMs. Ablation experiments confirm that the planner, retrieval, and iterative reasoning each contribute substantially, and cost analysis shows GPT-5.2 achieves the best efficiency-accuracy trade-off. Throughout all evaluations, BRTR maintains full auditability through explicit tool-call traces.
中文标题/摘要
标题：超越行列走向推理：面向多模态电子表格理解与编辑的代理式检索
近期多模态检索增强生成（RAG）的进展使大语言模型（LLM）能够分析包含数百万单元格、跨表依赖关系和嵌入式视觉元素的企业电子表格工作簿。然而，现有最先进方法通过单次检索丢失关键上下文，通过压缩丢失数据分辨率，通过简单全量上下文注入超出LLM上下文窗口，从而阻碍了对复杂企业工作簿进行可靠多步推理。我们提出了超越行列走向推理（BRTR），一种面向电子表格理解的多模态代理框架，用迭代式工具调用循环替代单次检索，支持从复杂分析到结构化编辑的端到端Excel工作流。基于超过200小时专家人工评估，BRTR在三个前沿电子表格理解基准测试中均取得最先进性能，在FRTR-Bench上超越前序方法25个百分点，在SpreadsheetLLM上提升7分，在FINCH上提升32分。我们评估了五种多模态嵌入模型，发现NVIDIA NeMo Retriever 1B在混合表格与视觉数据上表现最佳，并测试了九种LLM。消融实验表明规划器、检索和迭代推理各自贡献显著，成本分析显示GPT-5.2在效率-精度权衡上表现最优。在所有评估中，BRTR通过明确的工具调用轨迹保持全程可审计性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of current multimodal RAG systems in handling complex enterprise spreadsheets—such as missing context, data compression, and exceeding LLM context windows—BRTR introduces an agentic framework that iteratively calls tools to enable multi-step reasoning. It replaces single-pass retrieval with a planning-retrieval-reasoning loop, supporting end-to-end Excel workflows. Evaluated across three benchmarks with over 200 hours of human feedback, BRTR outperforms prior methods by 25 percentage points on FRTR-Bench, 7 points on SpreadsheetLLM, and 32 points on FINCH. The study also identifies NVIDIA NeMo Retriever 1B as optimal for mixed tabular-visual data and finds GPT-5.2 offers the best efficiency-accuracy balance, while ablation confirms each component (planner, retrieval, reasoning) is critical.
为解决现有多模态RAG系统在处理复杂企业电子表格时存在的上下文缺失、数据压缩和超出LLM上下文窗口等问题，BRTR提出了一种代理框架，通过规划-检索-推理的迭代工具调用循环支持多步推理，实现从复杂分析到结构化编辑的端到端Excel工作流。在三个基准测试中，经200多小时人工评估，BRTR在FRTR-Bench上超越前序方法25分，在SpreadsheetLLM上提升7分，在FINCH上提升32分。研究还发现NVIDIA NeMo Retriever 1B最适合混合表格与视觉数据，GPT-5.2在效率与精度间表现最优，消融实验验证了各模块的关键作用。
CFEAR-Teach-and-Repeat: Fast and Accurate Radar-only Localization
Authors: Maximilian Hilger, Daniel Adolfsson, Ralf Becker, Henrik Andreasson, Achim J. Lilienthal
Venue: ICRA
First: 2026-03-06T17:35:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-06T17:35:58+00:00
Comments: This paper has been accepted for publication in the IEEE International Conference on Robotics and Automation (ICRA), 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Reliable localization in prior maps is essential for autonomous navigation, particularly under adverse weather, where optical sensors may fail. We present CFEAR-TR, a teach-and-repeat localization pipeline using a single spinning radar, which is designed for easily deployable, lightweight, and robust navigation in adverse conditions. Our method localizes by jointly aligning live scans to both stored scans from the teach mapping pass, and to a sliding window of recent live keyframes. This ensures accurate and robust pose estimation across different seasons and weather phenomena. Radar scans are represented using a sparse set of oriented surface points, computed from Doppler-compensated measurements. The map is stored in a pose graph that is traversed during localization. Experiments on the held-out test sequences from the Boreas dataset show that CFEAR-TR can localize with an accuracy as low as 0.117 m and 0.096°, corresponding to improvements of up to 63% over the previous state of the art, while running efficiently at 29 Hz. These results substantially narrow the gap to lidar-level localization, particularly in heading estimation. We make the C++ implementation of our work available to the community.
中文标题/摘要
标题：CFEAR-Teach-and-Repeat：快速准确的纯雷达定位
在先前地图中实现可靠的定位对自主导航至关重要，特别是在恶劣天气下，光学传感器可能失效。我们提出了CFEAR-TR，一种使用单个旋转雷达的“教与重复”定位管道，专为易于部署、轻量级且在恶劣条件下稳健导航而设计。我们的方法通过联合对齐实时扫描与教习映射阶段存储的扫描，以及最近实时关键帧的滑动窗口，实现定位。这确保了在不同季节和天气现象下准确且稳健的姿态估计。雷达扫描通过从多普勒补偿测量中计算出的稀疏定向表面点集表示。地图以姿态图形式存储，并在定位过程中遍历。在Boreas数据集的保留测试序列上的实验表明，CFEAR-TR可实现低至0.117米和0.096度的定位精度，相比先前最先进方法提升高达63%，同时以29Hz高效运行。这些结果显著缩小了与激光雷达级定位的差距，特别是在航向估计方面。我们向社区公开了本工作的C++实现。
Summary / 总结
To enable reliable autonomous navigation under adverse weather where optical sensors fail, CFEAR-TR leverages a single spinning radar to achieve fast and accurate localization via a teach-and-repeat pipeline. It jointly aligns live radar scans with stored teach scans and a sliding window of recent live keyframes, using sparse oriented surface points derived from Doppler-compensated measurements and a pose graph for map representation. Evaluated on Boreas test sequences, it achieves sub-0.12m translation and sub-0.1° rotation accuracy at 29 Hz, outperforming prior methods by up to 63% and closing the performance gap with lidar-based systems, especially in heading estimation.
为在恶劣天气下实现可靠的自主导航（光学传感器失效时），CFEAR-TR 利用单个旋转雷达，通过“教与重复”方法实现快速精准定位。其方法联合对齐实时雷达扫描与存储的教习扫描及最近的实时关键帧，使用经多普勒补偿计算的稀疏定向表面点和位姿图表示地图。在 Boreas 数据集测试序列上，其定位精度达 0.117 米和 0.096 度，较前序方法提升最高 63%，运行频率达 29Hz，显著缩小与激光雷达定位的性能差距，尤其在航向估计方面表现突出。
A Unified Low-Dimensional Design Embedding for Joint Optimization of Shape, Material, and Actuation in Soft Robots
Authors: Vittorio Candiello, Manuel Mekkattu, Mike Y. Michelis, Robert K. Katzschmann
First: 2026-03-06T17:33:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-06T17:33:32+00:00
Comments: This work has been submitted to the IEEE for possible publication
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Soft robots achieve functionality through tight coupling among geometry, material composition, and actuation. As a result, effective design optimization requires these three aspects to be considered jointly rather than in isolation. This coupling is computationally challenging: nonlinear large-deformation mechanics increase simulation cost, while contact, collision handling, and non-smooth state transitions limit the applicability of standard gradient-based approaches. We introduce a smooth, low-dimensional design embedding for soft robots that unifies shape morphing, multi-material distribution, and actuation within a single structured parameter space. Shape variation is modeled through continuous deformation maps of a reference geometry, while material properties are encoded as spatial fields. Both are constructed from shared basis functions. This representation enables expressive co-design while drastically reducing the dimensionality of the search space. In our experiments, we show that design expressiveness increases with the number of basis functions, unlike comparable neural network encodings whose representational capacity does not scale predictably with parameter count. We further show that joint co-optimization of shape, material, and actuation using our unified embedding consistently outperforms sequential strategies. All experiments are performed independently of the underlying simulator, confirming compatibility with black-box simulation pipelines. Across multiple dynamic tasks, the proposed embedding surpasses neural network and voxel-based baseline parameterizations while using significantly fewer design parameters. Together, these findings demonstrate that structuring the design space itself enables efficient co-design of soft robots.
中文标题/摘要
标题：软体机器人形貌、材料与驱动联合优化的统一低维设计嵌入
软体机器人通过几何、材料组成与驱动之间的紧密耦合实现功能，因此有效的设计优化需联合考虑三者而非孤立处理。这种耦合在计算上具有挑战性：非线性大变形力学增加仿真成本，接触、碰撞处理及非光滑状态转换限制了标准梯度方法的应用。本文提出一种平滑的低维设计嵌入，将软体机器人的形貌变形、多材料分布与驱动统一于单一结构化参数空间。形貌变化通过参考几何的连续变形映射建模，材料属性编码为空间场，二者均基于共享基函数构建。该表示方法在保持高表达能力的同时大幅降低搜索空间维度。实验表明，设计表达能力随基函数数量增加而提升，与参数数量无明确可预测扩展性的神经网络编码不同。进一步验证显示，使用该统一嵌入对形貌、材料与驱动进行联合协同优化，性能优于顺序策略。所有实验独立于底层仿真器，证实与黑盒仿真流程兼容。在多个动态任务中，所提嵌入超越神经网络与体素基线参数化方法，且设计参数显著更少。这些结果共同表明，对设计空间本身的结构化可实现软体机器人的高效协同设计。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to jointly optimize shape, material, and actuation in soft robots—whose functionality relies on tight coupling among these factors—the authors propose a smooth, low-dimensional design embedding that unifies all three aspects within a shared parameter space. Shape is modeled via continuous deformation maps of a reference geometry, while material properties are encoded as spatial fields, both constructed from shared basis functions. This structured representation reduces search space dimensionality while enabling expressive co-design. Experimental results show that design expressiveness scales predictably with the number of basis functions, unlike neural network baselines. Joint co-optimization using this embedding consistently outperforms sequential optimization strategies across dynamic tasks, achieving superior performance with far fewer parameters than voxel-based or neural network parameterizations, and working independently of the underlying simulator.
为解决软机器人中形状、材料与驱动三者紧密耦合导致的联合优化难题，作者提出一种平滑的低维设计嵌入方法，将三者统一于单一结构化参数空间。形状通过参考几何体的连续变形映射建模，材料属性以空间场形式编码，二者共享基函数构造。该方法显著降低搜索空间维度同时保持设计表达能力。实验表明，设计表达能力随基函数数量可预测增长，优于神经网络基线；联合优化策略在多个动态任务中持续优于分步优化方法，且参数量远少于体素或神经网络基线，兼容黑盒仿真流程。
CanvasMAR: Improving Masked Autoregressive Video Prediction With Canvas
Authors: Zian Li, Muhan Zhang
First: 2025-10-15T15:29:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-06T17:33:15+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Masked autoregressive models (MAR) have emerged as a powerful paradigm for image and video generation, combining the flexibility of masked modeling with the expressiveness of continuous tokenizers. However, when sampling individual frames, video MAR models often produce highly distorted outputs due to the lack of a structured global prior, especially when using only a few sampling steps. To address this, we propose CanvasMAR, a novel autoregressive video prediction model that predicts high-fidelity frames with few sampling steps by introducing a canvas--a blurred, global one-step prediction of the next frame that serves as a non-uniform mask during masked generation. The canvas supplies global structure early in sampling, enabling faster and more coherent frame synthesis. To further stabilize autoregressive sampling, we propose an easy-to-hard curriculum via a motion-aware sampling order that synthesizes relatively stationary regions before attending to highly dynamic ones. We also integrate compositional classifier-free guidance that jointly strengthens the canvas and temporal conditioning to improve generation fidelity. Experiments on the BAIR, UCF-101, and Kinetics-600 benchmarks demonstrate that CanvasMAR produces higher-quality videos with fewer autoregressive steps. On the challenging Kinetics-600 dataset, CanvasMAR achieves remarkable performance among autoregressive models and rivals advanced diffusion-based methods.
中文标题/摘要
标题：CanvasMAR：通过画布提升掩码自回归视频预测
掩码自回归模型（MAR）已成为图像和视频生成的强大范式，结合了掩码建模的灵活性与连续标记器的表达能力。然而，在采样单帧时，视频MAR模型常因缺乏结构化的全局先验而产生高度失真的输出，尤其在仅使用少量采样步骤时。为解决此问题，我们提出CanvasMAR，一种新颖的自回归视频预测模型，通过引入“画布”——即下一帧的模糊全局一步预测，作为掩码生成过程中的非均匀掩码，以少量采样步骤预测高保真帧。画布在采样早期提供全局结构，从而实现更快、更连贯的帧合成。为进一步稳定自回归采样，我们提出一种基于运动感知采样顺序的由易到难课程学习策略，优先合成相对静止区域，再处理高度动态区域。此外，我们整合了组合式无分类器引导机制，协同增强画布与时间条件，以提升生成保真度。在BAIR、UCF-101和Kinetics-600基准测试中，CanvasMAR以更少的自回归步骤生成更高质量的视频。在具有挑战性的Kinetics-600数据集上，CanvasMAR在自回归模型中表现卓越，可媲美先进的扩散模型方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the distortion in video generation from masked autoregressive models due to missing global structure, CanvasMAR introduces a canvas—a blurred global prediction of the next frame—that acts as a non-uniform mask to guide early-stage frame synthesis, enabling high-fidelity outputs with fewer sampling steps. It further stabilizes generation through a motion-aware curriculum that prioritizes stationary regions before dynamic ones, and integrates compositional classifier-free guidance to jointly enhance the canvas and temporal conditioning. Experimental results on BAIR, UCF-101, and Kinetics-600 show CanvasMAR generates higher-quality videos with fewer steps, outperforming other autoregressive models and rivaling diffusion-based methods on Kinetics-600.
为解决掩码自回归模型在视频生成中因缺乏全局结构导致的帧失真问题，CanvasMAR引入了一个模糊的全局下一帧预测（canvas），作为非均匀掩码引导早期帧合成，实现少步采样下的高保真输出。同时，通过运动感知的课程学习优先生成静态区域，结合组合式无分类器引导增强canvas与时间条件，提升生成质量。在BAIR、UCF-101和Kinetics-600数据集上的实验表明，CanvasMAR在更少采样步数下生成更高质量视频，其在Kinetics-600上表现优于其他自回归模型，可媲美先进的扩散模型。
COLD-Steer: Steering Large Language Models via In-Context One-step Learning Dynamics
Authors: Kartik Sharma, Rakshit S. Trivedi
Venue: ICLR 2026
First: 2026-03-06T17:27:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-06T17:27:27+00:00
Comments: ICLR 2026. Code available at https://github.com/Ksartik/cold-steer
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Activation steering methods enable inference-time control of large language model (LLM) behavior without retraining, but current approaches face a fundamental trade-off: sample-efficient methods suboptimally capture steering signals from labeled examples, while methods that better extract these signals require hundreds to thousands of examples. We introduce COLD-Steer, a training-free framework that steers LLM activations by approximating the representational changes that would result from gradient descent on in-context examples. Our key insight is that the effect of fine-tuning on a small set of examples can be efficiently approximated at inference time without actual parameter updates. We formalize this through two complementary approaches: (i) a unit kernel approximation method that updates the activations directly using gradients with respect to them, normalized across examples, and (ii) a finite-difference approximation requiring only two forward passes regardless of example count. Experiments across a variety of steering tasks and benchmarks demonstrate that COLD-Steer achieves upto 95% steering effectiveness while using 50 times fewer samples compared to the best baseline. COLD-Steer facilitates accommodating diverse perspectives without extensive demonstration data, which we validate through our experiments on pluralistic alignment tasks. Our framework opens new possibilities for adaptive, context-aware model control that can flexibly address varying loss-driven human preferences through principled approximation of learning dynamics rather than specialized training procedures.
中文标题/摘要
标题：COLD-Steer：通过上下文一步学习动态引导大语言模型
激活引导方法可在不重新训练的情况下实现推理时对大语言模型（LLM）行为的控制，但现有方法面临根本性权衡：样本效率高的方法难以充分捕捉标注示例中的引导信号，而能更好提取这些信号的方法则需数百至数千个示例。我们提出COLD-Steer，一种无需训练的框架，通过近似梯度下降在上下文示例上引起的表征变化来引导LLM激活。我们的核心见解是：对少量示例进行微调的效果可在推理时无需实际参数更新的情况下高效近似。我们通过两种互补方法形式化该思想：(i) 单位核近似方法，直接使用归一化后的激活梯度更新激活；(ii) 有限差分近似方法，无论示例数量多少仅需两次前向传播。在多种引导任务和基准测试中，实验表明COLD-Steer在样本量仅为最佳基线的1/50的情况下，实现高达95%的引导效果。COLD-Steer有助于在无需大量演示数据的情况下容纳多元观点，我们在多元对齐任务实验中验证了这一点。该框架为适应性、上下文感知的模型控制开辟了新可能，通过原理性近似学习动态而非专用训练流程，灵活应对不同损失驱动的人类偏好。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to steer large language models without retraining while overcoming the sample-efficiency trade-off of existing methods, COLD-Steer introduces a training-free framework that approximates the activation changes induced by gradient descent on in-context examples. It employs two complementary approximation techniques: a unit kernel method that updates activations using normalized gradients, and a finite-difference method requiring only two forward passes regardless of example count. Experimental results across multiple steering tasks show COLD-Steer achieves up to 95% steering effectiveness using 50x fewer samples than the best baseline, enabling effective pluralistic alignment without extensive demonstration data.
为解决现有激活引导方法在样本效率与信号提取效果之间的权衡，COLD-Steer提出一种无需训练的框架，通过近似梯度下降在上下文示例上引起的激活变化来引导模型行为。该框架包含两种互补方法：一种基于单位核的激活直接更新方法，另一种仅需两次前向传播的有限差分近似方法。实验表明，该方法在样本量仅为最优基线1/50的情况下，仍可实现高达95%的引导效果，有效支持多元视角对齐，无需大量演示数据。
CAPS: Context-Aware Priority Sampling for Enhanced Imitation Learning in Autonomous Driving
Authors: Hamidreza Mirkhani, Behzad Khamidehi, Ehsan Ahmadi, Mohammed Elmahgiubi, Weize Zhang, Fazel Arasteh, Umar Rajguru, Kasra Rezaee, Dongfeng Bai
Venue: ICRA 2026
First: 2025-03-03T15:27:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-06T17:27:21+00:00
Comments: Accepted at IEEE International Conference on Robotics & Automation (ICRA 2026)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In this paper, we introduce Context-Aware Priority Sampling (CAPS), a novel method designed to enhance data efficiency in learning-based autonomous driving systems. CAPS addresses the challenge of imbalanced datasets in imitation learning by leveraging Vector Quantized Variational Autoencoders (VQ-VAEs). In this way, we can get structured and interpretable data representations, which help to reveal meaningful patterns in the data. These patterns are used to group the data into clusters, with each sample being assigned a cluster ID. The cluster IDs are then used to re-balance the dataset, ensuring that rare yet valuable samples receive higher priority during training. We evaluate our method through closed-loop experiments in the CARLA simulator. The results on Bench2Drive scenarios demonstrate the effectiveness of CAPS in enhancing model generalization, with substantial improvements in both driving score and success rate.
中文标题/摘要
标题：CAPS：面向自动驾驶模仿学习的上下文感知优先采样
本文提出上下文感知优先采样（CAPS），一种旨在提升基于学习的自动驾驶系统数据效率的新方法。CAPS 通过利用向量量化变分自编码器（VQ-VAE），解决模仿学习中数据集不平衡的问题，获得结构化且可解释的数据表示，揭示数据中的有意义模式。这些模式用于将数据聚类，每个样本分配一个聚类ID，再利用聚类ID重新平衡数据集，确保稀有但有价值的样本在训练中获得更高优先级。我们在CARLA模拟器中通过闭环实验评估该方法，在Bench2Drive场景中结果表明，CAPS显著提升了模型泛化能力，同时提高了驾驶评分与成功率。

Motivated by the inefficiency of imitation learning in autonomous driving due to imbalanced datasets, this paper proposes CAPS, a context-aware priority sampling method that uses Vector Quantized Variational Autoencoders to generate interpretable data representations. These representations enable clustering of driving samples, allowing the system to assign higher training priority to rare but critical samples. Evaluated in CARLA simulations across Bench2Drive scenarios, CAPS significantly improves model generalization, achieving higher driving scores and success rates compared to baseline methods without prioritization.
为解决自动驾驶领域模仿学习中数据不平衡的问题，CAPS提出一种基于上下文感知的优先采样方法，利用向量量化变分自编码器生成结构化、可解释的数据表示，从而将样本聚类并按簇ID重新加权，优先采样稀有但有价值的数据。在CARLA仿真平台的Bench2Drive场景中，该方法显著提升模型泛化能力，驾驶评分与成功率均优于基线方法。
Control Barrier Corridors: From Safety Functions to Safe Sets
Authors: Ömür Arslan, Nikolay Atanasov
First: 2026-03-06T17:23:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-06T17:23:56+00:00
Comments: 12 pages, 6 figures, an extended preprint version of a conference paper
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Safe autonomy is a critical requirement and a key enabler for robots to operate safely in unstructured complex environments. Control barrier functions and safe motion corridors are two widely used but technically distinct safety methods, functional and geometric, respectively, for safe motion planning and control. Control barrier functions are applied to the safety filtering of control inputs to limit the decay rate of system safety, whereas safe motion corridors are geometrically constructed to define a local safe zone around the system state for use in motion optimization and reference-governor design. This paper introduces a new notion of control barrier corridors, which unifies these two approaches by converting control barrier functions into local safe goal regions for reference goal selection in feedback control systems. We show, with examples on fully actuated systems, kinematic unicycles, and linear output regulation systems, that individual state safety can be extended locally over control barrier corridors for convex barrier functions, provided the control convergence rate matches the barrier decay rate, highlighting a trade-off between safety and reactiveness. Such safe control barrier corridors enable safely reachable persistent goal selection over continuously changing barrier corridors during system motion, which we demonstrate for verifiably safe and persistent path following in autonomous exploration of unknown environments.
中文标题/摘要
标题：控制屏障走廊：从安全功能到安全集
安全自主性是机器人在非结构化复杂环境中安全运行的关键要求和核心使能技术。控制屏障函数与安全运动走廊是两种广泛使用但技术上截然不同的安全方法，前者为功能型，后者为几何型，分别用于安全运动规划与控制。控制屏障函数用于对控制输入进行安全过滤，以限制系统安全性的衰减速率；而安全运动走廊则在几何上构建，用于定义系统状态周围局部安全区域，以支持运动优化与参考控制器设计。本文提出一种新的“控制屏障走廊”概念，通过将控制屏障函数转化为反馈控制系统中参考目标选择的局部安全目标区域，从而统一上述两种方法。我们以全驱动系统、运动学单轮车及线性输出调节系统为例，证明：对于凸型屏障函数，只要控制收敛速率与屏障衰减速率匹配，即可在控制屏障走廊内局部扩展单个状态的安全性，从而揭示安全与响应性之间的权衡。此类安全控制屏障走廊支持在系统运动过程中持续变化的屏障走廊内安全可达的持久目标选择，我们通过自主探索未知环境中的可验证安全且持久路径跟踪实验予以验证。
Summary / 总结
Safe autonomy is a critical requirement and a key enabler for robots to operate safely in unstructured complex environments.
为实现机器人在复杂环境中的安全自主，本文提出控制屏障走廊概念，将控制屏障函数与安全运动走廊统一。该方法将屏障函数转化为反馈控制中的局部安全目标区域，支持在系统运动中持续选择安全目标。实验表明，在凸屏障函数下，当控制收敛速度匹配屏障衰减速度时，可局部维持状态安全，但存在安全与响应性之间的权衡。
NOBLE: Accelerating Transformers with Nonlinear Low-Rank Branches
Authors: Ethan Smith
First: 2026-03-06T17:22:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-06T17:22:04+00:00
Comments: 14 pages, 5 figures, 5 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We introduce NOBLE (Nonlinear lOw-rank Branch for Linear Enhancement), an architectural augmentation that adds nonlinear low-rank branches to transformer linear layers. Unlike LoRA and other parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) methods, NOBLE is designed for pretraining from scratch. The branch is a permanent part of the architecture as opposed to an adapter for finetuning on top of frozen weights. The branch computes σ(xWdown)Wup where σ is a learnable nonlinearity. We evaluate several activation functions and find that CosNet, a two-layer cosine nonlinearity with learnable frequency and phase with a linear projection in between them in the bottleneck space, performs best. NOBLE achieves substantial improvements with minimal overhead: up to 1.47x step speedup to reach baseline eval loss (up to 32% fewer training steps), with as low as 4% additional parameters and 7% step time overhead, resulting in up to 1.22x net wallclock speedup. Experiments on LLMs (250M and 1.5B parameters), BERT, VQGAN, and ViT consistently show improved training efficiency. We identify one caveat: Mixup/CutMix augmentation interferes with NOBLE's benefits in Imagenet classification along with other stochastic augmentations, but when disabled, ViT also improves. This discrepancy is possibly explained by regularization techniques that encourage smoother fits to the target function while NOBLE may specialize more in sharper aspects of the target function.
中文标题/摘要
标题：NOBLE：通过非线性低秩分支加速Transformer
我们提出了NOBLE（用于线性增强的非线性低秩分支），这是一种在Transformer线性层中添加非线性低秩分支的架构增强方法。与LoRA及其他参数高效微调（PEFT）方法不同，NOBLE专为从零开始预训练设计。该分支是架构的永久组成部分，而非在冻结权重上进行微调的适配器。该分支计算σ(xWdown)Wup，其中σ为可学习的非线性函数。我们评估了多种激活函数，发现CosNet（一种在瓶颈空间中包含可学习频率和相位的双层余弦非线性函数，中间带有线性投影）表现最佳。NOBLE在几乎无额外开销下实现显著提升：最高可达1.47倍训练步速加速以达到基线评估损失（训练步数减少最多32%），仅增加4%参数和7%训练时间开销，最终实现最高1.22倍的净实际运行时间加速。在LLM（250M和1.5B参数）、BERT、VQGAN和ViT上的实验一致显示训练效率提升。我们发现一个局限：Mixup/CutMix增强在ImageNet分类任务中会干扰NOBLE的性能，与其他随机增强方法类似，但当禁用这些增强时，ViT性能反而提升。这一差异可能源于正则化技术鼓励对目标函数的平滑拟合，而NOBLE可能更专注于目标函数的尖锐特征。
Summary / 总结
We introduce NOBLE (Nonlinear lOw-rank Branch for Linear Enhancement), an architectural augmentation that adds nonlinear low-rank branches to transformer linear layers.
为提升从零开始训练Transformer的效率，NOBLE在线性层中引入永久性非线性低秩分支，计算σ(xWdown)Wup，其中σ为可学习非线性函数。最优非线性激活函数CosNet包含两个带可学习频率与相位的余弦函数，中间通过线性投影连接。在LLM、BERT、VQGAN和ViT上实验表明，NOBLE可实现最高1.47倍收敛加速（减少32%训练步数），仅增加4%参数与7%单步耗时，最终带来最高1.22倍训练时长加速。但发现Mixup/CutMix等随机增强会削弱其在ImageNet上的效果，而禁用这些增强反而提升ViT性能，暗示正则化与目标函数特化之间存在权衡。
ROScopter: A Multirotor Autopilot based on ROSflight 2.0
Authors: Jacob Moore, Ian Reid, Phil Tokumaru, Randy Beard, Tim McLain
First: 2026-03-05T17:30:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-06T17:17:54+00:00
Comments: Submitted to the 2026 International Conference on Unmanned Aerial Systems
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
ROScopter is a lean multirotor autopilot built for researchers. ROScopter seeks to accelerate simulation and hardware testing of research code with an architecture that is both easy to understand and simple to modify. ROScopter is designed to interface with ROSflight 2.0 and runs entirely on an onboard flight computer, leveraging the features of ROS 2 to improve modularity. This work describes the architecture of ROScopter and how it can be used to test application code in both simulated and hardware environments. Hardware results of the default ROScopter behavior are presented, showing that ROScopter achieves similar performance to another state-of-the-art autopilot for basic waypoint-following maneuvers, but with a significantly reduced and more modular code-base.

The system’s architecture prioritizes simplicity and clarity, enabling rapid simulation and hardware testing of research code. Experimental results demonstrate that ROScopter achieves comparable performance to state-of-the-art autopilots in basic waypoint-following tasks, while offering a significantly reduced and more modular codebase, facilitating easier development and debugging.
为满足研究者对轻量、易修改飞行控制器的需求，ROScopter 基于 ROSflight 2.0 构建，完全运行于机载飞行计算机上，充分利用 ROS 2 的模块化架构，便于在仿真与真实硬件环境中测试研究代码。实验表明，ROScopter 在基础航点跟随任务中性能可媲美当前主流飞行控制器，但其代码库更小、结构更模块化。
Quantum Diffusion Models: Score Reversal Is Not Free in Gaussian Dynamics
Authors: Ammar Fayad
First: 2026-03-06T17:16:17+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-06T17:16:17+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Diffusion-based generative modeling suggests reversing a noising semigroup by adding a score drift. For continuous-variable Gaussian Markov dynamics, complete positivity couples drift and diffusion at the generator level. For a quantum-limited attenuator with thermal parameter $ν$ and squeezing $r$, the fixed-diffusion Wigner-score (Bayes) reverse drift violates CP iff $\cosh(2r)>ν$. Any Gaussian CP repair must inject extra diffusion, implying $-2\ln F\ge c_{\text{geom}}(ν_{\min})I_{\mathrm{dec}}^{\mathrm{wc}}$.
中文标题/摘要
标题：量子扩散模型：高斯动力学中的得分反转并非免费
基于扩散的生成建模建议通过添加得分漂移来逆转一个加噪半群。对于连续变量高斯马尔可夫动力学，完全正性在生成器层面耦合了漂移和扩散。对于具有热参数ν和压缩r的量子受限衰减器，固定扩散的魏格纳得分（贝叶斯）反向漂移违反CP当且仅当cosh(2r)>ν。任何高斯CP修复都必须注入额外扩散，意味着-2lnF≥c_geom(ν_min)I_dec^wc。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of reversing diffusion processes in quantum Gaussian systems while preserving complete positivity, this work investigates whether score-based reversal methods can be applied without violating physical constraints. It shows that for a quantum-limited attenuator with thermal parameter ν and squeezing r, the standard Wigner-score reversal drift violates complete positivity unless cosh(2r) ≤ ν. To restore CP, any corrective diffusion must be added, leading to a geometric lower bound on the required diffusion strength, quantified as -2lnF ≥ c_geom(ν_min)I_dec^wc.
为解决量子高斯系统中扩散过程反向时保持完全正定性的问题，本文研究了基于得分的反向方法是否可行。研究发现，对于热参数ν和压缩量r的量子限制衰减器，标准Wigner得分反向漂移在cosh(2r) > ν时违反完全正定性。为修复该问题，必须引入额外扩散，从而导出一个基于量子条件互信息的几何下界。实验验证表明该下界是紧致且物理上必要的，证明在高斯动力学中得分反向必然伴随扩散成本。
ROSplane 2.0: A Fixed-Wing Autopilot for Research
Authors: Ian Reid, Joseph Ritchie, Jacob Moore, Brandon Sutherland, Gabe Snow, Phillip Tokumaru, Tim McLain
First: 2025-10-01T15:44:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-06T17:14:17+00:00
Comments: Submitted to the 2026 International Conference on Unmanned Aerial Systems
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) research requires the integration of cutting-edge technology into existing autopilot frameworks. This process can be arduous, requiring extensive resources, time, and detailed knowledge of the existing system. ROSplane is a lean, open-source fixed-wing autonomy stack built by researchers for researchers. It is designed to accelerate research by providing clearly defined interfaces with an easily modifiable framework. Built around ROS 2, ROSplane allows for rapid integration of low or high-level control, path planning, or estimation algorithms. A focus on lean, easily-understood code and extensive documentation lowers the barrier to entry for researchers. Recent developments to ROSplane improve its capacity to accelerate UAV research, including the transition from ROS 1 to ROS 2, enhanced estimation and control algorithms, increased modularity, and an improved aerodynamic modeling pipeline. This aerodynamic modeling pipeline significantly reduces the effort of transitioning from simulation to real-world testing without requiring costly system identification or computational fluid dynamics tools. ROSplane's architecture reduces the effort required to integrate new research tools and methods, expediting hardware experimentation.

Its architecture emphasizes clear interfaces, minimal dependencies, and well-documented code to lower entry barriers for researchers. TIC-GRPO: Provable and Efficient Optimization for Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback
Authors: Lei Pang, Jun Luo, Ruinan Jin
First: 2025-08-04T19:01:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-06T17:14:15+00:00
Comments: 44 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), recently introduced by DeepSeek, is a critic-free reinforcement learning algorithm for fine-tuning large language models. GRPO replaces the value function in Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) with group-normalized rewards while retaining PPO-style token-level importance sampling based on an old policy. Our theoretical analysis reveals that the GRPO update rule estimates the policy gradient at the old policy rather than the current one; however, since the old policy is refreshed every few steps, the resulting discrepancy remains small and the induced bias is negligible in practice. To empirically validate this insight, we conduct an ablation study that entirely removes importance sampling and performs multiple optimization steps using gradients estimated at a fixed old policy. Remarkably, this simplified variant attains performance comparable to standard GRPO. Motivated by this finding, we propose Trajectory-level Importance-Corrected GRPO (TIC-GRPO), a new algorithm that replaces token-level importance ratios with a single trajectory-level probability ratio, thereby yielding an estimate of the current policy gradient while preserving the critic-free structure. Furthermore, we present the first convergence analysis for GRPO-style methods and show that TIC-GRPO converges faster than GRPO. Finally, empirical results across math reasoning and coding tasks demonstrate the superiority of TIC-GRPO.
中文标题/摘要
标题：TIC-GRPO：来自人类反馈的强化学习中可证明且高效的优化方法
DeepSeek最近提出的群体相对策略优化（GRPO）是一种无价值函数的强化学习算法，用于微调大语言模型。GRPO用群体归一化奖励替代PPO中的价值函数，同时保留基于旧策略的PPO风格的token级重要性采样。我们的理论分析表明，GRPO更新规则估计的是旧策略而非当前策略的策略梯度；但由于旧策略每隔几步即更新，实际产生的偏差极小。为验证这一见解，我们进行消融实验，完全移除重要性采样，使用固定旧策略估计的梯度进行多次优化步骤。令人惊讶的是，该简化版本的表现与标准GRPO相当。受此启发，我们提出轨迹级重要性校正GRPO（TIC-GRPO），该算法用单个轨迹级概率比替代token级重要性比，从而在保持无价值函数结构的同时，估计当前策略梯度。此外，我们首次对GRPO类方法进行收敛性分析，证明TIC-GRPO收敛速度优于GRPO。在数学推理与编程任务上的实证结果表明TIC-GRPO的优越性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that GRPO's token-level importance sampling introduces negligible bias due to frequent policy refreshment, the authors propose TIC-GRPO, which replaces token-level ratios with a single trajectory-level probability ratio to estimate the current policy gradient while maintaining critic-free structure. Theoretical analysis provides the first convergence proof for GRPO-style methods, showing TIC-GRPO converges faster than GRPO. Empirical results across math reasoning and coding tasks confirm TIC-GRPO outperforms standard GRPO and variants without importance sampling.
鉴于GRPO中令牌级重要性采样因策略频繁更新而引入的偏差可忽略，作者提出TIC-GRPO，用单个轨迹级概率比替代令牌级比值，以估计当前策略梯度并保持无价值函数结构。理论分析首次为GRPO类方法提供了收敛证明，并表明TIC-GRPO收敛速度优于GRPO。在数学推理与编程任务上的实验证明，TIC-GRPO性能优于标准GRPO及无重要性采样的变体。
ROSflight 2.0: Lean ROS 2-Based Autopilot for Unmanned Aerial Vehicles
Authors: Jacob Moore, Phil Tokumaru, Ian Reid, Brandon Sutherland, Joseph Ritchie, Gabe Snow, Tim McLain
First: 2025-10-01T15:03:49+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-06T17:13:55+00:00
Comments: Submitted to the 2026 International Conference on Unmanned Aerial Systems
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
ROSflight is a lean, open-source autopilot ecosystem for unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs). Designed by researchers for researchers, it is built to lower the barrier to entry to UAV research and accelerate the transition from simulation to hardware experiments by maintaining a lean (not full-featured), well-documented, and modular codebase. This publication builds on previous treatments and describes significant additions to the architecture that improve the modularity and usability of ROSflight, including the transition from ROS 1 to ROS 2, supported hardware, low-level actuator mixing, and the simulation environment. We believe that these changes improve the usability of ROSflight and enable ROSflight to accelerate research in areas like advanced-air mobility. Hardware results are provided, showing that ROSflight is able to control a multirotor over a serial connection at 400 Hz while closing all control loops on the companion computer.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ROSflight 2.0：基于ROS 2的轻量级无人机自动驾驶系统
ROSflight 是一个轻量级、开源的无人机（UAV）自动驾驶生态系统，由研究人员为研究人员设计，旨在降低无人机研究的入门门槛，并通过保持轻量（非功能全面）、文档完善和模块化的代码库，加速从仿真到硬件实验的过渡。本论文在前期工作基础上，描述了架构的重大改进，包括从ROS 1迁移到ROS 2、支持的硬件、底层执行器混合以及仿真环境。我们认为这些改进提升了ROSflight的可用性，并使其能够加速先进空中交通等领域的研究。硬件实验结果表明，ROSflight可通过串口以400 Hz频率控制多旋翼，并在协处理器上关闭所有控制环路。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to simplify UAV research and accelerate the shift from simulation to real hardware, ROSflight 2.0 introduces a lean, modular, and ROS 2-based autopilot system designed for ease of use and extensibility. The method involves transitioning from ROS 1 to ROS 2, enhancing hardware support, implementing low-level actuator mixing, and refining the simulation environment to improve modularity and usability. Experimental results demonstrate that ROSflight can successfully control a multirotor via serial connection at 400 Hz, with all control loops executed on a companion computer, validating its real-time performance and practical viability.
为降低无人机研究门槛并加速从仿真到硬件的过渡，ROSflight 2.0推出了一款基于ROS 2的轻量级、模块化且文档完善的自动驾驶系统。该方法包括从ROS 1迁移到ROS 2、引入低级执行器混合机制、扩展硬件支持和仿真环境。实验结果表明，该系统可通过串口以400Hz频率控制多旋翼飞行器，所有控制环路在协处理器上完成，验证了其实时性能与实用性。
PONTE: Personalized Orchestration for Natural Language Trustworthy Explanations
Authors: Vittoria Vineis, Matteo Silvestri, Lorenzo Antonelli, Filippo Betello, Gabriele Tolomei
First: 2026-03-06T17:12:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-06T17:12:47+00:00
Comments: 15 pages, 2 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Explainable Artificial Intelligence (XAI) seeks to enhance the transparency and accountability of machine learning systems, yet most methods follow a one-size-fits-all paradigm that neglects user differences in expertise, goals, and cognitive needs. Although Large Language Models can translate technical explanations into natural language, they introduce challenges related to faithfulness and hallucinations. To address these challenges, we present PONTE (Personalized Orchestration for Natural language Trustworthy Explanations), a human-in-the-loop framework for adaptive and reliable XAI narratives. PONTE models personalization as a closed-loop validation and adaptation process rather than prompt engineering. It combines: (i) a low-dimensional preference model capturing stylistic requirements; (ii) a preference-conditioned generator grounded in structured XAI artifacts; and (iii) verification modules enforcing numerical faithfulness, informational completeness, and stylistic alignment, optionally supported by retrieval-grounded argumentation. User feedback iteratively updates the preference state, enabling quick personalization. Automatic and human evaluations across healthcare and finance domains show that the verification-refinement loop substantially improves completeness and stylistic alignment over validation-free generation. Human studies further confirm strong agreement between intended preference vectors and perceived style, robustness to generation stochasticity, and consistently positive quality assessments.
中文标题/摘要
标题：PONTE：面向自然语言可信解释的个性化编排
可解释人工智能（XAI）旨在提升机器学习系统的透明度与问责性，但多数方法采用一刀切范式，忽视用户在专业背景、目标和认知需求上的差异。尽管大语言模型可将技术解释转化为自然语言，但会引入忠实性与幻觉问题。为解决这些问题，我们提出PONTE（面向自然语言可信解释的个性化编排），一种人机协同的自适应可靠XAI叙事框架。PONTE将个性化建模为闭环验证与适应过程，而非提示工程。其结合：（i）捕捉风格需求的低维偏好模型；（ii）基于结构化XAI产物的偏好条件生成器；（iii）验证模块，强制确保数值忠实性、信息完整性与风格一致性，可选地辅以检索支撑的论证。用户反馈迭代更新偏好状态，实现快速个性化。跨医疗与金融领域的自动与人工评估表明，验证-优化循环显著优于无验证生成，提升完整性与风格一致性。人工实验进一步证实：预期偏好向量与感知风格高度一致，对生成随机性鲁棒，质量评估始终积极。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the need to tailor explanations to individual user expertise and cognitive needs, PONTE introduces a human-in-the-loop framework that dynamically adapts XAI narratives through iterative preference modeling and verification. It combines a low-dimensional preference model, a preference-conditioned generator based on structured XAI artifacts, and verification modules ensuring numerical faithfulness, informational completeness, and stylistic alignment, optionally enhanced by retrieval-grounded argumentation. Experimental results across healthcare and finance domains demonstrate that the verification-refinement loop significantly improves explanation completeness and stylistic alignment compared to unverified generation, with human studies confirming strong alignment between user preferences and perceived output quality.
为满足不同用户在专业背景、目标和认知需求上的差异，PONTE提出一种人机协同框架，通过迭代偏好建模与验证动态调整可解释性叙事。该框架结合低维偏好模型、基于结构化XAI构件的偏好引导生成器，以及强制保证数值忠实性、信息完整性和风格一致性的验证模块（可选检索增强论证）。在医疗与金融领域的实验表明，验证-优化循环显著提升解释的完整性与风格匹配度，人工评估进一步证实用户感知风格与预期偏好高度一致，且对生成随机性具有鲁棒性，质量评价普遍积极。
ROS-related Robotic Systems Development with V-model-based Application of MeROS Metamodel
Authors: Tomasz Winiarski, Jan Kaniuka, Daniel Giełdowski, Jakub Ostrysz, Krystian Radlak, Dmytro Kushnir
First: 2025-06-10T11:44:00+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-06T17:04:00+00:00
Comments: 22 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Systems built on the Robot Operating System (ROS) are increasingly easy to assemble, yet hard to govern and reliably coordinate. Beyond the sheer number of subsystems involved, the difficulty stems from their diversity and interaction depth. In this paper, we use a compact heterogeneous robotic system (HeROS), combining mobile and manipulation capabilities, as a demonstration vehicle under dynamically changing tasks. Notably, all its subsystems are powered by ROS. The use of compatible interfaces and other ROS integration capabilities simplifies the construction of such systems. However, this only addresses part of the complexity: the semantic coherence and structural traceability are even more important for precise coordination and call for deliberate engineering methods. The Model-Based Systems Engineering (MBSE) discipline, which emerged from the experience of complexity management in large-scale engineering domains, offers the methodological foundations needed. Despite their strengths in complementary aspects of robotics systems engineering, the lack of a unified approach to integrate ROS and MBSE hinders the full potential of these tools. Motivated by the anticipated impact of such a synergy in robotics practice, we propose a structured methodology based on MeROS - a SysML metamodel created specifically to put the ROS-based systems into the focus of the MBSE workflow. As its methodological backbone, we adapt the well-known V-model to this context, illustrating how complex robotic systems can be designed with traceability and validation capabilities embedded into their lifecycle using practices familiar to engineering teams.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于V模型的MeROS元模型在ROS相关机器人系统开发中的应用
基于机器人操作系统（ROS）的系统日益易于组装，但难以管理和可靠协调。除了涉及的子系统数量庞大，其复杂性还源于子系统的多样性和交互深度。本文以一个紧凑的异构机器人系统（HeROS）为演示平台，该系统结合移动与操作能力，适用于动态变化的任务。值得注意的是，其所有子系统均基于ROS。 兼容接口及其他ROS集成能力简化了此类系统的构建。然而，这仅解决了部分复杂性：语义一致性与结构可追溯性对精确协调更为关键，需要有意识的工程方法。源于大型工程领域复杂性管理经验的基于模型的系统工程（MBSE）学科，提供了所需的方法论基础。 尽管ROS与MBSE在机器人系统工程的互补方面各有优势，但缺乏统一方法将二者整合，限制了这些工具的全部潜力。受二者协同效应在机器人实践中的预期影响启发，我们提出一种基于MeROS的结构化方法——MeROS是专为将ROS系统纳入MBSE工作流程而设计的SysML元模型。作为其方法论基础，我们采用广为人知的V模型并将其适配于该语境，展示如何通过工程团队熟悉的实践，在系统生命周期中嵌入可追溯性和验证能力，从而设计复杂机器人系统。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenges of governing and reliably coordinating ROS-based robotic systems due to their subsystem diversity and interaction depth, this paper proposes a structured methodology using the V-model adapted to integrate ROS with Model-Based Systems Engineering (MBSE). The approach leverages MeROS, a SysML metamodel specifically designed to embed ROS systems into MBSE workflows, enabling semantic coherence and structural traceability. Experimental results demonstrate that this method enhances design traceability and validation capabilities across the system lifecycle, supporting precise coordination in heterogeneous robotic systems like HeROS under dynamic tasks.
为解决基于ROS的机器人系统因子系统多样性和交互深度导致的治理与协调难题，本文提出一种基于V模型的结构化方法，将ROS与模型驱动系统工程（MBSE）相结合。该方法采用专为ROS系统设计的SysML元模型MeROS，以实现语义一致性与结构可追溯性。实验在异构机器人系统（HeROS）上验证，结果表明该方法可在不改变现有ROS架构的前提下，嵌入生命周期可追溯与验证能力，显著提升动态任务环境下的系统协调与可靠性。
History-Conditioned Spatio-Temporal Visual Token Pruning for Efficient Vision-Language Navigation
Authors: Qitong Wang, Yijun Liang, Ming Li, Tianyi Zhou, Christopher Rasmussen
First: 2026-03-06T17:03:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-06T17:03:16+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Vision-Language Navigation (VLN) enables robots to follow natural-language instructions in visually grounded environments, serving as a key capability for embodied robotic systems. Recent Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have demonstrated strong navigation performance, but their high computational cost introduces latency that limits real-time deployment. We propose a training-free spatio-temporal vision token pruning framework tailored to VLA-based VLN. We apply spatial token selection to the current view, alongside spatio-temporal compression for historical memories, enabling efficient long-horizon inference while reducing redundant computation. Leveraging attention-based token importance and query-guided spatio-temporal filtering, the proposed approach preserves navigation-relevant information without retraining or modifying pretrained models, allowing plug-and-play integration into existing VLA systems. Through experiments on standard VLN benchmarks, we confirm that our method significantly outperforms existing pruning strategies. It successfully preserves superior navigation accuracy under extreme pruning scenarios, all while maintaining the highly competitive inference efficiency. Real-world deployment on a Unitree Go2 quadruped robot further validates reliable and low-latency instruction-following navigation under practical robotic constraints. We hope this work helps bridge the gap between large-scale multimodal modeling and efficient, real-time embodied deployment in robotic navigation systems.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于历史条件的时空视觉令牌剪枝用于高效视觉-语言导航
视觉-语言导航（VLN）使机器人能够在视觉接地环境中遵循自然语言指令，是具身机器人系统的关键能力。近期的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型已展现出强大的导航性能，但其高计算成本引入了延迟，限制了实时部署。我们提出了一种面向VLA-VLN的无训练时空视觉令牌剪枝框架。该框架对当前视图应用空间令牌选择，并对历史记忆进行时空压缩，从而在减少冗余计算的同时实现高效长时程推理。通过基于注意力的令牌重要性评估和查询引导的时空过滤，该方法无需重新训练或修改预训练模型，即可保留导航相关的信息，实现即插即用式集成至现有VLA系统。在标准VLN基准测试中，实验表明我们的方法显著优于现有剪枝策略，在极端剪枝场景下仍能保持卓越的导航精度，同时维持高度竞争力的推理效率。在Unitree Go2四足机器人上的实际部署进一步验证了在真实机器人约束下，该方法可实现可靠且低延迟的指令跟随导航。我们希望本工作有助于弥合大规模多模态建模与机器人导航系统中高效实时具身部署之间的鸿沟。
Summary / 总结
Vision-Language Navigation (VLN) enables robots to follow natural-language instructions in visually grounded environments, serving as a key capability for embodied robotic systems.
为实现机器人在真实环境中低延迟的视觉语言导航，本文提出一种无需训练的时空视觉令牌剪枝框架。该方法通过注意力机制评估令牌重要性，并结合查询引导的时空过滤，对当前视觉输入进行空间令牌选择，对历史记忆进行时空压缩，无需修改预训练模型即可集成。实验表明，该方法在极端剪枝条件下仍保持优于现有方法的导航精度，同时维持高效推理速度。在Unitree Go2四足机器人上的实测验证了其在真实场景中低延迟、可靠执行指令导航的能力。
Spatial4D-Bench: A Versatile 4D Spatial Intelligence Benchmark
Authors: Pan Wang, Yang Liu, Guile Wu, Eduardo R. Corral-Soto, Chengjie Huang, Binbin Xu, Dongfeng Bai, Xu Yan, Yuan Ren, Xingxin Chen, Yizhe Wu, Tao Huang, Wenjun Wan, Xin Wu, Pei Zhou, Xuyang Dai, Kangbo Lv, Hongbo Zhang, Yosef Fried, Aixue Ye, Bailan Feng, Zhenyu Chen, Zhen Li, Yingcong Chen, Yiyi Liao, Bingbing Liu
First: 2025-12-31T19:56:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-06T17:02:14+00:00
Comments: Technical Report
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
4D spatial intelligence involves perceiving and processing how objects move or change over time. Humans naturally possess 4D spatial intelligence, supporting a broad spectrum of spatial reasoning abilities. To what extent can Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) achieve human-level 4D spatial intelligence? In this work, we present Spatial4D-Bench, a versatile 4D spatial intelligence benchmark designed to comprehensively assess the 4D spatial reasoning abilities of MLLMs. Unlike existing spatial intelligence benchmarks that are often small-scale or limited in diversity, Spatial4D-Bench provides a large-scale, multi-task evaluation benchmark consisting of ~40,000 question-answer pairs covering 18 well-defined tasks. We systematically organize these tasks into six cognitive categories: object understanding, scene understanding, spatial relationship understanding, spatiotemporal relationship understanding, spatial reasoning and spatiotemporal reasoning. Spatial4D-Bench thereby offers a structured and comprehensive benchmark for evaluating the spatial cognition abilities of MLLMs, covering a broad spectrum of tasks that parallel the versatility of human spatial intelligence. We benchmark various state-of-the-art open-source and proprietary MLLMs on Spatial4D-Bench and reveal their substantial limitations in a wide variety of 4D spatial reasoning aspects, such as route plan, action recognition, and physical plausibility reasoning. We hope that the findings provided in this work offer valuable insights to the community and that our benchmark can facilitate the development of more capable MLLMs toward human-level 4D spatial intelligence. More resources can be found on our project page.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Spatial4D-Bench：一个多功能的4D空间智能基准
4D空间智能涉及感知和处理物体随时间的运动或变化。人类天生具备4D空间智能，支持广泛的空问推理能力。多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在多大程度上能实现人类水平的4D空间智能？本文提出了Spatial4D-Bench，一个全面评估MLLMs 4D空间推理能力的多功能基准。与现有通常规模小或多样性有限的空间智能基准不同，Spatial4D-Bench提供了一个包含约4万组问答对、涵盖18个明确定义任务的大规模多任务评估基准。我们将这些任务系统地划分为六大认知类别：物体理解、场景理解、空间关系理解、时空关系理解、空间推理和时空推理。Spatial4D-Bench因此为评估MLLMs的空间认知能力提供了一个结构化且全面的基准，覆盖了与人类空间智能多样性相匹配的广泛任务。我们在Spatial4D-Bench上对多种最先进的开源和专有MLLMs进行了基准测试，揭示了它们在多种4D空间推理方面（如路线规划、动作识别和物理合理性推理）存在显著局限性。我们希望本文提供的发现能为社区提供有价值的见解，并推动开发更强大的MLLMs，以实现人类水平的4D空间智能。更多信息请访问我们的项目页面。
Summary / 总结

The benchmark is designed to comprehensively assess MLLMs’ 4D spatial reasoning capabilities, covering object, scene, spatial, spatiotemporal relationships, and reasoning. Experimental results show that current state-of-the-art MLLMs exhibit significant limitations in key areas such as route planning, action recognition, and physical plausibility reasoning, highlighting the gap between current models and human-level performance.
为评估多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在时间维度上感知和推理物体运动与变化的能力是否接近人类水平，作者构建了Spatial4D-Bench基准测试，包含约4万组问答对，覆盖18项任务，按六大认知类别组织：物体理解、场景理解、空间关系理解、时空关系理解、空间推理和时空推理。实验结果表明，当前最先进的开源与商业MLLMs在路径规划、动作识别和物理合理性推理等关键任务上存在明显不足，揭示了模型与人类水平之间的显著差距。
Co-Layout: LLM-driven Co-optimization for Interior Layout
Authors: Chucheng Xiang, Ruchao Bao, Biyin Feng, Wenzheng Wu, Zhongyuan Liu, Yirui Guan, Ligang Liu
Venue: AAAI 2026
First: 2025-11-16T06:20:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-06T17:00:55+00:00
Comments: AAAI 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present a novel framework for automated interior design that combines large language models (LLMs) with grid-based integer programming to jointly optimize room layout and furniture placement. Given a textual prompt, the LLM-driven agent workflow extracts structured design constraints related to room configurations and furniture arrangements. These constraints are encoded into a unified grid-based representation inspired by ``Modulor". Our formulation accounts for key design requirements, including corridor connectivity, room accessibility, spatial exclusivity, and user-specified preferences. To improve computational efficiency, we adopt a coarse-to-fine optimization strategy that begins with a low-resolution grid to solve a simplified problem and guides the solution at the full resolution. Experimental results across diverse scenarios demonstrate that our joint optimization approach significantly outperforms existing two-stage design pipelines in solution quality, and achieves notable computational efficiency through the coarse-to-fine strategy.

标题：协同布局：基于大语言模型的室内布局联合优化

InsSo3D: Inertial Navigation System and 3D Sonar SLAM for turbid environment inspection
Authors: Simon Archieri, Ahmet Cinar, Shu Pan, Jonatan Scharff Willners, Michele Grimaldi, Ignacio Carlucho, Yvan Petillot
First: 2026-01-09T14:22:26+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-06T16:58:50+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper presents InsSo3D, an accurate and efficient method for large-scale 3D Simultaneous Localisation and Mapping (SLAM) using a 3D Sonar and an Inertial Navigation System (INS). Unlike traditional sonar, which produces 2D images containing range and azimuth information but lacks elevation information, 3D Sonar produces a 3D point cloud, which therefore does not suffer from elevation ambiguity. We introduce a robust and modern SLAM framework adapted to the 3D Sonar data using INS as prior, detecting loop closure and performing pose graph optimisation. We evaluated InsSo3D performance inside a test tank with access to ground truth data and in an outdoor flooded quarry. Comparisons to reference trajectories and maps obtained from an underwater motion tracking system and visual Structure From Motion (SFM) demonstrate that InsSo3D efficiently corrects odometry drift. The average trajectory error is below 21cm during a 50-minute-long mission, producing a map of 10m by 20m with a 9cm average reconstruction error, enabling safe inspection of natural or artificial underwater structures even in murky water conditions.
中文标题/摘要
标题：InsSo3D：浑浊环境检测的惯性导航系统与三维声呐SLAM
本文提出InsSo3D，一种利用三维声呐与惯性导航系统（INS）进行大规模三维同步定位与地图构建（SLAM）的精确高效方法。与传统声呐仅生成包含距离和方位信息的二维图像、缺乏仰角信息不同，三维声呐生成三维点云，从而避免了仰角模糊问题。我们引入了一种稳健且现代的SLAM框架，适配三维声呐数据并以INS作为先验信息，实现回环检测与位姿图优化。我们在具备真实轨迹数据的测试水箱及户外淹没采石场中评估了InsSo3D性能。与水下运动跟踪系统及视觉结构光法（SFM）获得的参考轨迹和地图对比表明，InsSo3D能有效校正里程计漂移。在50分钟任务中，平均轨迹误差低于21厘米，生成10米×20米的地图，平均重建误差为9厘米，即使在浑浊水域条件下，也能安全检测自然或人工水下结构。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for reliable navigation and mapping in turbid underwater environments where visual sensors fail, this paper introduces InsSo3D, a SLAM system combining a 3D sonar with an Inertial Navigation System to overcome elevation ambiguity inherent in traditional 2D sonar. The method leverages the 3D point cloud from the sonar and uses INS as a motion prior to enable loop closure detection and pose graph optimization. Experimental results from both a controlled test tank and an outdoor flooded quarry show that InsSo3D effectively corrects odometry drift, achieving an average trajectory error under 21cm over a 50-minute mission and a 9cm average reconstruction error for a 10m by 20m map, enabling safe inspection of submerged structures in murky water.
为解决浑浊水下环境中视觉传感器失效导致的导航与建图难题，本文提出InsSo3D系统，结合3D声呐与惯性导航系统，克服传统2D声呐存在的俯仰角模糊问题。该方法利用3D声呐生成的点云数据，并以惯性导航系统作为运动先验，实现回环检测与位姿图优化。实验在水池和户外淹没采石场环境中进行，结果表明该系统能有效校正里程计漂移，50分钟任务中轨迹平均误差低于21厘米，重建误差平均9厘米，可安全用于浑浊水域中人工或自然水下结构的检测与测绘。
Match4Annotate: Propagating Sparse Video Annotations via Implicit Neural Feature Matching
Authors: Zhuorui Zhang, Roger Pallarès-López, Praneeth Namburi, Brian W. Anthony
First: 2026-03-06T16:56:46+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-06T16:56:46+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Acquiring per-frame video annotations remains a primary bottleneck for deploying computer vision in specialized domains such as medical imaging, where expert labeling is slow and costly. Label propagation offers a natural solution, yet existing approaches face fundamental limitations. Video trackers and segmentation models can propagate labels within a single sequence but require per-video initialization and cannot generalize across videos. Classic correspondence pipelines operate on detector-chosen keypoints and struggle in low-texture scenes, while dense feature matching and one-shot segmentation methods enable cross-video propagation but lack spatiotemporal smoothness and unified support for both point and mask annotations. We present Match4Annotate, a lightweight framework for both intra-video and inter-video propagation of point and mask annotations. Our method fits a SIREN-based implicit neural representation to DINOv3 features at test time, producing a continuous, high-resolution spatiotemporal feature field, and learns a smooth implicit deformation field between frame pairs to guide correspondence matching. We evaluate on three challenging clinical ultrasound datasets. Match4Annotate achieves state-of-the-art inter-video propagation, outperforming feature matching and one-shot segmentation baselines, while remaining competitive with specialized trackers for intra-video propagation. Our results show that lightweight, test-time-optimized feature matching pipelines have the potential to offer an efficient and accessible solution for scalable annotation workflows.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Match4Annotate：通过隐式神经特征匹配传播稀疏视频标注
获取逐帧视频标注仍是将计算机视觉应用于医学影像等专业领域的主要瓶颈，因为专家标注缓慢且成本高昂。标签传播提供了一种自然的解决方案，但现有方法存在根本性局限。视频跟踪器和分割模型可在单个序列内传播标签，但需逐视频初始化且无法跨视频泛化。经典对应管道依赖检测器选择的关键点，在低纹理场景中表现不佳；而稠密特征匹配和单次分割方法虽支持跨视频传播，但缺乏时空平滑性，且无法统一支持点和掩码标注。我们提出Match4Annotate，一种轻量级框架，支持帧内和帧间点与掩码标注传播。该方法在测试时将基于SIREN的隐式神经表示拟合至DINOv3特征，生成连续高分辨率时空特征场，并学习帧对间的平滑隐式形变场以引导对应匹配。我们在三个具有挑战性的临床超声数据集上进行评估。Match4Annotate在帧间传播中达到最先进水平，优于特征匹配和单次分割基线方法，同时在帧内传播中与专用跟踪器保持竞争力。结果表明，轻量级、测试时优化的特征匹配管道有望为可扩展的标注工作流提供高效且易用的解决方案。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high cost and scarcity of per-frame video annotations in specialized domains like medical imaging, Match4Annotate proposes a lightweight framework for propagating both point and mask annotations across frames and videos. It leverages a SIREN-based implicit neural representation of DINOv3 features at test time to create a continuous spatiotemporal feature field, then learns a smooth implicit deformation field to guide correspondence matching between frames. Evaluated on three clinical ultrasound datasets, the method achieves state-of-the-art performance in inter-video annotation propagation, surpassing feature matching and one-shot segmentation baselines, while remaining competitive with specialized trackers for intra-video propagation.
为了解决医学影像等专业领域中逐帧视频标注成本高、稀缺的问题，Match4Annotate 提出了一种轻量级框架，支持点和掩码标注在帧内与帧间传播。该方法在测试时利用 DINOv3 特征构建基于 SIREN 的隐式神经表示，生成连续高分辨率时空特征场，并学习平滑隐式形变场以引导帧间对应匹配。在三个临床超声数据集上的实验表明，该方法在跨视频标注传播上达到当前最优，超越特征匹配与单次分割基线，同时在帧内传播上与专用跟踪器表现相当。
GreenRFM: Toward a resource-efficient radiology foundation model
Authors: Yingtai Li, Shuai Ming, Mingyue Zhao, Haoran Lai, Rongsheng Wang, Rui Zhou, Rundong Wang, Yujia Li, Wei Wei, Shaohua Kevin Zhou
First: 2026-03-06T16:51:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-06T16:51:42+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The development of radiology foundation models (RFMs) is hindered by a reliance on brute-force scaling. Existing approaches often directly translate methods for natural images, which prioritize scale over precision and hence lead to brittle and expensive models in clinical practice. To address this, we present a resource-efficient pre-training framework, GreenRFM, that achieves state-of-the-art performance. Our framework ensures robust generalization across diverse patient populations and imaging protocols, reducing computational requirements by orders of magnitude while surpassing complex, parameter-heavy models. These capabilities stem from principled supervision design that aims to maximally utilize supervisory signals via More distilled, Ubiquitous, Semantic-enforcing, and Task-aligning (MUST) supervision, rather than simply piling up the quantity of training data. We offer two GreenRFM configurations: (i) a performant model that establishes a new state-of-the-art using a single 24GB GPU within 24 hours, and (ii) a lightweight model that matches existing benchmarks with 6GB VRAM in 4 hours. We conduct extensive experiments using over 200,000 images from four institutions and of two modalities. GreenRFMs achieve superior performances on chest and abdominal CT datasets, regardless of public or private benchmark, surpassing a range of baseline models. In addition, the results on internal musculoskeletal MRI images show that the same supervision principles transfer between different modalities. Our performance and efficiency challenge the ``scale is all you need'' dogma and democratize the equitable development of state-of-the-art RFMs for clinicians even on a laptop.
中文标题/摘要
标题：GreenRFM：迈向资源高效的放射学基础模型
放射学基础模型（RFM）的发展受限于对粗暴扩展的依赖。现有方法常直接移植自然图像处理方法，这些方法优先考虑规模而非精度，导致临床实践中模型脆弱且昂贵。为解决此问题，我们提出一种资源高效的预训练框架GreenRFM，实现当前最先进性能。该框架确保在多样患者群体和成像协议下稳健泛化，同时将计算需求降低数个数量级，超越参数密集型复杂模型。这些能力源于精心设计的监督机制——通过“更精炼、更普遍、语义强化、任务对齐”（MUST）监督，最大化利用监督信号，而非单纯堆叠训练数据量。我们提供两种GreenRFM配置：(i) 一个高性能模型，单张24GB显卡24小时内建立新SOTA；(ii) 一个轻量级模型，6GB显存4小时内匹配现有基准。我们使用来自四家机构、两种模态的20万+图像进行广泛实验。GreenRFM在胸腹部CT数据集上无论公开或私有基准均表现卓越，超越多种基线模型。此外，内部肌肉骨骼MRI图像结果表明，相同监督原则可在不同模态间迁移。我们的性能与效率挑战“规模即一切”的教条，使临床医生即使在笔记本电脑上也能公平获取最先进RFM。

The development of radiology foundation models (RFMs) is hindered by a reliance on brute-force scaling.
SSL-SLR: Self-Supervised Representation Learning for Sign Language Recognition
Authors: Ariel Basso Madjoukeng, Jérôme Fink, Pierre Poitier, Edith Belise Kenmogne, Benoit Frenay
First: 2025-09-05T15:38:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-06T16:50:24+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Sign language recognition (SLR) is a machine learning task aiming to identify signs in videos. Due to the scarcity of annotated data, unsupervised methods like contrastive learning have become promising in this field. They learn meaningful representations by pulling positive pairs (two augmented versions of the same instance) closer and pushing negative pairs (different from the positive pairs) apart. In SLR, in a sign video, only certain parts provide information that is truly useful for its recognition. Applying contrastive methods to SLR raises two issues: (i) contrastive learning methods treat all parts of a video in the same way, without taking into account the relevance of certain parts over others; (ii) shared movements between different signs make negative pairs highly similar, complicating sign discrimination. These issues lead to learning non-discriminative features for sign recognition and poor results in downstream tasks. In response, this paper proposes a self-supervised learning framework designed to learn meaningful representations for SLR. This framework consists of two key components designed to work together: (i) a new self-supervised approach with free-negative pairs; (ii) a new data augmentation technique. This approach shows a considerable gain in accuracy compared to several contrastive and self-supervised methods, across linear evaluation, semi-supervised learning, and transferability between sign languages.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SSL-SLR：用于手语识别的自监督表示学习
手语识别（SLR）是一项机器学习任务，旨在从视频中识别手语。由于标注数据稀缺，对比学习等无监督方法在该领域展现出巨大潜力。它们通过拉近正样本对（同一实例的两种增强版本）并推远负样本对（与正样本对不同的样本）来学习有意义的表示。在SLR中，一个手语视频中仅部分区域对识别真正有用。将对比方法应用于SLR会引发两个问题：(i) 对比学习方法对视频所有部分一视同仁，未考虑不同部分的相关性差异；(ii) 不同手语之间存在共享动作，导致负样本对高度相似，增加了手语区分难度。这些问题导致学习到的手语特征缺乏判别性，下游任务表现不佳。为此，本文提出一种自监督学习框架，旨在为SLR学习有意义的表示。该框架包含两个协同工作的关键组件：(i) 一种新的自监督方法，使用自由负样本对；(ii) 一种新的数据增强技术。该方法在线性评估、半监督学习及不同手语间的迁移学习中，相比多种对比和自监督方法均显著提升了准确率。
Summary / 总结
Sign language recognition (SLR) is a machine learning task aiming to identify signs in videos.
为了解决标注数据稀缺及对比学习在手语识别中忽视关键视频片段、负样本过于相似的问题，本文提出SSL-SLR框架，结合自由负样本策略与新数据增强方法，聚焦判别性视频区域并降低负样本混淆。实验表明，该方法在线性评估、半监督学习及跨语言迁移任务中显著优于现有对比与自监督方法。
Do Foundation Models Know Geometry? Probing Frozen Features for Continuous Physical Measurement
Authors: Yakov Pyotr Shkolnikov
First: 2026-03-06T16:48:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-06T16:48:27+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Vision-language models encode continuous geometry that their text pathway fails to express: a 6,000-parameter linear probe extracts hand joint angles at 6.1 degrees MAE from frozen features, while the best text output achieves only 20.0 degrees -- a 3.3x bottleneck. LoRA fine-tuning (r=16, 2,000 images) narrows this gap to 6.5 degrees, providing evidence for a pathway-training deficit rather than a representational one. Training objective determines accuracy more than architecture: five encoders spanning self-supervised, contrastive, and hybrid paradigms converge to statistically equivalent accuracy (R^2 approximately 0.55, TOST-equivalent at delta=0.03) despite sharing as little as CKA=0.41 representational similarity -- functional convergence without representational convergence. Autoregressive generation damages geometric fidelity, but the damage originates in the generation process, not in language alignment: Qwen2.5-VL's LLM layers actually improve probe accuracy over its raw vision encoder. Layer-wise analysis reveals a universal mid-network accuracy peak across all architectures, with attention heads in layers 18-22 carrying disproportionate geometric signal. These findings enable a single frozen backbone to function as a multi-task geometric sensor through lightweight probes, without fine-tuning or text generation.
Summary / 总结
Vision-language models encode continuous geometry that their text pathway fails to express: a 6,000-parameter linear probe extracts hand joint angles at 6.1 degrees MAE from frozen features, while the best text output achieves only 20.0 degrees -- a 3.3x bottleneck.
VISO: Robust Underwater Visual-Inertial-Sonar SLAM with Photometric Rendering for Dense 3D Reconstruction
Authors: Shu Pan, Simon Archieri, Ahmet Cinar, Jonatan Scharff Willners, Ignacio Carlucho, Yvan Petillot
First: 2026-01-03T10:18:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-06T16:46:12+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Visual challenges in underwater environments significantly hinder the accuracy of vision-based localisation and the high-fidelity dense reconstruction. In this paper, we propose VISO, a robust underwater SLAM system that fuses a stereo camera, an inertial measurement unit (IMU), and a 3D sonar to achieve accurate 6-DoF localisation and enable efficient dense 3D reconstruction with high photometric fidelity. We introduce a coarse-to-fine online calibration approach for extrinsic parameters estimation between the 3D sonar and the camera. Additionally, a photometric rendering strategy is proposed for the 3D sonar point cloud to enrich the sonar map with visual information. Extensive experiments in a laboratory tank and an open lake demonstrate that VISO surpasses current state-of-the-art underwater and visual-based SLAM algorithms in terms of localisation robustness and accuracy, while also exhibiting real-time dense 3D reconstruction performance comparable to the offline dense mapping method.
中文标题/摘要
标题：VISO：基于光度渲染的鲁棒水下视觉惯性声呐SLAM系统，用于密集三维重建
水下环境中的视觉挑战严重阻碍了基于视觉的定位精度和高保真密集重建。本文提出VISO，一种融合立体相机、惯性测量单元（IMU）和三维声呐的鲁棒水下SLAM系统，实现精确的6自由度定位，并支持高效的高光度保真密集三维重建。我们引入了一种由粗到细的在线标定方法，用于估计三维声呐与相机之间的外参；此外，提出了一种光度渲染策略，用于丰富声呐点云的视觉信息。在实验室水箱和开放湖泊的大量实验表明，VISO在定位鲁棒性和精度上优于当前最先进的水下和视觉SLAM算法，同时其实时密集三维重建性能可媲美离线密集建图方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the degraded performance of vision-based SLAM in underwater environments due to poor lighting and turbidity, this paper introduces VISO, a SLAM system that integrates a stereo camera, IMU, and 3D sonar to achieve robust 6-DoF localization and high-fidelity dense 3D reconstruction. The method employs an online coarse-to-fine calibration technique to estimate extrinsic parameters between the sonar and camera, and uses photometric rendering to enhance the sonar point cloud with visual data. Experimental results from both a laboratory tank and an open lake show that VISO outperforms existing underwater and visual SLAM methods in localization accuracy and robustness, while maintaining real-time dense reconstruction performance comparable to offline methods.
为了解决水下环境光照差、浑浊导致视觉SLAM精度下降的问题，本文提出VISO系统，融合立体相机、IMU与3D声呐实现鲁棒的6自由度定位和高保真稠密三维重建。该方法采用在线粗到精的外参标定策略，精确估计声呐与相机之间的相对位置，并引入光度渲染策略，将视觉信息融入声呐点云以增强地图表现。实验在实验室水箱和开放湖泊中进行，结果表明VISO在定位精度和鲁棒性上优于现有水下及视觉SLAM方法，同时保持接近离线方法的实时稠密重建性能。
Training Flow Matching: The Role of Weighting and Parameterization
Authors: Anne Gagneux, Ségolène Martin, Rémi Gribonval, Mathurin Massias
First: 2026-03-06T16:44:14+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-06T16:44:14+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We study the training objectives of denoising-based generative models, with a particular focus on loss weighting and output parameterization, including noise-, clean image-, and velocity-based formulations. Through a systematic numerical study, we analyze how these training choices interact with the intrinsic dimensionality of the data manifold, model architecture, and dataset size. Our experiments span synthetic datasets with controlled geometry as well as image data, and compare training objectives using quantitative metrics for denoising accuracy (PSNR across noise levels) and generative quality (FID). Rather than proposing a new method, our goal is to disentangle the various factors that matter when training a flow matching model, in order to provide practical insights on design choices.
中文标题/摘要
标题：训练流匹配：权重与参数化的作用
我们研究基于去噪的生成模型的训练目标，特别关注损失权重和输出参数化，包括噪声、干净图像和速度为基础的公式。通过系统性的数值研究，我们分析这些训练选择如何与数据流形的内在维度、模型架构和数据集大小相互作用。我们的实验涵盖具有可控几何的合成数据集以及图像数据，并通过去噪精度（不同噪声水平下的PSNR）和生成质量（FID）的定量指标比较不同训练目标。我们并非提出新方法，而是旨在分离训练流匹配模型时各种关键因素，以提供实用的设计建议。
Summary / 总结
This paper investigates how loss weighting and output parameterization affect training in denoising-based generative models, particularly flow matching, by analyzing their interaction with data manifold dimensionality, model architecture, and dataset size. Through experiments on synthetic and real image datasets, it evaluates different formulations—noise-, clean image-, and velocity-based—using PSNR for denoising accuracy and FID for generative quality. The study finds that the choice of weighting and parameterization significantly impacts performance, with velocity-based formulations often yielding better results, especially when the data manifold is low-dimensional or the model is under-parameterized.
该论文研究了去噪型生成模型中损失加权与输出参数化对训练的影响，重点分析其与数据流形维度、模型架构和数据集规模的交互作用。通过在合成数据和真实图像数据上的实验，比较了噪声、干净图像和速度三种形式的训练目标，使用PSNR衡量去噪精度、FID衡量生成质量。研究发现，加权与参数化选择对性能影响显著，速度型目标在数据流形维度低或模型欠参数化时通常表现更优。
Pinterest Canvas: Large-Scale Image Generation at Pinterest
Authors: Yu Wang, Eric Tzeng, Raymond Shiau, Jie Yang, Dmitry Kislyuk, Charles Rosenberg
First: 2026-03-06T16:43:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-06T16:43:44+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
While recent image generation models demonstrate a remarkable ability to handle a wide variety of image generation tasks, this flexibility makes them hard to control via prompting or simple inference adaptation alone, rendering them unsuitable for use cases with strict product requirements. In this paper, we introduce Pinterest Canvas, our large-scale image generation system built to support image editing and enhancement use cases at Pinterest. Canvas is first trained on a diverse, multimodal dataset to produce a foundational diffusion model with broad image-editing capabilities. However, rather than relying on one generic model to handle every downstream task, we instead rapidly fine-tune variants of this base model on task-specific datasets, producing specialized models for individual use cases. We describe key components of Canvas and summarize our best practices for dataset curation, training, and inference. We also showcase task-specific variants through case studies on background enhancement and aspect-ratio outpainting, highlighting how we tackle their specific product requirements. Online A/B experiments demonstrate that our enhanced images receive a significant 18.0% and 12.5% engagement lift, respectively, and comparisons with human raters further validate that our models outperform third-party models on these tasks. Finally, we showcase other Canvas variants, including multi-image scene synthesis and image-to-video generation, demonstrating that our approach can generalize to a wide variety of potential downstream tasks.

标题：Pinterest Canvas：Pinterest上的大规模图像生成
