Experimental results show that these variants deliver a 18.0% and 12.5% engagement lift in online A/B tests, and human evaluations confirm superior performance over third-party models. The system also generalizes to other tasks like multi-image synthesis and image-to-video generation, demonstrating its adaptability across diverse downstream applications.
为满足实际产品中对可控且任务特定图像生成的需求，Pinterest Canvas 首先训练一个广义多模态扩散模型，再针对不同任务进行微调，生成专用模型。方法包括数据集构建、任务导向微调与推理优化，应用于背景增强和宽高比扩展等场景。实验表明，这些模型在A/B测试中分别带来18.0%和12.5%的互动提升，人工评估也证实其优于第三方模型。系统还可扩展至多图合成与图像生成视频等任务，展示其在多种下游应用中的泛化能力。
Entropic Mirror Descent for Linear Systems: Polyak's Stepsize and Implicit Bias
Authors: Yura Malitsky, Alexander Posch
First: 2025-05-05T12:33:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-06T16:43:39+00:00
Comments: 20 pages, 2 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper focuses on applying entropic mirror descent to solve linear systems, where the main challenge for the convergence analysis stems from the unboundedness of the domain. To overcome this without imposing restrictive assumptions, we introduce a variant of Polyak-type stepsizes. Along the way, we strengthen the bound for $\ell_1$-norm implicit bias, obtain sublinear and linear convergence results, and generalize the convergence result to arbitrary convex $L$-smooth functions. We also propose an alternative method that avoids exponentiation, resembling the original Hadamard descent, but with provable convergence.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于线性系统的熵镜像下降法：Polyak步长与隐式偏差
本文聚焦于将熵镜像下降法应用于求解线性系统，其中收敛性分析的主要挑战源于域的无界性。为在不施加严格假设的前提下克服这一问题，我们引入了一种Polyak型步长变体。在此过程中，我们加强了ℓ₁范数隐式偏差的界，获得了次线性和线性收敛结果，并将收敛性结果推广至任意凸L-光滑函数。此外，我们还提出了一种避免指数运算的替代方法，类似于原始的Hadamard下降法，但具有可证明的收敛性。
Summary / 总结
This paper focuses on applying entropic mirror descent to solve linear systems, where the main challenge for the convergence analysis stems from the unboundedness of the domain.
为解决线性系统中熵镜像下降法在无界域上收敛性分析的困难，本文引入一种Polyak型步长变体，无需附加限制假设即可保证收敛。该方法强化了$\ell_1$范数隐式偏差的界，同时获得次线性和线性收敛结果，并将收敛性推广至任意凸$L$-光滑函数。此外，还提出一种避免指数运算的替代方法，形式类似Hadamard下降，且具备可证明的收敛性。
Data Analogies Enable Efficient Cross-Embodiment Transfer
Authors: Jonathan Yang, Chelsea Finn, Dorsa Sadigh
First: 2026-03-06T16:42:46+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-06T16:42:46+00:00
Comments: 14 pages, 11 Figures, 6 Tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Generalist robot policies are trained on demonstrations collected across a wide variety of robots, scenes, and viewpoints. Yet it remains unclear how to best organize and scale such heterogeneous data so that it genuinely improves performance in a given target setting. In this work, we ask: what form of demonstration data is most useful for enabling transfer across robot set-ups? We conduct controlled experiments that vary end-effector morphology, robot platform appearance, and camera perspective, and compare the effects of simply scaling the number of demonstrations against systematically broadening the diversity in different ways. Our simulated experiments show that while perceptual shifts such as viewpoint benefit most from broad diversity, morphology shifts benefit far less from unstructured diversity and instead see the largest gains from data analogies, i.e. paired demonstrations that align scenes, tasks, and/or trajectories across different embodiments. Informed by the simulation results, we improve real-world cross-embodiment transfer success by an average of $22.5\%$ over large-scale, unpaired datasets by changing only the composition of the data.
中文标题/摘要
标题：数据类比促进高效跨具身迁移
通用型机器人策略通常在多种机器人、场景和视角收集的演示数据上进行训练。然而，如何最好地组织和扩展此类异构数据，以真正提升特定目标环境中的性能，仍不明确。本研究探讨：哪种形式的演示数据最有利于实现跨机器人配置的迁移？我们通过控制实验，分别改变末端执行器形态、机器人平台外观和相机视角，并比较单纯增加演示数量与系统性扩展多样性在不同方式下的效果。模拟实验表明，虽然视角等感知变化最受益于广泛多样性，但形态变化对无结构多样性收益甚微，反而在数据类比（即跨不同具身对齐场景、任务和/或轨迹的配对演示）中获得最大增益。受模拟结果启发，我们仅通过调整数据构成，便将真实世界跨具身迁移成功率平均提升了22.5%，优于大规模无配对数据集。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of efficiently transferring robot policies across diverse physical embodiments, this work investigates how to best structure heterogeneous demonstration data for cross-embodiment transfer. Through controlled simulations varying end-effector morphology, robot appearance, and camera viewpoint, the authors compare scaling data quantity versus enhancing diversity via data analogies—paired demonstrations that align scenes, tasks, or trajectories across different robot forms. Results show that while viewpoint diversity benefits from broad data, morphology shifts gain significantly more from structured analogies, leading to a 22.5% average improvement in real-world transfer performance when replacing unpaired datasets with analogy-aligned ones.
为解决机器人策略在不同物理形态间高效迁移的问题，本研究探讨如何最优组织异构演示数据以促进跨形态迁移。通过控制实验，分别改变末端执行器形态、机器人外观和摄像机视角，对比单纯增加数据量与通过数据类比（即跨形态对齐场景、任务或轨迹的配对演示）提升多样性效果。结果表明，视角多样性从广泛数据中获益显著，而形态变化则更依赖结构化类比，采用类比数据后，真实世界迁移成功率平均提升22.5%。
How Reliable is Language Model Micro-Benchmarking?
Authors: Gregory Yauney, Shahzaib Saqib Warraich, Swabha Swayamdipta
Venue: ICLR 2026
First: 2025-10-09T18:37:03+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-06T16:42:35+00:00
Comments: Published at ICLR 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Micro-benchmarking offers a solution to the often prohibitive time and cost of language model development: evaluate on a very small subset of existing benchmarks. Can these micro-benchmarks, however, rank models as consistently as the full benchmarks they replace? And can they rank models more consistently than selecting a random subset of data points? In many scenarios, we find that the answer is no. We introduce a meta-evaluation measure for micro-benchmarking which investigates how well a micro-benchmark can rank two models as a function of their performance difference on the full benchmark. This approach can determine which model pairs can be ranked correctly by a micro-benchmark, allowing for a finer-grained analysis of the trade-off between micro-benchmark size and reliability. Prior work has suggested selecting as few as 10 examples; we find that no micro-benchmarking method can consistently rank model pairs 3.5 points of accuracy apart on MMLU-Pro or 4 points apart on BIG-bench Hard. In order to consistently rank model pairs with relatively similar performances, we show that often as many as 250 examples must be selected, at which point random sampling is competitive with existing micro-benchmarking methods. When comparing only 8B instruction-tuned models on MMLU-Pro micro-benchmarks with 25 examples, we find that more than half of pairwise comparisons are not likely to be preserved. Our work provides actionable guidance for both micro-benchmark users and developers in navigating the trade-off between evaluation efficiency and reliability.
Summary / 总结
This paper investigates whether micro-benchmarks—small subsets of existing language model evaluation datasets—can reliably rank models as consistently as full benchmarks. The authors introduce a meta-evaluation framework that measures how well micro-benchmarks can distinguish model pairs based on their true performance gap on full benchmarks. Their experiments reveal that even with as few as 10 examples, micro-benchmarks fail to consistently rank models differing by 3.5 points on MMLU-Pro or 4 points on BIG-bench Hard. They find that 250 examples are often needed to match or exceed random sampling performance for reliable ranking, and even then, over half of pairwise comparisons among 8B instruction-tuned models on 25-example micro-benchmarks are unreliable. The study offers practical guidance for developers and users seeking to balance evaluation efficiency with accuracy.
本文研究了微基准测试（即现有评估数据集的小子集）是否能像完整基准一样可靠地对语言模型进行排序。作者提出一种元评估框架，用于衡量微基准在多大程度上能根据模型在完整基准上的真实性能差距区分模型对。实验表明，即使仅用10个样本，微基准也无法稳定区分MMLU-Pro上相差3.5分或BIG-bench Hard上相差4分的模型。研究发现，通常需要250个样本才能达到或超越随机采样的排序效果，而即便如此，在仅用25个样本对8B指令调优模型进行比较时，超过一半的模型对排序结果不可靠。该研究为开发者和用户提供了在评估效率与可靠性之间权衡的实用指导。
Taxonomy-aware Dynamic Motion Generation on Hyperbolic Manifolds
Authors: Luis Augenstein, Noémie Jaquier, Tamim Asfour, Leonel Rozo
Venue: ICRA
First: 2025-09-25T15:03:03+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-06T16:42:28+00:00
Comments: Accepted for publication in IEEE Conference on Robotics and Automation (ICRA), 8 pages, 6 figures, 1 table
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Human-like motion generation for robots often draws inspiration from biomechanical studies, which often categorize complex human motions into hierarchical taxonomies. While these taxonomies provide rich structural information about how movements relate to one another, this information is frequently overlooked in motion generation models, leading to a disconnect between the generated motions and their underlying hierarchical structure. This paper introduces the \ac{gphdm}, a novel approach that learns latent representations preserving both the hierarchical structure of motions and their temporal dynamics to ensure physical consistency. Our model achieves this by extending the dynamics prior of the Gaussian Process Dynamical Model (GPDM) to the hyperbolic manifold and integrating it with taxonomy-aware inductive biases. Building on this geometry- and taxonomy-aware frameworks, we propose three novel mechanisms for generating motions that are both taxonomically-structured and physically-consistent: two probabilistic recursive approaches and a method based on pullback-metric geodesics. Experiments on generating realistic motion sequences on the hand grasping taxonomy show that the proposed GPHDM faithfully encodes the underlying taxonomy and temporal dynamics, and it generates novel physically-consistent trajectories.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于双曲流形的分类感知动态运动生成
机器人的人类化运动生成常借鉴生物力学研究，后者常将复杂的人类运动划分为分层分类体系。尽管这些分类体系提供了运动间关系的丰富结构信息，但运动生成模型常忽略此类信息，导致生成运动与底层分层结构脱节。本文提出GPHDM，一种新颖方法，通过学习保留运动分层结构及其时间动态的潜在表示，确保物理一致性。该模型通过将高斯过程动态模型（GPDM）的动力学先验扩展至双曲流形，并结合分类感知的归纳偏置实现。基于此几何与分类感知框架，我们提出三种新机制，生成兼具分类结构与物理一致性的运动：两种概率递归方法及基于拉回度量测地线的方法。在手部抓取分类体系上的实验表明，所提GPHDM忠实地编码了底层分类与时间动态，并生成新颖的物理一致轨迹。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to align robot motion generation with hierarchical biomechanical taxonomies often overlooked by existing models, this paper introduces GPHDM, a method that embeds motion dynamics into hyperbolic manifolds to preserve hierarchical structure and physical consistency. By extending Gaussian Process Dynamical Models to hyperbolic geometry and integrating taxonomy-aware inductive biases, GPHDM employs two probabilistic recursive mechanisms and a pullback-metric geodesic method to generate structured, physically valid motion sequences. Experiments on hand grasping taxonomy demonstrate that GPHDM accurately encodes hierarchical motion relationships and produces novel, realistic trajectories that respect both taxonomy and dynamics.
为使机器人运动生成与生物力学分类体系（其捕捉运动的层次结构）对齐，本文提出GPHDM模型，该模型在双曲流形上学习同时保留运动层次结构与时间动态的潜在表示。它将高斯过程动力学模型扩展至双曲几何，并融合分类感知的归纳偏置，实现物理一致的运动生成。提出三种新机制——概率递归方法与拉回度量测地线方法——以生成结构化且物理合理的轨迹。在手部抓取分类体系上的实验表明，GPHDM准确编码了运动的层次关系，并生成了新颖、真实且物理一致的运动序列。
CaTok: Taming Mean Flows for One-Dimensional Causal Image Tokenization
Authors: Yitong Chen, Zuxuan Wu, Xipeng Qiu, Yu-Gang Jiang
First: 2026-03-06T16:39:17+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-06T16:39:17+00:00
Comments: Project website is available in https://sharelab-sii.github.io/catok-web
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Autoregressive (AR) language models rely on causal tokenization, but extending this paradigm to vision remains non-trivial. Current visual tokenizers either flatten 2D patches into non-causal sequences or enforce heuristic orderings that misalign with the "next-token prediction" pattern. Recent diffusion autoencoders similarly fall short: conditioning the decoder on all tokens lacks causality, while applying nested dropout mechanism introduces imbalance. To address these challenges, we present CaTok, a 1D causal image tokenizer with a MeanFlow decoder. By selecting tokens over time intervals and binding them to the MeanFlow objective, as illustrated in Fig. 1, CaTok learns causal 1D representations that support both fast one-step generation and high-fidelity multi-step sampling, while naturally capturing diverse visual concepts across token intervals. To further stabilize and accelerate training, we propose a straightforward regularization REPA-A, which aligns encoder features with Vision Foundation Models (VFMs). Experiments demonstrate that CaTok achieves state-of-the-art results on ImageNet reconstruction, reaching 0.75 FID, 22.53 PSNR and 0.674 SSIM with fewer training epochs, and the AR model attains performance comparable to leading approaches.
Summary / 总结
Autoregressive (AR) language models rely on causal tokenization, but extending this paradigm to vision remains non-trivial.
What if? Emulative Simulation with World Models for Situated Reasoning
Authors: Ruiping Liu, Yufan Chen, Yuheng Zhang, Junwei Zheng, Kunyu Peng, Chengzhi Wu, Chenguang Huang, Di Wen, Jiaming Zhang, Kailun Yang, Rainer Stiefelhagen
First: 2026-03-06T16:37:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-06T16:37:15+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Situated reasoning often relies on active exploration, yet in many real-world scenarios such exploration is infeasible due to physical constraints of robots or safety concerns of visually impaired users. Given only a limited observation, can an agent mentally simulate a future trajectory toward a target situation and answer spatial what-if questions? We introduce WanderDream, the first large-scale dataset designed for the emulative simulation of mental exploration, enabling models to reason without active exploration. WanderDream-Gen comprises 15.8K panoramic videos across 1,088 real scenes from HM3D, ScanNet++, and real-world captures, depicting imagined trajectories from current viewpoints to target situations. WanderDream-QA contains 158K question-answer pairs, covering starting states, paths, and end states along each trajectory to comprehensively evaluate exploration-based reasoning. Extensive experiments with world models and MLLMs demonstrate (1) that mental exploration is essential for situated reasoning, (2) that world models achieve compelling performance on WanderDream-Gen, (3) that imagination substantially facilitates reasoning on WanderDream-QA, and (4) that WanderDream data exhibit remarkable transferability to real-world scenarios. The source code and all data will be released.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for situated reasoning without physical exploration, especially in constrained or unsafe environments, this work introduces WanderDream, a large-scale dataset enabling mental simulation of trajectories. It consists of 15.8K panoramic videos from real scenes and 158K QA pairs covering imagined paths to target states. Experiments show that world models excel at generating simulated trajectories, imagination significantly improves reasoning performance on QA tasks, and the dataset transfers well to real-world applications.
鉴于现实场景中主动探索受限或存在安全风险，本研究提出WanderDream数据集，支持智能体在无实际移动情况下进行心理模拟。该数据集包含1088个真实场景的15.8K全景视频及15.8万个问答对，覆盖从当前视角到目标状态的想象路径。实验表明世界模型在生成模拟轨迹方面表现优异，想象能力显著提升问答推理性能，且该数据集在真实场景中具有良好的迁移能力，验证了心理模拟对情境推理的重要性。
Prosodic Boundary-Aware Streaming Generation for LLM-Based TTS with Streaming Text Input
Authors: Changsong Liu, Tianrui Wang, Ye Ni, Yizhou Peng, Eng Siong Chng
First: 2026-03-06T16:36:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-06T16:36:51+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Streaming TTS that receives streaming text is essential for interactive systems, yet this scheme faces two major challenges: unnatural prosody due to missing lookahead and long-form collapse due to unbounded context. We propose a prosodic-boundary-aware post-training strategy, adapting a pretrained LLM-based TTS model using weakly time-aligned data. Specifically, the model is adapted to learn early stopping at specified content boundaries when provided with limited future text. During inference, a sliding-window prompt carries forward previous text and speech tokens, ensuring bounded context and seamless concatenation. Evaluations show our method outperforms CosyVoice-Style interleaved baseline in both short and long-form scenarios. In long-text synthesis, especially, it achieves a 66.2% absolute reduction in word error rate (from 71.0% to 4.8%) and increases speaker and emotion similarity by 16.1% and 1.5% relatively, offering a robust solution for streaming TTS with incremental text.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向流式文本输入的基于LLM的TTS流式生成方法
支持流式文本输入的流式TTS对交互式系统至关重要，但该方案面临两大挑战：因缺乏前瞻导致的不自然语调和因上下文无界导致的长文本崩溃。我们提出一种基于韵律边界的后训练策略，利用弱时序对齐数据微调预训练的基于LLM的TTS模型。具体而言，模型被训练在有限未来文本条件下，在指定内容边界处提前停止生成。推理时，滑动窗口提示携带前序文本与语音token，确保上下文有界并实现无缝拼接。评估表明，该方法在短文本与长文本场景均优于CosyVoice风格的交错基线。在长文本合成中，尤其实现词错误率绝对下降66.2%（从71.0%降至4.8%），说话人与情感相似度分别提升16.1%和1.5%，为增量文本流式TTS提供稳健解决方案。
Summary / 总结
To address unnatural prosody and long-form collapse in streaming TTS systems, this work proposes a prosodic-boundary-aware post-training method that adapts a pretrained LLM-based TTS model using weakly time-aligned data, enabling early stopping at content boundaries with limited future text. During inference, a sliding-window prompt maintains bounded context by carrying forward tokens, ensuring seamless concatenation. Experiments show the method outperforms a CosyVoice-style baseline in both short and long-form synthesis, reducing word error rate by 66.2% in long texts (from 71.0% to 4.8%) and improving speaker and emotion similarity by 16.1% and 1.5% respectively.
为解决流式TTS中因缺乏前瞻导致的语调不自然和长文本生成崩溃问题，该研究提出一种基于韵律边界感知的后训练方法，利用弱时序对齐数据微调预训练的LLM-TTS模型，使其能在有限未来文本下提前终止生成。推理时采用滑动窗口提示机制，携带历史文本与语音令牌以保持上下文有界并实现无缝拼接。实验表明该方法在短文本与长文本场景均优于CosyVoice风格基线，长文本合成中词错误率下降66.2%（从71.0%降至4.8%），说话人与情感相似度分别提升16.1%和1.5%。
The Persistence of Cultural Memory: Investigating Multimodal Iconicity in Diffusion Models
Authors: Maria-Teresa De Rosa Palmini, Eva Cetinic
First: 2025-11-14T16:03:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-06T16:35:01+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The ambiguity between generalization and memorization in TTI diffusion models becomes pronounced when prompts invoke culturally shared visual references, a phenomenon we term multimodal iconicity. These are instances in which images and texts reflect established cultural associations, such as when a title recalls a familiar artwork or film scene. Such cases challenge existing approaches to evaluating memorization, as they define a setting in which instance-level memorization and culturally grounded generalization are structurally intertwined. To address this challenge, we propose an evaluation framework to assess a model's ability to remain culturally grounded without relying on visual replication. Specifically, we introduce the Cultural Reference Transformation (CRT) metric, which separates two dimensions of model behavior: Recognition, whether a model evokes a reference, from Realization, how it depicts it through replication or reinterpretation. We evaluate five diffusion models on 767 Wikidata-derived cultural references, covering both still and moving imagery, and find differences in how they respond to multimodal iconicity: some show weaker recognition, while others rely more heavily on replication. To assess linguistic sensitivity, we conduct prompt perturbation experiments using synonym substitutions and literal image descriptions, finding that models often reproduce iconic visual structures even when textual cues are altered. Finally, we find that cultural reference recognition correlates not only with training data frequency, but also textual uniqueness, reference popularity, and creation date. Our findings show that the behavior of diffusion models in culturally iconic settings cannot be reduced to simple reproduction, but depends on how references are recognized and realized, advancing evaluation beyond simple text-image matching toward richer contextual understanding.
中文标题/摘要
标题：文化记忆的持久性：探究扩散模型中的多模态 iconicity
当提示语调用文化共享的视觉参照时，TTI扩散模型中泛化与记忆之间的模糊性变得尤为明显，我们称之为多模态iconicity。这些情况中，图像与文本反映既定文化关联，例如标题唤起熟悉的艺术作品或电影场景。此类案例挑战现有记忆评估方法，因为它们定义了一种实例级记忆与文化根基泛化结构交织的环境。为应对这一挑战，我们提出评估框架，以衡量模型在不依赖视觉复制的情况下保持文化根基的能力。具体而言，我们引入文化参照转换（CRT）指标，将模型行为分为两个维度：识别（模型是否唤起参照）与实现（通过复制或重新诠释如何描绘参照）。我们在767个源自Wikidata的文化参照上评估五个扩散模型，涵盖静态与动态影像，发现它们对多模态iconicity的响应存在差异：部分模型识别能力较弱，而另一些则更依赖复制。为评估语言敏感性，我们通过同义词替换与字面图像描述进行提示扰动实验，发现即使文本线索改变，模型仍常复制iconic视觉结构。最后，我们发现文化参照识别不仅与训练数据频率相关，还与文本独特性、参照流行度及创作日期相关。我们的研究结果表明，扩散模型在文化iconic环境中的行为不能简化为简单复制，而取决于参照如何被识别与实现，推动评估从简单图文匹配迈向更丰富的语境理解。
Summary / 总结
The ambiguity between generalization and memorization in TTI diffusion models becomes pronounced when prompts invoke culturally shared visual references, a phenomenon we term multimodal iconicity.
该研究旨在评估扩散模型在处理文化共享视觉参考（即多模态标志性）时的表现，这类场景中模型需在文化泛化与实例记忆间取得平衡。作者提出文化参考转换（CRT）指标，将模型行为拆解为“识别”（是否唤起文化参考）与“实现”（通过复制或重构如何呈现）两个维度。在767个来自Wikidata的文化参考上对五种扩散模型进行评估，结果显示模型在识别能力和依赖视觉复制方面存在差异。提示扰动实验表明，即使文本提示被替换，模型仍常复现标志性视觉结构，说明其对语言变化具有鲁棒性。识别能力与训练数据频率、文本独特性、参考流行度及创作日期相关，表明模型在标志性场景中的行为取决于识别与实现方式，而非简单的图文匹配。
VisualPrompter: Semantic-Aware Prompt Optimization with Visual Feedback for Text-to-Image Synthesis
Authors: Shiyu Wu, Mingzhen Sun, Weining Wang, Yequan Wang, Jing Liu
First: 2025-06-29T08:24:39+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-06T16:34:03+00:00
Comments: ICLR2026 Camera Ready
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
The notable gap between user-provided and model-preferred prompts poses a significant challenge for generating high-quality images with text-to-image models, compelling the need for prompt engineering. Current studies on prompt engineering can effectively enhance the style and aesthetics of generated images. However, they often neglect the semantic alignment between generated images and user descriptions, resulting in visually appealing but content-wise unsatisfying outputs. In this work, we propose VisualPrompter, a novel training-free prompt engineering framework that refines user inputs to model-preferred sentences. VisualPrompter utilizes an automatic self-reflection module that identifies absent concepts in the generated images, followed by a target-specific prompt optimization mechanism that revises the prompts in a fine-grained manner. By deconstructing prompts, introducing new elements at the atomic semantic level, and then reassembling them, our framework is able to maintain semantic consistency and integrity throughout the optimization process. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of VisualPrompter, which achieves new state-of-the-art performance on multiple benchmarks for text-image alignment evaluation. Additionally, our framework features a plug-and-play design, making it highly adaptable to various generative models. Our code is available at https://github.com/teheperinko541/VisualPrompter.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the mismatch between user prompts and model-preferred inputs that leads to visually appealing but semantically misaligned images, VisualPrompter introduces a training-free framework that refines user prompts using visual feedback. It employs an automatic self-reflection module to detect missing semantic concepts in generated images and a fine-grained prompt optimization mechanism that reassembles prompts at the atomic semantic level to preserve meaning. Experimental results show VisualPrompter achieves state-of-the-art performance on multiple text-image alignment benchmarks while maintaining compatibility with various generative models.
为解决用户提示与模型偏好提示不匹配导致图像视觉美观但语义不符的问题，VisualPrompter提出了一种无需训练的框架，通过视觉反馈优化用户提示。该框架首先利用自动自反思模块识别生成图像中缺失的语义概念，再通过细粒度提示优化机制在原子语义层面拆解、修改并重组提示，确保语义一致性。实验表明，VisualPrompter在多个图文对齐基准上达到最先进性能，且其插拔式设计使其可适配多种生成模型。
Toward Generative Quantum Utility via Correlation-Complexity Map
Authors: Chen-Yu Liu, Leonardo Placidi, Eric Brunner, Enrico Rinaldi
First: 2026-03-06T16:25:17+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-06T16:25:17+00:00
Comments: 33 pages, 8 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We propose a Correlation-Complexity Map as a practical diagnostic tool for determining when real-world data distributions are structurally aligned with IQP-type quantum generative models. Characterized by two complementary indicators: (i) a Quantum Correlation-Likeness Indicator (QCLI), computed from the dataset's correlation-order (Walsh-Hadamard/Fourier) power spectrum aggregated by interaction order and quantified via Jensen-Shannon divergence from an i.i.d. binomial reference; and (ii) a Classical Correlation-Complexity Indicator (CCI), defined as the fraction of total correlation not captured by the optimal Chow-Liu tree approximation, normalized by total correlation. We provide theoretical support by relating QCLI to a support-mismatch mechanism, for fixed-architecture IQP families trained with an MMD objective, higher QCLI implies a smaller irreducible approximation floor. Using the map, we identify the classical turbulence data as both IQP-compatible and classically complex (high QCLI/high CCI). Guided by this placement, we use an invertible float-to-bitstring representation and a latent-parameter adaptation scheme that reuses a compact IQP circuit over a temporal sequence by learning and interpolating a low-dimensional latent trajectory. In comparative evaluations against classical models such as Restricted Boltzmann Machine (RBM) and Deep Convolutional Generative Adversarial Networks (DCGAN), the IQP approach achieves competitive distributional alignment while using substantially fewer training snapshots and a small latent block, supporting the use of QCLI/CCI as practical indicators for locating IQP-aligned domains and advancing generative quantum utility.
Summary / 总结
We propose a Correlation-Complexity Map as a practical diagnostic tool for determining when real-world data distributions are structurally aligned with IQP-type quantum generative models.
Contact-Safe Reinforcement Learning with ProMP Reparameterization and Energy Awareness
Authors: Bingkun Huang, Yuhe Gong, Zewen Yang, Tianyu Ren, Luis Figueredo
First: 2025-11-17T15:05:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-06T16:22:20+00:00
Comments: 8 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Reinforcement learning (RL) approaches based on Markov Decision Processes (MDPs) are predominantly applied in the robot joint space, often relying on limited task-specific information and partial awareness of the 3D environment. In contrast, episodic RL has demonstrated advantages over traditional MDP-based methods in terms of trajectory consistency, task awareness, and overall performance in complex robotic tasks. Moreover, traditional step-wise and episodic RL methods often neglect the contact-rich information inherent in task-space manipulation, especially considering the contact-safety and robustness. In this work, contact-rich manipulation tasks are tackled using a task-space, energy-safe framework, where reliable and safe task-space trajectories are generated through the combination of Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) and movement primitives. Furthermore, an energy-aware Cartesian Impedance Controller objective is incorporated within the proposed framework to ensure safe interactions between the robot and the environment. Our experimental results demonstrate that the proposed framework outperforms existing methods in handling tasks on various types of surfaces in 3D environments, achieving high success rates as well as smooth trajectories and energy-safe interactions.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于ProMP重参数化与能量感知的接触安全强化学习
基于马尔可夫决策过程（MDP）的强化学习（RL）方法主要应用于机器人关节空间，常依赖有限的任务特定信息和对3D环境的部分感知。相比之下，基于序列的RL方法在复杂机器人任务中展现出优于传统MDP方法的轨迹一致性、任务感知能力和整体性能。此外，传统步进式和序列式RL方法往往忽视任务空间操作中固有的接触丰富信息，尤其在考虑接触安全与鲁棒性时。本文采用任务空间、能量安全框架处理接触丰富操作任务，通过结合近端策略优化（PPO）与运动原语生成可靠且安全的任务空间轨迹。同时，在所提框架中引入能量感知的笛卡尔阻抗控制器目标，以确保机器人与环境的安全交互。实验结果表明，所提框架在3D环境中各类表面任务中优于现有方法，实现高成功率、平滑轨迹及能量安全交互。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of traditional MDP-based RL in handling contact-rich robotic tasks with partial environmental awareness, this work proposes a task-space, energy-safe framework combining Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) with movement primitives to generate safe and reliable trajectories. The method integrates an energy-aware Cartesian Impedance Controller objective to ensure contact safety and robustness during manipulation. Experimental results show the framework achieves higher success rates and smoother trajectories across diverse 3D surfaces compared to existing methods, demonstrating superior performance in energy-safe, contact-aware robotic manipulation.
为了解决传统基于MDP的强化学习在接触密集型机器人任务中表现不佳、缺乏环境安全感知的问题，本文提出一种结合近端策略优化与运动基元的任务空间能量安全框架，并引入笛卡尔阻抗控制器目标以确保接触安全与能耗效率。实验表明，该框架在多种表面环境下成功率更高，轨迹更平滑、交互更安全，优于现有方法。
Certified and accurate computation of function space norms of deep neural networks
Authors: Johannes Gründler, Moritz Maibaum, Philipp Petersen
First: 2026-03-06T16:08:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-06T16:08:16+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Neural network methods for PDEs require reliable error control in function space norms. However, trained neural networks can typically only be probed at a finite number of point values. Without strong assumptions, point evaluations alone do not provide enough information to derive tight deterministic and guaranteed bounds on function space norms. In this work, we move beyond a purely black-box setting and exploit the neural network structure directly. We present a framework for the certified and accurate computation of integral quantities of neural networks, including Lebesgue and Sobolev norms, by combining interval arithmetic enclosures on axis-aligned boxes with adaptive marking/refinement and quadrature-based aggregation. On each box, we compute guaranteed lower and upper bounds for function values and derivatives, and propagate these local certificates to global lower and upper bounds for the target integrals. Our analysis provides a general convergence theorem for such certified adaptive quadrature procedures and instantiates it for function values, Jacobians, and Hessians, yielding certified computation of $L^p$, $W^{1,p}$, and $W^{2,p}$ norms. We further show how these ingredients lead to practical certified bounds for PINN interior residuals. Numerical experiments illustrate the accuracy and practical behavior of the proposed methods.
中文标题/摘要
标题：深度神经网络函数空间范数的认证与精确计算
用于偏微分方程的神经网络方法需要在函数空间范数中实现可靠的误差控制。然而，训练好的神经网络通常只能在有限个点处进行探查。在无强假设条件下，仅凭点值评估无法提供足够信息以推导出紧致的确定性且可保证的函数空间范数界。本工作中，我们超越纯黑盒设定，直接利用神经网络结构。我们提出了一种框架，通过将轴对齐矩形上的区间算术包围盒与自适应标记/细化及基于数值积分的聚合相结合，实现神经网络积分量（包括勒贝格和索博列夫范数）的认证与精确计算。在每个矩形上，我们计算函数值和导数的确定性上下界，并将这些局部证书传播至目标积分的全局上下界。我们的分析为这类认证自适应数值积分方法提供了通用收敛定理，并将其应用于函数值、雅可比矩阵和海森矩阵，从而实现 $L^p$、$W^{1,p}$ 和 $W^{2,p}$ 范数的认证计算。我们进一步展示了这些成分如何用于获得PINN内部残差的实用认证界。数值实验展示了所提方法的精度与实际行为。
Summary / 总结
Neural network methods for PDEs require reliable error control in function space norms.
为实现PDE神经网络解的可靠误差控制，本文提出一种基于神经网络结构的框架，用于计算函数空间范数（如$L^p$和Sobolev范数）的认证界。该方法结合轴对齐盒子上的区间算术、自适应标记/细化与基于数值积分的聚合，计算函数值与导数的全局上下界。通过局部证书的传播，实现目标积分的认证估计。理论分析证明该自适应积分方法在函数值、雅可比和海森矩阵上的收敛性，支持范数的精确计算。实验表明，该方法能有效生成PINN内部残差的紧致认证界。
Abductive Reasoning with Syllogistic Forms in Large Language Models
Authors: Hirohiko Abe, Risako Ando, Takanobu Morishita Kentaro Ozeki, Koji Mineshima, Mitsuhiro Okada
Venue: Lecture Notes in Computer Science, vol. 15504, pp. 3-17, 2024
First: 2026-03-06T16:06:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-06T16:06:25+00:00
Comments: Published in Proceedings of the 3rd International Conference on Human and Artificial Rationalities (HAR 2024), LNCS 15504, pp. 3-17
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Research in AI using Large-Language Models (LLMs) is rapidly evolving, and the comparison of their performance with human reasoning has become a key concern. Prior studies have indicated that LLMs and humans share similar biases, such as dismissing logically valid inferences that contradict common beliefs. However, criticizing LLMs for these biases might be unfair, considering our reasoning not only involves formal deduction but also abduction, which draws tentative conclusions from limited information. Abduction can be regarded as the inverse form of syllogism in its basic structure, that is, a process of drawing a minor premise from a major premise and conclusion. This paper explores the accuracy of LLMs in abductive reasoning by converting a syllogistic dataset into one suitable for abduction. It aims to investigate whether the state-of-the-art LLMs exhibit biases in abduction and to identify potential areas for improvement, emphasizing the importance of contextualized reasoning beyond formal deduction. This investigation is vital for advancing the understanding and application of LLMs in complex reasoning tasks, offering insights into bridging the gap between machine and human cognition.
中文标题/摘要
标题：大语言模型中的溯因推理与三段论形式
人工智能领域使用大语言模型（LLMs）的研究迅速发展，其与人类推理能力的比较已成为关键议题。先前研究表明，LLMs与人类共享类似偏见，例如忽视与常识相悖但逻辑有效的推理。然而，批评LLMs这些偏见可能不公平，因为人类推理不仅包含形式演绎，还包括溯因推理——从有限信息中得出暂定结论。溯因可视为三段论基本结构的逆向形式，即从大前提和结论推导出小前提。本文通过将三段论数据集转换为适用于溯因推理的形式，探讨LLMs在溯因推理中的准确性，旨在研究当前最先进的LLMs在溯因中是否存在偏见，并识别改进方向，强调超越形式演绎的语境化推理的重要性。该研究对推进LLMs在复杂推理任务中的理解与应用至关重要，有助于弥合机器与人类认知之间的差距。
Summary / 总结
Research in AI using Large-Language Models (LLMs) is rapidly evolving, and the comparison of their performance with human reasoning has become a key concern.
该研究旨在探究大型语言模型（LLM）在溯因推理中的偏见问题，溯因推理是传统三段论的逆向推理，即从大前提和结论反推小前提。鉴于LLM与人类在面对与常识冲突的逻辑有效推理时均倾向于拒绝，作者将三段论数据集转化为溯因推理格式进行测试。实验结果表明，当前最先进的LLM在溯因推理中表现不佳，常生成错误或不一致的结论，揭示其在超越形式演绎、处理情境化假设推理方面存在明显不足。
DETECT: Determining Ease and Textual Clarity of German Text Simplifications
Authors: Maria Korobeynikova, Alessia Battisti, Lukas Fischer, Yingqiang Gao
First: 2025-10-25T08:20:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-06T16:06:13+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Current evaluation of German automatic text simplification (ATS) relies on general-purpose metrics such as SARI, BLEU, and BERTScore, which insufficiently capture simplification quality in terms of simplicity, meaning preservation, and fluency. While specialized metrics like LENS have been developed for English, corresponding efforts for German have lagged behind due to the absence of human-annotated corpora. To close this gap, we introduce DETECT, the first German-specific metric that holistically evaluates ATS quality across all three dimensions of simplicity, meaning preservation, and fluency, and is trained entirely on synthetic large language model (LLM) responses. Our approach adapts the LENS framework to German and extends it with (i) a pipeline for generating synthetic quality scores via LLMs, enabling dataset creation without human annotation, and (ii) an LLM-based refinement step for aligning grading criteria with simplification requirements. To the best of our knowledge, we also construct the largest German human evaluation dataset for text simplification to validate our metric directly. Experimental results show that DETECT achieves substantially higher correlations with human judgments than widely used ATS metrics, with particularly strong gains in meaning preservation and fluency. Beyond ATS, our findings highlight both the potential and the limitations of LLMs for automatic evaluation and provide transferable guidelines for general language accessibility tasks.

标题：DETECT：评估德语文本简化易懂性与清晰度
当前德语自动文本简化（ATS）的评估依赖于通用指标如SARI、BLEU和BERTScore，这些指标不足以充分捕捉简化文本在简洁性、语义保留和流畅性方面的质量。虽然英语领域已开发出专用指标如LENS，但德语领域因缺乏人工标注语料而进展缓慢。为弥合这一差距，我们提出了DETECT，这是首个全面评估德语ATS质量的专用指标，涵盖简洁性、语义保留和流畅性三个维度，并完全基于合成的大语言模型（LLM）响应进行训练。我们的方法将LENS框架适配至德语，并扩展了两项功能：(i) 通过LLM生成合成质量评分的流水线，无需人工标注即可创建数据集；(ii) 基于LLM的精炼步骤，使评分标准与简化需求对齐。据我们所知，我们还构建了目前最大的德语人工评估数据集，用于直接验证该指标。实验结果表明，DETECT与人工判断的相关性显著高于广泛使用的ATS指标，尤其在语义保留和流畅性方面表现突出。此外，我们的研究结果不仅揭示了LLM在自动评估中的潜力与局限，也为通用语言可及性任务提供了可迁移的指导原则。

DETECT adapts the LENS framework for German, incorporating an LLM-based pipeline to generate synthetic quality scores and a refinement step to align grading criteria with simplification goals. Evaluated against the largest German human-annotated text simplification dataset, DETECT demonstrates significantly stronger correlation with human judgments than prior metrics, especially in meaning preservation and fluency, while also revealing LLMs' potential and limitations in automatic evaluation.
为解决现有通用指标在评估德语文本简化质量时对简洁性、语义保留和流畅性覆盖不足的问题，作者提出了首个专为德语设计的评估指标DETECT，该指标完全基于合成的大语言模型响应训练。DETECT基于LENS框架进行德语适配，通过LLM生成合成评分数据并引入精炼步骤以对齐评分标准与简化需求。在目前最大的德语人工标注文本简化数据集上验证后，DETECT在语义保留和流畅性维度上显著优于SARI、BLEU和BERTScore等主流指标，同时揭示了大语言模型在自动评估中的潜力与局限性。
CLoPA: Continual Low Parameter Adaptation of Interactive Segmentation for Medical Image Annotation
Authors: Parhom Esmaeili, Chayanin Tangwiriyasakul, Eli Gibson, Sebastien Ourselin, M. Jorge Cardoso
First: 2026-03-06T16:03:20+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-06T16:03:20+00:00
Comments: 10 pages, 2 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Interactive segmentation enables clinicians to guide annotation, but existing zero-shot models like nnInteractive fail to consistently reach expert-level performance across diverse medical imaging tasks. Because annotation campaigns produce a growing stream of task-specific labelled data, online adaptation of the segmentation model is a natural complement to zero-shot inference. We propose CLoPA, a continual adaptation strategy that tunes a small fraction of nnInteractive's parameters on the annotation cache, triggered by lightweight episode scheduling. CLoPA requires no new parameters or changes to the inference pipeline, and operates entirely within the existing annotation workflow. Across eight Medical Segmentation Decathlon tasks spanning diverse anatomical targets and imaging characteristics, CLoPA rapidly elevates performance to expert-level, even for tasks where nnInteractive previously failed, with the majority of gains realised after a single training episode. We show that the benefits of tuning different parameter groups depends on task characteristics and data regimes. Also, that for targets with complex geometries (e.g., hepatic vessels), instance normalisation and low-level feature tuning saturates, suggesting a need for deeper feature-representation alignment in the most challenging scenarios.
中文标题/摘要
标题：CLoPA：面向医学图像标注的交互式分割持续低参数自适应方法
交互式分割使临床医生能够指导标注，但现有零样本模型（如nnInteractive）难以在多样化的医学成像任务中持续达到专家级性能。由于标注活动会产生不断增长的特定任务标注数据流，在线自适应分割模型是零样本推理的自然补充。我们提出CLoPA，一种持续自适应策略，通过轻量级回合调度触发，在标注缓存上微调nnInteractive的少量参数。CLoPA无需新增参数或修改推理流程，完全嵌入现有标注工作流。在涵盖多种解剖目标和成像特性的八个Medical Segmentation Decathlon任务中，CLoPA迅速将性能提升至专家级，即使在nnInteractive此前失败的任务中亦如此，且大部分性能提升在单次训练回合后即实现。我们表明，不同参数组的微调收益取决于任务特性和数据分布；此外，对于几何结构复杂的靶标（如肝血管），实例归一化与低层特征微调会饱和，暗示在最困难场景中需更深层次的特征表示对齐。

Interactive segmentation enables clinicians to guide annotation, but existing zero-shot models like nnInteractive fail to consistently reach expert-level performance across diverse medical imaging tasks.
ECLARE: Efficient cross-planar learning for anisotropic resolution enhancement
Authors: Samuel W. Remedios, Shuwen Wei, Shuo Han, Jinwei Zhang, Aaron Carass, Kurt G. Schilling, Dzung L. Pham, Jerry L. Prince, Blake E. Dewey
First: 2025-03-14T18:24:35+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-06T16:01:11+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
In clinical imaging, magnetic resonance (MR) image volumes are often acquired as stacks of 2D slices with decreased scan times, improved signal-to-noise ratio, and image contrasts unique to 2D MR pulse sequences. While this is sufficient for clinical evaluation, automated algorithms designed for 3D analysis perform poorly on multi-slice 2D MR volumes, especially those with thick slices and gaps between slices. Super-resolution (SR) methods aim to address this problem, but previous methods do not address all of the following: slice profile shape estimation, slice gap, domain shift, and non-integer or arbitrary upsampling factors. In this paper, we propose ECLARE (Efficient Cross-planar Learning for Anisotropic Resolution Enhancement), a self-SR method that addresses each of these factors. ECLARE uses a slice profile estimated from the multi-slice 2D MR volume, trains a network to learn the mapping from low-resolution to high-resolution in-plane patches from the same volume, and performs SR with anti-aliasing. We compared ECLARE to cubic B-spline interpolation, SMORE, and other contemporary SR methods. We used realistic and representative simulations so that quantitative performance against ground truth can be computed, and ECLARE outperformed all other methods in both signal recovery and downstream tasks. Importantly, as ECLARE does not use external training data it cannot suffer from domain shift between training and testing. Our code is open-source and available at https://www.github.com/sremedios/eclare.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ECLARE：用于各向异性分辨率增强的高效跨平面学习
在临床成像中，磁共振（MR）图像体通常以2D切片堆栈形式获取，具有扫描时间缩短、信噪比提高以及2D MR脉冲序列特有的图像对比度。虽然这足以满足临床评估需求，但为3D分析设计的自动化算法在多切片2D MR体数据上表现不佳，尤其在切片较厚且切片间存在间隙的情况下。超分辨率（SR）方法旨在解决此问题，但以往方法未能同时处理以下因素：切片轮廓形状估计、切片间隙、领域偏移及非整数或任意上采样因子。本文提出ECLARE（用于各向异性分辨率增强的高效跨平面学习），一种自监督SR方法，可同时应对上述所有因素。ECLARE从多切片2D MR体中估计切片轮廓，训练网络学习同一体数据中低分辨率到高分辨率平面块的映射，并结合抗混叠进行SR。我们对比了ECLARE与三次B样条插值、SMORE及其他当代SR方法。通过真实且具代表性的模拟，可计算与真实值的定量性能，结果表明ECLARE在信号恢复及下游任务中均优于其他方法。重要的是，由于ECLARE不使用外部训练数据，故不会因训练与测试数据域差异而产生领域偏移。我们的代码开源，可访问https://www.github.com/sremedios/eclare。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of enhancing resolution in 2D multi-slice MR volumes where 3D algorithms perform poorly due to slice gaps, thickness, and domain shift, ECLARE introduces a self-supervised super-resolution method that directly learns from the input volume without external data. It estimates slice profiles, trains a network to map low-resolution in-plane patches to high-resolution counterparts within the same volume, and applies anti-aliasing during upscaling. Evaluated against cubic B-spline, SMORE, and other methods using realistic simulations with ground truth, ECLARE achieved superior performance in signal recovery and downstream tasks, demonstrating robustness without domain shift due to its self-training approach.
为了解决3D算法在具有切片间隙和各向异性分辨率的2D多切片MR图像上的表现不佳问题，ECLARE提出了一种自监督超分辨率方法，显式建模切片轮廓、处理切片间隙、避免域偏移并支持任意上采样因子。该方法在同一体积内训练网络，将低分辨率块映射到高分辨率块，并结合抗混叠处理。在真实模拟数据上的实验表明，ECLARE在信号恢复和下游任务中均优于立方B样条、SMORE及其他超分辨率方法，且无需外部训练数据。
From Prompting to Preference Optimization: A Comparative Study of LLM-based Automated Essay Scoring
Authors: Minh Hoang Nguyen, Vu Hoang Pham, Xuan Thanh Huynh, Phuc Hong Mai, Vinh The Nguyen, Quang Nhut Huynh, Huy Tien Nguyen, Tung Le
First: 2026-03-06T15:59:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-06T15:59:32+00:00
Comments: 19 pages, 10 figures, 7 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) have recently reshaped Automated Essay Scoring (AES), yet prior studies typically examine individual techniques in isolation, limiting understanding of their relative merits for English as a Second Language (L2) writing. To bridge this gap, we presents a comprehensive comparison of major LLM-based AES paradigms on IELTS Writing Task~2. On this unified benchmark, we evaluate four approaches: (i) encoder-based classification fine-tuning, (ii) zero- and few-shot prompting, (iii) instruction tuning and Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG), and (iv) Supervised Fine-Tuning combined with Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) and RAG. Our results reveal clear accuracy-cost-robustness trade-offs across methods, the best configuration, integrating k-SFT and RAG, achieves the strongest overall results with F1-Score 93%. This study offers the first unified empirical comparison of modern LLM-based AES strategies for English L2, promising potential in auto-grading writing tasks. Code is public at https://github.com/MinhNguyenDS/LLM_AES-EnL2
中文标题/摘要
标题：从提示到偏好优化：基于大语言模型的自动作文评分方法比较研究
大语言模型（LLMs）近期重塑了自动作文评分（AES），但以往研究通常孤立考察单个技术，限制了对英语作为第二语言（L2）写作中各方法相对优劣的理解。为弥合这一差距，我们在雅思写作任务2上对主流LLM-based AES范式进行了全面比较。在该统一基准上，我们评估了四种方法：（i）编码器分类微调，（ii）零样本与少样本提示，（iii）指令调优与检索增强生成（RAG），以及（iv）监督微调结合直接偏好优化（DPO）与RAG。结果揭示各方法在准确率、成本与鲁棒性之间存在明显权衡，最佳配置（k-SFT与RAG结合）实现F1分数93%，表现最优。本研究首次对面向英语L2的现代LLM-based AES策略进行统一实证比较，为自动作文评分任务提供潜在应用价值。代码公开于https://github.com/MinhNguyenDS/LLM_AES-EnL2
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand the relative effectiveness of different LLM-based approaches for scoring English as a Second Language writing, this study systematically compares four major paradigms—encoder-based fine-tuning, prompting (zero/few-shot), instruction tuning with RAG, and supervised fine-tuning with DPO and RAG—on the IELTS Writing Task 2 benchmark. The authors find that while each method offers distinct trade-offs between accuracy, computational cost, and robustness, the optimal configuration combining k-SFT and RAG achieves the highest performance with an F1-score of 93%, outperforming all other methods. This work provides the first unified empirical evaluation of modern LLM-based AES strategies for L2 writing, offering actionable insights for automated grading systems.
为理解不同大语言模型（LLM）方法在评分英语作为第二语言（L2）作文中的相对有效性，本研究在雅思写作Task 2基准上系统比较了四种主流方法：编码器微调、提示（零样本/少样本）、指令调优结合RAG，以及SFT结合DPO与RAG。实验发现，集成k-SFT与RAG的方法表现最优，F1分数达93%，同时揭示了各方法在准确率、成本与鲁棒性之间的权衡。该研究首次对现代LLM自动作文评分方法进行了统一实证比较，为自动评分系统提供了可操作的参考。
Non-invasive Growth Monitoring of Small Freshwater Fish in Home Aquariums via Stereo Vision
Authors: Clemens Seibold, Anna Hilsmann, Peter Eisert
First: 2026-03-06T15:57:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-06T15:57:53+00:00
Comments: Accepted at VISAPP 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Monitoring fish growth behavior provides relevant information about fish health in aquaculture and home aquariums. Yet, monitoring fish sizes poses different challenges, as fish are small and subject to strong refractive distortions in aquarium environments. Image-based measurement offers a practical, non-invasive alternative that allows frequent monitoring without disturbing the fish. In this paper, we propose a non-invasive refraction-aware stereo vision method to estimate fish length in aquariums. Our approach uses a YOLOv11-Pose network to detect fish and predict anatomical keypoints on the fish in each stereo image. A refraction-aware epipolar constraint accounting for the air-glass-water interfaces enables robust matching, and unreliable detections are removed using a learned quality score. A subsequent refraction-aware 3D triangulation recovers 3D keypoints, from which fish length is measured. We validate our approach on a new stereo dataset of endangered Sulawesi ricefish captured under aquarium-like conditions and demonstrate that filtering low-quality detections is essential for accurate length estimation. The proposed system offers a simple and practical solution for non-invasive growth monitoring and can be easily applied in home aquariums.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过立体视觉对家庭水族箱中小型淡水鱼的无创生长监测
监测鱼类生长行为可为水产养殖和家庭水族箱中的鱼类健康提供相关信息。然而，监测鱼体大小面临不同挑战，因鱼体型小且在水族箱环境中易受强折射畸变影响。基于图像的测量提供了一种实用、无创的替代方案，可实现频繁监测而不干扰鱼类。本文提出一种无创且考虑折射的立体视觉方法，用于估算水族箱中鱼的体长。该方法使用YOLOv11-Pose网络检测鱼类并预测每张立体图像中鱼体的解剖关键点。通过考虑空气-玻璃-水界面的折射感知极线约束实现稳健匹配，并利用学习到的质量评分剔除不可靠检测。随后，通过折射感知的3D三角测量恢复3D关键点，从而测量鱼体长度。我们在新采集的、模拟水族箱环境的濒危苏拉威西米鱼立体数据集上验证了该方法，并证明剔除低质量检测对准确长度估算至关重要。所提系统为无创生长监测提供了一种简单实用的解决方案，易于应用于家庭水族箱。
Summary / 总结
To enable non-invasive monitoring of small freshwater fish growth in home aquariums, this study proposes a stereo vision method that compensates for refractive distortions at air-glass-water interfaces. It employs a YOLOv11-Pose network to detect fish and predict anatomical keypoints, then uses a refraction-aware epipolar constraint for robust stereo matching and a learned quality score to filter unreliable detections. A refraction-aware 3D triangulation recovers keypoint positions to compute fish length. Evaluated on a new stereo dataset of Sulawesi ricefish under aquarium-like conditions, the method demonstrates that quality filtering significantly improves accuracy, offering a practical, non-invasive solution for growth tracking in home aquariums.
为实现家庭水族箱中小型淡水鱼的非侵入式生长监测，该研究提出一种补偿空气-玻璃-水界面折射效应的立体视觉方法。该方法利用YOLOv11-Pose检测鱼体并预测关键点，结合折射感知的极线约束实现鲁棒匹配，并通过学习得分过滤低质量检测结果。随后采用折射感知三维三角测量重建关键点，计算鱼体长度。在新采集的苏拉威西米鱼立体数据集上验证表明，质量过滤对长度估计精度至关重要，该系统提供了一种简单实用的持续非侵入式生长监测方案。
Validating Interpretability in siRNA Efficacy Prediction: A Perturbation-Based, Dataset-Aware Protocol
Authors: Zahra Khodagholi, Niloofar Yousefi
Venue: ICLR 2026
First: 2026-02-09T19:27:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-06T15:55:21+00:00
Comments: Accepted at the Machine Learning for Genomics Explorations (MLGenX) Workshop at ICLR 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Saliency maps are increasingly used as design guidance in siRNA efficacy prediction, yet attribution methods are rarely validated before motivating sequence edits. We introduce a pre-synthesis gate: a protocol for counterfactual sensitivity faithfulness that tests whether mutating high-saliency positions changes model output more than composition-matched controls. Cross-dataset transfer reveals two failure modes that would otherwise go undetected: faithful-but-wrong (saliency valid, predictions fail) and inverted saliency (top-saliency edits less impactful than random). Strikingly, models trained on mRNA-level assays collapse on a luciferase reporter dataset, demonstrating that protocol shifts can silently invalidate deployment. Across four benchmarks, 19/20 fold instances pass; the single failure shows inverted saliency. A biology-informed regularizer (BioPrior) strengthens saliency faithfulness with modest, dataset-dependent predictive trade-offs. Our results establish saliency validation as essential pre-deployment practice for explanation-guided therapeutic design. Code is available at https://github.com/shadi97kh/BioPrior.
中文标题/摘要
标题：siRNA效力预测中可解释性的验证：一种基于扰动且适配数据集的协议
显著性图在siRNA效力预测中日益被用作设计指导，但归因方法在引导序列编辑前鲜有验证。我们引入一个合成前门限：一种反事实敏感性忠实性测试协议，用于检验突变高显著性位点是否比组成匹配的对照组更显著改变模型输出。跨数据集迁移揭示了两种否则难以发现的失效模式：忠实但错误（显著性有效，预测失败）和反向显著性（最高显著性编辑影响小于随机编辑）。令人惊讶的是，基于mRNA水平检测训练的模型在荧光素酶报告基因数据集上完全失效，表明协议变更可能无声地使部署无效。在四个基准测试中，19/20个折叠实例通过；唯一失败案例显示反向显著性。一种基于生物学先验的正则化器（BioPrior）在适度且依赖数据集的预测性能折损下增强了显著性忠实性。我们的结果确立了显著性验证作为解释引导治疗设计部署前的必要实践。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to validate saliency maps before guiding siRNA design, the study introduces a perturbation-based protocol that tests whether mutating high-saliency positions alters model predictions more than control mutations. Applied across four benchmarks, the protocol uncovered two failure modes: models could produce faithful but incorrect predictions or exhibit inverted saliency where top-saliency edits were less impactful than random ones. Notably, models trained on mRNA assays failed on a luciferase reporter dataset, revealing silent deployment risks. A biology-informed regularizer (BioPrior) improved saliency faithfulness with minor predictive trade-offs, and 19 out of 20 test cases passed, highlighting the protocol’s effectiveness in ensuring reliable interpretability for therapeutic design.
为在siRNA设计前验证显著性图的可靠性，作者提出一种基于扰动、面向数据集的协议，通过比较高显著性位点突变与对照突变对模型输出的影响来检验解释的忠实性。在四个基准测试中，该协议揭示了两种失效模式：模型可能产生忠实但错误的预测，或出现显著性倒置（高显著性位点突变影响小于随机突变）。值得注意的是，基于mRNA检测训练的模型在荧光素酶报告基因数据集上失效，表明部署时可能隐藏风险。引入生物信息正则化器（BioPrior）可适度提升显著性忠实性，伴随轻微且依赖数据集的预测性能折损，20个测试案例中有19个通过验证。
Evaluation of Deontic Conditional Reasoning in Large Language Models: The Case of Wason's Selection Task
Authors: Hirohiko Abe, Kentaro Ozeki, Risako Ando, Takanobu Morishita, Koji Mineshima, Mitsuhiro Okada
First: 2026-03-06T15:55:13+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-06T15:55:13+00:00
Comments: To appear in the Proceedings of EACL 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
As large language models (LLMs) advance in linguistic competence, their reasoning abilities are gaining increasing attention. In humans, reasoning often performs well in domain specific settings, particularly in normative rather than purely formal contexts. Although prior studies have compared LLM and human reasoning, the domain specificity of LLM reasoning remains underexplored. In this study, we introduce a new Wason Selection Task dataset that explicitly encodes deontic modality to systematically distinguish deontic from descriptive conditionals, and use it to examine LLMs' conditional reasoning under deontic rules. We further analyze whether observed error patterns are better explained by confirmation bias (a tendency to seek rule-supporting evidence) or by matching bias (a tendency to ignore negation and select items that lexically match elements of the rule). Results show that, like humans, LLMs reason better with deontic rules and display matching-bias-like errors. Together, these findings suggest that the performance of LLMs varies systematically across rule types and that their error patterns can parallel well-known human biases in this paradigm.
中文标题/摘要
标题：大型语言模型的道义条件推理评估：以沃森选择任务为例
随着大型语言模型（LLMs）在语言能力上的进步，其推理能力正受到越来越多的关注。在人类中，推理往往在特定领域表现良好，尤其是在规范性而非纯形式语境中。尽管已有研究比较了LLM与人类的推理能力，但LLM推理的领域特异性仍待深入探索。本研究引入了一个新的沃森选择任务数据集，明确编码道义模态，以系统区分道义与描述性条件句，并用其考察LLMs在道义规则下的条件推理能力。我们进一步分析观察到的错误模式是更符合确认偏误（倾向于寻求支持规则的证据）还是匹配偏误（倾向于忽略否定，选择与规则词项字面匹配的项目）。结果显示，与人类相似，LLMs在道义规则下推理表现更好，且表现出类似匹配偏误的错误。这些发现共同表明，LLMs在不同规则类型下的表现存在系统性差异，其错误模式可与该范式中已知的人类偏误相平行。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand how large language models (LLMs) reason under normative, deontic rules rather than purely formal ones, this study introduces a modified Wason Selection Task dataset explicitly encoding deontic modality to contrast it with descriptive conditionals. Using this dataset, the researchers evaluated LLMs’ performance on conditional reasoning tasks and analyzed error patterns to determine whether they stemmed from confirmation bias or matching bias. Results indicate that LLMs, like humans, perform better with deontic rules and exhibit matching-bias-like errors—selecting items that lexically match rule components while ignoring negations—suggesting systematic variation in LLM reasoning based on rule type and alignment with known human cognitive biases.
本研究旨在探究大语言模型（LLM）在道义条件句下的推理能力，动机源于人类在规范性情境中推理表现更优的现象。研究者设计了一套修改版的Wason选择任务数据集，明确编码道义模态以区分道义与描述性条件句，从而系统评估LLM在道义规则下的推理表现。实验结果表明，LLM在道义规则下表现更优，且其错误模式与人类匹配偏见相似——倾向于选择与规则语义匹配的项目而忽略否定项，而非确认偏见。这些发现表明LLM的推理表现随规则类型系统性变化，其错误模式可与该任务中已知的人类认知偏见相对应。
